
===== FILE: C:\Users\tejas\OneDrive\Desktop\LastChance\Backend\fix_db_schema.js =====

import 'dotenv/config'
import { PrismaClient } from '@prisma/client'

const prisma = new PrismaClient()

async function migrate() {
  try {
    console.log('Migrating AppState table...')
    await prisma.$executeRawUnsafe(`
      DO $$
      BEGIN
        IF EXISTS (
          SELECT 1 FROM information_schema.columns 
          WHERE table_name='AppState' AND column_name='clientId'
        ) THEN
          ALTER TABLE "AppState" RENAME COLUMN "clientId" TO "id";
          ALTER TABLE "AppState" ALTER COLUMN "id" TYPE TEXT USING "id"::text;
        END IF;
      END $$;
    `)

    await prisma.$executeRawUnsafe(`
      ALTER TABLE "AppState" ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS "key" TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'default';
    `)

    await prisma.$executeRawUnsafe(`
      CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS "AppState_key_key" ON "AppState"("key");
    `)

    console.log('Migration completed successfully!')
  } catch (err) {
    console.error('Migration failed:', err)
  } finally {
    await prisma.$disconnect()
  }
}

migrate()


===== FILE: C:\Users\tejas\OneDrive\Desktop\LastChance\Backend\index.js =====

import 'dotenv/config'
import express from 'express'
import cors from 'cors'
import { PrismaClient } from '@prisma/client'

const app = express()
const prisma = new PrismaClient()
const PORT = Number(process.env.PORT || 3001)
const DEFAULT_STATE_KEY = 'default'

app.use(cors())
app.use(express.json({ limit: '5mb' }))

app.get('/api/health', async (_req, res) => {
  try {
    await prisma.$queryRaw`SELECT 1`
    res.json({ ok: true })
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Database health check failed:', error)
    res.status(500).json({ ok: false, error: 'Database connection failed' })
  }
})

// Load Cloud State (data aur questData dono return karega)
app.get('/api/state', async (req, res, next) => {
  try {
    const key = String(req.query.key || DEFAULT_STATE_KEY)
    const record = await prisma.appState.findUnique({ where: { key } })

    res.json({
      key,
      data: record?.data ?? null,
      questData: record?.questData ?? null,
      updatedAt: record?.updatedAt ?? null,
    })
  } catch (error) {
    next(error)
  }
})

// Save Cloud State (data aur questData dono save karega)
app.put('/api/state', async (req, res, next) => {
  try {
    const key = String(req.body?.key || DEFAULT_STATE_KEY)
    const data = req.body?.data
    const questData = req.body?.questData ?? {}

    if (!data || typeof data !== 'object' || Array.isArray(data)) {
      res.status(400).json({ error: 'Request body must include an object payload in `data`.' })
      return
    }

    const record = await prisma.appState.upsert({
      where: { key },
      update: { data, questData },
      create: { key, data, questData },
    })

    res.json({
      key: record.key,
      updatedAt: record.updatedAt,
    })
  } catch (error) {
    next(error)
  }
})

app.use((error, _req, res, _next) => {
  console.error('API request failed:', error)
  res.status(500).json({
    error: 'Internal server error',
    message: error.message,
  })
})

const server = app.listen(PORT, () => {
  console.log(`Hunter API listening on port ${PORT}`)
})

const shutdown = async () => {
  server.close(async () => {
    await prisma.$disconnect()
    process.exit(0)
  })
}

process.on('SIGINT', shutdown)
process.on('SIGTERM', shutdown)

generator client {
  provider = "prisma-client-js"
}

datasource db {
  provider  = "postgresql"
  url       = env("DATABASE_URL")
  directUrl = env("DIRECT_URL")
}

model AppState {
  id        String   @id @default(uuid())
  key       String   @unique @default("default")
  data      Json     // <-- Aapka poora app state yahan save hoga
  questData Json?    // <-- Aapka quest-related data yahan safe rahega
  createdAt DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt
}

CREATE TABLE "AppState" (
    "clientId" UUID NOT NULL,
    "data" JSONB NOT NULL,
    "questData" JSONB NOT NULL,
    "createdAt" TIMESTAMP(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    "updatedAt" TIMESTAMP(3) NOT NULL,

    CONSTRAINT "AppState_pkey" PRIMARY KEY ("clientId")
);

===== FILE: C:\Users\tejas\OneDrive\Desktop\LastChance\Frontend\src\api.js =====

// Yahan apna Render/Backend URL daal sakte hain, ya Vercel ke Environment Variable mein:
const RAW_BASE_URL = import.meta.env.VITE_API_URL || 'http://localhost:3001'
const API_BASE_URL = RAW_BASE_URL.replace(/\/+$/, '')

const DEFAULT_STATE_KEY = 'default'

// --- Legacy / LocalStorage Functions ---
const LOCAL_STORAGE_KEY = 'hunter_app_state_legacy'

export function readLegacyState(defaultQuestData = {}) {
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(LOCAL_STORAGE_KEY)
    if (!raw) return null
    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw)
    return {
      data: parsed.data || parsed,
      questData: parsed.questData || defaultQuestData
    }
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('Failed to read legacy local state', e)
    return null
  }
}

export function clearLegacyState() {
  try {
    localStorage.removeItem(LOCAL_STORAGE_KEY)
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('Failed to clear legacy local state', e)
  }
}

// --- Cloud API Functions ---
export async function checkApiHealth() {
  const response = await fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}/api/health`)
  if (!response.ok) {
    throw new Error(`Health check failed with status ${response.status}`)
  }
  return response.json()
}

export async function loadCloudState(key = DEFAULT_STATE_KEY) {
  const params = new URLSearchParams({ key })
  const response = await fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}/api/state?${params.toString()}`)

  if (!response.ok) {
    throw new Error(`Cloud load failed with status ${response.status}`)
  }

  const result = await response.json()
  // Ensure it returns an object with data and questData
  return {
    data: result.data || null,
    questData: result.questData || null,
    updatedAt: result.updatedAt || null
  }
}

export async function saveCloudState(payload, key = DEFAULT_STATE_KEY) {
  const bodyData = {
    key,
    data: payload.data || payload,
    questData: payload.questData || {}
  }

  const response = await fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}/api/state`, {
    method: 'PUT',
    headers: {
      'Content-Type': 'application/json',
    },
    body: JSON.stringify(bodyData),
  })

  if (!response.ok) {
    throw new Error(`Cloud save failed with status ${response.status}`)
  }

  return response.json()
}

const DAILY_WIN_XP = 10;
const DAILY_WIN_XP_CAP = 50;

function createInitialQuestData() {
  return {
    xp: 0,
    completed: {},
    days: {},
    sideCompleted: {},
    dailyWinRewards: {},
    levelRewards: {},
  };
}

function mergeSavedData(saved) {
  const defaults = createInitialData();
  return {
    ...defaults,
    ...saved,
    version: SCHEMA_VERSION,
    profile: {
      ...defaults.profile,
      ...(saved.profile || {}),
      targets: {
        ...defaults.profile.targets,
        ...(saved.profile?.targets || {}),
      },
    },
    days: { ...defaults.days, ...(saved.days || {}) },
    groom: { ...defaults.groom, ...(saved.groom || {}) },
    settings: { ...defaults.settings, ...(saved.settings || {}) },
  };
}

function mergeQuestData(saved) {
  return { ...createInitialQuestData(), ...saved };
}

function getDailyWinXP(questData, date) {
  const rewards = questData.dailyWinRewards?.[date];
  return Math.min(
    DAILY_WIN_XP_CAP,
    (Array.isArray(rewards) ? rewards.length : 0) * DAILY_WIN_XP
  );
}

/* Every page the app can show. Swipe order = NAV order first, then any extra page. */
const ALL_PAGES = [
  "Today",
  "Habits",
  "Quest",
  "Rank",
  "Lifestyle",
  "Looks",
  "Reset",
  "Yoga",
  "Goals",
  "Reflection",
  "Speak",
  "More",
];
const PAGES = [...NAV.map(([name]) => name), ...ALL_PAGES].filter(
  (n, i, a) => a.indexOf(n) === i
);

export default function App() {
  const [data, setData] = useState(createInitialData);
  const [questData, setQuestData] = useState(createInitialQuestData);
  const [cloudReady, setCloudReady] = useState(false);
  const [cloudSyncEnabled, setCloudSyncEnabled] = useState(false);
  const [cloudStatus, setCloudStatus] = useState("connecting");
  const [connectionError, setConnectionError] = useState("");
  const [modal, setModal] = useState(null);
  const [sosOpen, setSosOpen] = useState(false);
  const [cmdOpen, setCmdOpen] = useState(false);
  const [cmdQuery, setCmdQuery] = useState("");
  const [toast, setToast] = useState("");
  const [query, setQuery] = useState("");
  const [showArchived, setShowArchived] = useState(false);
  const dark = Boolean(data.settings?.darkTheme);
  const [today, setToday] = useState(todayISO);
  const dailyWinXP = getDailyWinXP(questData, today);
  const [selectedISO, setSelectedISO] = useState(todayISO);
  const mobileNavRef = useRef(null);
  const cloudSaveQueue = useRef(Promise.resolve());

  const p = data.profile || {};
  const dm = useCallback(
    (txt) => discreetText(txt, Boolean(p.discreet)),
    [p.discreet]
  );

  /* Page navigation: real browser history (Back works) + swipe between pages */
  const { page, direction, navigate } = usePageNav(PAGES, "Today");
  const setPage = navigate; // every existing setPage("...") call now pushes history
  useSwipeNav({
    page,
    pages: PAGES,
    navigate,
    enabled: !modal && !sosOpen && !cmdOpen, // no swiping while a popup is open
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    const activeLink = mobileNavRef.current?.querySelector(
      '[aria-current="page"]'
    );
    activeLink?.scrollIntoView({
      block: "nearest",
      inline: "center",
      behavior: window.matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)").matches
        ? "auto"
        : "smooth",
    });
  }, [page]);

  const connectToCloud = useCallback(async () => {
    setCloudReady(false);
    setCloudSyncEnabled(false);
    setCloudStatus("connecting");
    setConnectionError("");
    try {
      const savedState = await loadCloudState();
      const state = savedState || readLegacyState(createInitialQuestData());
      if (state) {
        setData(mergeSavedData(state.data));
        setQuestData(mergeQuestData(state.questData));
      } else {
        setData(createInitialData());
        setQuestData(createInitialQuestData());
      }
      setCloudSyncEnabled(true);
      setCloudStatus(savedState ? "saved" : "saving");
      setCloudReady(true);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("Could not load saved data from the cloud:", error);
      setConnectionError(
        "The API or database is unavailable. Check your connection settings, then try again."
      );
      setCloudStatus("error");
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    const timeoutId = window.setTimeout(connectToCloud, 0);
    return () => window.clearTimeout(timeoutId);
  }, [connectToCloud]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!cloudReady || !cloudSyncEnabled) return undefined;
    let cancelled = false;
    const timeoutId = window.setTimeout(async () => {
      setCloudStatus("saving");
      const save = cloudSaveQueue.current.then(() =>
        saveCloudState({ data, questData })
      );
      const saveResult = save.then(
        () => true,
        (error) => {
          if (!cancelled) {
            console.error("Could not save data to the cloud:", error);
            setCloudStatus("error");
          }
          return false;
        }
      );
      cloudSaveQueue.current = saveResult.then(() => undefined);
      if (await saveResult && !cancelled) {
        clearLegacyState();
        setCloudStatus("saved");
      }
    }, 500);

    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
      window.clearTimeout(timeoutId);
    };
  }, [cloudReady, cloudSyncEnabled, data, questData]);

  useEffect(() => {
    document
      .querySelector('meta[name="theme-color"]')
      ?.setAttribute("content", dark ? "#0f1117" : "#ffffff");
    document
      .querySelector('meta[name="color-scheme"]')
      ?.setAttribute("content", dark ? "dark" : "light");
  }, [dark]);

  /* Auto-detect midnight rollover so 'today' stays accurate */
  useEffect(() => {
    const id = window.setInterval(() => {
      const nowISO = todayISO();
      setToday((prev) => {
        if (prev !== nowISO) {
          setSelectedISO((sel) => (sel === prev ? nowISO : sel));
          return nowISO;
        }
        return prev;
      });
    }, 60000);
    return () => window.clearInterval(id);
  }, []);

  /* Global keyboard shortcuts: Ctrl/Cmd+K (Command Palette) & Alt+S (Urge SOS) */
  useEffect(() => {
    const onKeyDown = (e) => {
      if ((e.metaKey || e.ctrlKey) && e.key.toLowerCase() === "k") {
        e.preventDefault();
        setCmdOpen((o) => !o);
        setCmdQuery("");
      } else if (e.altKey && e.key.toLowerCase() === "s") {
        e.preventDefault();
        setSosOpen(true);
      }
    };
    window.addEventListener("keydown", onKeyDown);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKeyDown);
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!toast) return undefined;
    const id = setTimeout(() => setToast(""), 2600);
    return () => clearTimeout(id);
  }, [toast]);

  const notify = useCallback((msg) => setToast(msg), []);

  const rewardDailyWin = useCallback(
    (source, label) => {
      const saved = questData;
      const rewardHistory =
        saved.dailyWinRewards &&
        typeof saved.dailyWinRewards === "object" &&
        !Array.isArray(saved.dailyWinRewards)
          ? saved.dailyWinRewards
          : {};
      const todayRewards = Array.isArray(rewardHistory[today])
        ? rewardHistory[today]
        : [];

      if (todayRewards.includes(source)) {
        triggerCompletionFeedback("tick");
        notify(`${label} complete â€” XP for this win is already claimed.`);
        return;
      }

      if (todayRewards.length * DAILY_WIN_XP >= DAILY_WIN_XP_CAP) {
        triggerCompletionFeedback("tick");
        notify(
          `${label} complete â€” today's bonus XP is earned. Keep going at your own pace.`
        );
        return;
      }

      const previousXP = Math.max(0, Number(saved.xp) || 0);
      const nextXP = previousXP + DAILY_WIN_XP;
      const nextRewards = [...todayRewards, source];
      setQuestData({
        ...saved,
        xp: nextXP,
        dailyWinRewards: {
          ...rewardHistory,
          [today]: nextRewards,
        },
      });

      const leveledUp = Math.floor(nextXP / 100) > Math.floor(previousXP / 100);
      triggerCompletionFeedback(leveledUp ? "levelUp" : "success");
      notify(
        leveledUp
          ? `Level ${Math.floor(nextXP / 100) + 1} unlocked! +${DAILY_WIN_XP} XP Â· ${label}`
          : `+${DAILY_WIN_XP} XP earned Â· ${label}`
      );
    },
    [notify, questData, today]
  );

  const update = useCallback((key, fn) => {
    setData((old) => ({
      ...old,
      [key]: typeof fn === "function" ? fn(old[key] || []) : fn,
    }));
  }, []);

  const updateProfile = useCallback((patch) => {
    setData((old) => ({
      ...old,
      profile: {
        ...old.profile,
        ...(typeof patch === "function" ? patch(old.profile) : patch),
      },
    }));
  }, []);

  const updateDay = useCallback((isoDate, updater) => {
    setData((old) => {
      const existing = (old.days && old.days[isoDate]) || createEmptyDay();
      const nextDay =
        typeof updater === "function"
          ? updater(existing)
          : { ...existing, ...updater };
      return {
        ...old,
        days: { ...(old.days || {}), [isoDate]: nextDay },
      };
    });
  }, []);

  const todayHabits = useMemo(
    () =>
      (data.habits || []).filter(
        (h) =>
          !h.archived &&
          (h.schedule !== "weekdays" ||
            [1, 2, 3, 4, 5].includes(new Date().getDay()))
      ),
    [data.habits]
  );

  const doneHabits = todayHabits.filter((h) => h.doneDate === today).length;
  const activeTasks = (data.tasks || []).filter((t) => !t.archived);
  const doneTasks = activeTasks.filter((t) => t.done).length;
  const todayMood = (data.moods || []).find((m) => m.date === today);
  const activeGoals = (data.goals || []).filter((g) => !g.archived);
  const todayDayObj = (data.days && data.days[today]) || createEmptyDay();
  const todayBreakdown = useMemo(
    () => calcDailyScoreBreakdown(todayDayObj, p),
    [todayDayObj, p]
  );
  const streaks = useMemo(
    () => calcStreaks(data.days, p, today),
    [data.days, p, today]
  );

  const saveRecord = (key, record, id) => {
    update(key, (arr) =>
      id
        ? arr.map((x) => (x.id === id ? { ...x, ...record } : x))
        : [{ id: uid(), createdAt: Date.now(), ...record }, ...arr]
    );
    setModal(null);
    notify(id ? "Changes queued for cloud sync" : "Added Â· syncing to cloud");
  };

  const removeRecord = (key, id) => {
    if (window.confirm("Delete this item? This cannot be undone.")) {
      update(key, (arr) => arr.filter((x) => x.id !== id));
      notify("Item deleted");
    }
  };

  const setDoneHabit = (habit) => {
    const completing = habit.doneDate !== today;
    update("habits", (arr) =>
      arr.map((h) =>
        h.id === habit.id
          ? { ...h, doneDate: h.doneDate === today ? "" : today }
          : h
      )
    );
    update("checkins", (arr) => {
      const existing = arr.find(
        (c) => c.habitId === habit.id && c.date === today
      );
      if (existing) {
        return arr.map((c) =>
          c.id === existing.id ? { ...c, done: !existing.done } : c
        );
      }
      return [
        {
          id: uid(),
          habitId: habit.id,
          name: habit.name,
          date: today,
          done: true,
        },
        ...arr,
      ];
    });
    if (completing) {
      rewardDailyWin(`habit:${habit.id}`, "Habit checked in");
    } else {
      notify("Check-in undone");
    }
  };

  const startModal = (type, item = null) => setModal({ type, item });

  const exportData = () => {
    const blob = new Blob([JSON.stringify({ data, questData }, null, 2)], {
      type: "application/json",
    });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const a = document.createElement("a");
    a.href = url;
    a.download = `day-by-day-comeback90-backup-${today}.json`;
    a.click();
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
    notify("Backup exported");
  };

  const importData = (file) => {
    if (!file) return;
    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = () => {
      try {
        const parsed = JSON.parse(reader.result);
        if (!parsed || typeof parsed !== "object") {
          throw new Error("Invalid JSON");
        }
        const importedData =
          parsed.data && typeof parsed.data === "object"
            ? parsed.data
            : parsed;
        const def = createInitialData();
        setData((old) => ({
          ...def,
          ...old,
          ...importedData,
          profile: {
            ...def.profile,
            ...(old.profile || {}),
            ...(importedData.profile || {}),
            targets: {
              ...def.profile.targets,
              ...(old.profile?.targets || {}),
              ...(importedData.profile?.targets || {}),
            },
          },
          days: { ...(old.days || {}), ...(importedData.days || {}) },
          groom: { ...(old.groom || {}), ...(importedData.groom || {}) },
          settings: { ...(old.settings || {}), ...(importedData.settings || {}) },
        }));
        if (parsed.questData && typeof parsed.questData === "object") {
          setQuestData(mergeQuestData(parsed.questData));
        }
        notify("Backup restored safely");
      } catch {
        notify("That backup file is not valid");
      }
    };
    reader.readAsText(file);
  };

  const resetAll = () => {
    if (
      window.confirm(
        "Delete all saved data from the cloud? Export a backup first if you may need it."
      )
    ) {
      setData(createInitialData());
      setQuestData(createInitialQuestData());
      setPage("Today");
      notify("Cloud data cleared");
    }
  };

  const renderModal = () => {
    if (!modal) return null;

  const renderPage = () => {

        );

            setSelectedISO={setSelectedISO}
            updateDay={updateDay}
            dm={dm}
            onAdd={() => startModal("habit")}
            onEdit={(x) => startModal("habit", x)}
            onToggle={setDoneHabit}
            onDelete={(id) => {
              const h = data.habits.find((x) => x.id === id);
              if (h && !h.archived) {
                update("habits", (a) =>
                  a.map((x) => (x.id === id ? { ...x, archived: true } : x))
                );
                notify("Habit archived; history kept");
              } else {
                removeRecord("habits", id);
              }
            }}
            showArchived={showArchived}
            setShowArchived={setShowArchived}
            query={query}
            setQuery={setQuery}
            onOpenSOS={() => setSosOpen(true)}
          />
        );
      case "Quest":

        );
      case "Goals":
        return (

            onEdit={(item) => startModal("goal", item)}
            onMilestone={(goal) => startModal("milestone", goal)}
            onMeasure={(goal) => startModal("measurement", goal)}
            onDelete={(key, id, mode) => {
              if (mode === "toggle" && key === "milestones") {
                update("milestones", (a) =>
                  a.map((m) => (m.id === id ? { ...m, done: !m.done } : m))
                );
              } else if (mode === "restore" && key === "goals") {
                update("goals", (a) =>
                  a.map((g) => (g.id === id ? { ...g, archived: false } : g))
                );
              } else if (key === "goals") {
                update("goals", (a) =>
                  a.map((g) => (g.id === id ? { ...g, archived: true } : g))
                );

      case "Reflection":
        return (
          <ReflectionAndMindPage
            data={data}

    return (
      <main className="main-area cloud-loading">

          <h1>{connectionError ? "Can't connect to the database" : "Loading your saved dataâ€¦"}</h1>
          {connectionError && (
            <>
              <p>{connectionError}</p>
              <Button kind="primary" onClick={connectToCloud}>
                Retry connection
              </Button>
            </>
          )}
        </div>
      </main>
    );
  }

    connecting: "Connecting to cloudâ€¦",

const CLASS_TIERS = [
  { rank: "E", title: "Awakening", minLevel: 1 },
  { rank: "D", title: "Initiate", minLevel: 4 },
  { rank: "C", title: "Strider", minLevel: 7 },
  { rank: "B", title: "Vanguard", minLevel: 10 },
  { rank: "A", title: "Elite", minLevel: 13 },
  { rank: "S", title: "Sovereign", minLevel: 16 },
  { rank: "SS", title: "Mythic", minLevel: 19 },
  { rank: "SSS", title: "Apex", minLevel: 22 },
  { rank: "X", title: "Transcendent", minLevel: 25 },
];

const REWARD_IDEAS = {
  2: "A guilt-free evening for a favorite film, game, or hobby",
  4: "A favorite meal or cafÃ© visit within your usual budget",
  7: "A book, art supply, or small hobby upgrade you will use",
  10: "A half-day outing or activity you have been looking forward to",
  13: "A planned experience or hobby upgrade within your budget",
  16: "A day trip, class, or meaningful upgrade you have saved for",
  19: "A bigger experience you can comfortably plan and afford",
  22: "A memorable day doing something that matters to you",
  25: "A meaningful reward you choose and plan in advance",
};

const REWARD_LEVELS = [2, 4, 7, 10, 13, 16, 19, 22, 25];

function xpForLevel(level) {
  const completedLevels = Math.max(0, Math.floor(level) - 1);
  return 600 * completedLevels + 50 * completedLevels * (completedLevels - 1);
}

function getLevelProgress(totalXP) {
  const xp = Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(totalXP) || 0));
  let level = 1;
  while (xp >= xpForLevel(level + 1)) level += 1;

  const levelStartXP = xpForLevel(level);
  const nextLevelXP = xpForLevel(level + 1);
  return {
    level,
    totalXP: xp,
    levelStartXP,
    nextLevelXP,
    levelProgressXP: xp - levelStartXP,
    levelRequirementXP: nextLevelXP - levelStartXP,
    progressPercent: Math.round(
      ((xp - levelStartXP) / (nextLevelXP - levelStartXP)) * 100
    ),
  };
}

function getClassForLevel(level) {
  return (
    [...CLASS_TIERS].reverse().find((tier) => level >= tier.minLevel) ||
    CLASS_TIERS[0]
  );
}

function getNextClassForLevel(level) {
  return CLASS_TIERS.find((tier) => tier.minLevel > level) || null;
}

export {
  CLASS_TIERS,
  REWARD_IDEAS,
  REWARD_LEVELS,
  getClassForLevel,
  getLevelProgress,
  getNextClassForLevel,
  xpForLevel,
};

import React, {
  useState,
  useEffect,
  useMemo,
  useCallback,
  useRef,
} from "react";

const STORAGE_KEY = "day-by-day-comeback90-unified-v1";

const QUEST_KEY = "day-by-day-comeback90-quest-v1";

const SCHEMA_VERSION = 2;

const WEEKDAY_KEYS = ["Sun", "Mon", "Tue", "Wed", "Thu", "Fri", "Sat"];

/* â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â• DATE & MATH UTILITIES â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â• */

const todayISO = (dateObj = new Date()) => {
  const d =
    dateObj instanceof Date && !Number.isNaN(dateObj.getTime())
      ? dateObj
      : new Date();
  return `${d.getFullYear()}-${String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(
    2,
    "0"
  )}-${String(d.getDate()).padStart(2, "0")}`;
};

const uid = () =>
  `${Date.now().toString(36)}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8)}`;

const parseLocalISO = (isoStr) => {
  if (typeof isoStr !== "string" || !/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(isoStr)) {
    const now = new Date();
    return new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), now.getDate());
  }
  const [y, m, d] = isoStr.split("-").map(Number);
  return new Date(y, m - 1, d);
};

const addDaysISO = (isoStr, deltaDays) => {
  const dt = parseLocalISO(isoStr);
  dt.setDate(dt.getDate() + Number(deltaDays || 0));
  return todayISO(dt);
};

const dayDiff = (startISO, endISO) => {
  const a = parseLocalISO(startISO);
  const b = parseLocalISO(endISO);
  return Math.round((b.getTime() - a.getTime()) / 86400000);
};

const getDayIndex = (startDateISO, currentISO = todayISO()) =>
  Math.max(1, dayDiff(startDateISO, currentISO) + 1);

const getWeekdayShort = (isoStr = todayISO()) =>
  WEEKDAY_KEYS[parseLocalISO(isoStr).getDay()] || "Mon";

const fmtDate = (value) =>
  value
    ? new Date(`${value}T12:00:00`).toLocaleDateString(undefined, {
        month: "short",
        day: "numeric",
        year: "numeric",
      })
    : "No date";

const percent = (n, d) => (d ? Math.round((n / d) * 100) : 0);

const safeNum = (val, fallback = 0) => {
  const n = Number(val);
  return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : fallback;
};

const clamp = (val, min, max) => Math.max(min, Math.min(max, safeNum(val, min)));

function discreetText(str, isDiscreet) {
  if (!isDiscreet || typeof str !== "string") return str;
  return str
    .replace(/\bpornography\b/gi, "Streak A")
    .replace(/\bporn\b/gi, "Streak A")
    .replace(/\bmasturbation\b/gi, "Habit B")
    .replace(/\bmasturbate\b/gi, "Habit B")
    .replace(/\brelapses\b/gi, "Resets")
    .replace(/\brelapse\b/gi, "Reset")
    .replace(/\bslips\b/gi, "Resets")
    .replace(/\bslip\b/gi, "Reset")
    .replace(/\burges\b/gi, "Cravings")
    .replace(/\burge\b/gi, "Craving");
}

/* â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â• SINGLETON WEB AUDIO & HAPTIC ENGINE â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â• */

let sharedAudioCtx = null;
let activeNoiseNode = null;
let activeNoiseGain = null;

function getAudioContext() {
  if (typeof window === "undefined") return null;
  const AudioCtx = window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext;
  if (!AudioCtx) return null;
  if (!sharedAudioCtx || sharedAudioCtx.state === "closed") {
    sharedAudioCtx = new AudioCtx();
  }
  if (sharedAudioCtx.state === "suspended") {
    sharedAudioCtx.resume().catch(() => {});
  }
  return sharedAudioCtx;
}

/**
 * Upgraded multi-mode acoustic & haptic feedback.
 * Modes: "success" (default 528Hz + 660Hz chord), "tick", "levelUp", "breatheIn", "breatheOut"
 */
function triggerCompletionFeedback(mode = "success") {
  try {
    if (typeof navigator !== "undefined" && navigator.vibrate) {
      if (mode === "tick") navigator.vibrate(15);
      else if (mode === "levelUp") navigator.vibrate([50, 40, 60, 40, 120]);
      else navigator.vibrate([60, 40, 80]);
    }
  } catch {
    /* ignore vibration restriction */
  }
  try {
    const ctx = getAudioContext();
    if (!ctx) return;
    const now = ctx.currentTime;

    const playTone = (freq, startOffset, duration, peakGain = 0.12, type = "sine") => {
      const osc = ctx.createOscillator();
      const gain = ctx.createGain();
      osc.type = type;
      osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(freq, now + startOffset);
      gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001, now + startOffset);
      gain.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(peakGain, now + startOffset + 0.02);
      gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0008, now + startOffset + duration);
      osc.connect(gain);
      gain.connect(ctx.destination);
      osc.start(now + startOffset);
      osc.stop(now + startOffset + duration + 0.02);
    };

    if (mode === "tick") {
      playTone(880, 0, 0.06, 0.05, "triangle");
    } else if (mode === "breatheIn") {
      playTone(432, 0, 0.55, 0.06, "sine");
    } else if (mode === "breatheOut") {
      playTone(324, 0, 0.65, 0.06, "sine");
    } else if (mode === "levelUp") {
      playTone(523.25, 0, 0.18, 0.11); // C5
      playTone(659.25, 0.12, 0.22, 0.11); // E5
      playTone(783.99, 0.24, 0.28, 0.12); // G5
      playTone(1046.5, 0.38, 0.6, 0.14); // C6
    } else {
      playTone(528, 0, 0.45, 0.11);
      playTone(660, 0.09, 0.65, 0.09);
    }
  } catch {
    /* ignore audio restriction */
  }
}

/**
 * Pure Web-Audio Brown Noise Generator for Deep Focus (Apna Kaam) blocks.
 * Requires zero external audio files and works 100% offline.
 */
const AmbientSoundEngine = {
  startBrownNoise(volume = 0.08) {
    try {
      const ctx = getAudioContext();
      if (!ctx) return false;
      this.stop();
      const bufferSize = ctx.sampleRate * 3;
      const buffer = ctx.createBuffer(1, bufferSize, ctx.sampleRate);
      const output = buffer.getChannelData(0);
      let lastOut = 0.0;
      for (let i = 0; i < bufferSize; i += 1) {
        const white = Math.random() * 2 - 1;
        output[i] = (lastOut + 0.02 * white) / 1.02;
        lastOut = output[i];
        output[i] *= 3.5;
      }
      const whiteNoise = ctx.createBufferSource();
      whiteNoise.buffer = buffer;
      whiteNoise.loop = true;

      const filter = ctx.createBiquadFilter();
      filter.type = "lowpass";
      filter.frequency.setValueAtTime(420, ctx.currentTime);

      const gainNode = ctx.createGain();
      gainNode.gain.setValueAtTime(0.001, ctx.currentTime);
      gainNode.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(
        clamp(volume, 0.01, 0.3),
        ctx.currentTime + 0.6
      );

      whiteNoise.connect(filter);
      filter.connect(gainNode);
      gainNode.connect(ctx.destination);
      whiteNoise.start(0);

      activeNoiseNode = whiteNoise;
      activeNoiseGain = gainNode;
      return true;
    } catch {
      return false;
    }
  },
  stop() {
    try {
      if (activeNoiseNode) {
        activeNoiseNode.stop();
        activeNoiseNode.disconnect();
        activeNoiseNode = null;
      }
      if (activeNoiseGain) {
        activeNoiseGain.disconnect();
        activeNoiseGain = null;
      }
    } catch {
      /* ignore */
    }
  },
};
