function readUnifiedStore() {
  const def = createInitialData();
  try {
    if (typeof window === "undefined" || !window.localStorage) return def;
    const raw = window.localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);
    if (!raw) return def;
    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
    if (!parsed || typeof parsed !== "object") return def;
    return {
      ...def,
      ...parsed,
      version: SCHEMA_VERSION,
      profile: {
        ...def.profile,
        ...(parsed.profile || {}),
        targets: {
          ...def.profile.targets,
          ...((parsed.profile && parsed.profile.targets) || {}),
        },
      },
      days: { ...def.days, ...(parsed.days || {}) },
      groom: { ...def.groom, ...(parsed.groom || {}) },
      settings: { ...def.settings, ...(parsed.settings || {}) },
    };
  } catch {
    return def;
  }
}

function exportStoreBackup(dataObj) {
  const payload = {
    app: "day-by-day-comeback90",
    exportedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    schemaVersion: SCHEMA_VERSION,
    data: dataObj || createInitialData(),
  };
  return JSON.stringify(payload, null, 2);
}

function importStoreBackup(jsonString) {
  try {
    const parsed = JSON.parse(jsonString);
    const rawData = parsed && parsed.data ? parsed.data : parsed;
    if (!rawData || typeof rawData !== "object" || !rawData.profile) {
      return { ok: false, error: "Invalid backup file structure." };
    }
    const def = createInitialData();
    const merged = {
      ...def,
      ...rawData,
      profile: { ...def.profile, ...(rawData.profile || {}) },
      days: { ...def.days, ...(rawData.days || {}) },
    };
    return { ok: true, data: merged };
  } catch (err) {
    return { ok: false, error: err?.message || "Failed to parse JSON." };
  }
}

/* â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â• SCORING, STREAKS & ANALYTICS ENGINES â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â• */

function calcDailyScoreBreakdown(dayObj, profile) {
  if (!dayObj || typeof dayObj !== "object") {
    return {
      total: 0,
      coreClean: 0,
      fitnessBody: 0,
      sleepScreen: 0,
      mindCare: 0,
    };
  }
  const c = dayObj.c || {};
  const targets = (profile && profile.targets) || {
    steps: 8000,
    waterL: 3,
    screenH: 3,
  };

  /* 1. Core Clean Streak (20 pts) */
  let coreClean = 0;
  if (c.noPorn && !dayObj.slip) coreClean += 20;

  /* 2. Fitness, Steps, Protein & Hydration (32 pts) */
  let fitnessBody = 0;
  const workoutSetsDone = safeNum(dayObj.homeWorkoutLog?.totalSets, 0);
  const yogaSetsDone = safeNum(dayObj.yogaLog?.setsDone, 0);
  if (c.workout || workoutSetsDone >= 6 || yogaSetsDone >= 5) {
    fitnessBody += 12;
  } else if (workoutSetsDone > 0 || yogaSetsDone > 0) {
    const combinedSets = workoutSetsDone + yogaSetsDone;
    fitnessBody += Math.min(1, combinedSets / 6) * 12;
  }

  const targetSteps = Math.max(1000, safeNum(targets.steps, 8000));
  const actualSteps = Math.max(0, safeNum(dayObj.steps, 0));
  if (c.steps) fitnessBody += 8;
  else if (actualSteps > 0)
    fitnessBody += Math.min(1, actualSteps / targetSteps) * 8;

  const targetProteinMin = Math.round(
    Math.max(40, safeNum(profile?.weightKg, 56)) * 1.6
  );
  const loggedProteinG = Array.isArray(dayObj.dietItems)
    ? dayObj.dietItems.reduce((sum, item) => sum + safeNum(item.protein, 0), 0)
    : 0;
  if (c.protein || loggedProteinG >= targetProteinMin) {
    fitnessBody += 8;
  } else if (loggedProteinG > 0) {
    fitnessBody += Math.min(1, loggedProteinG / targetProteinMin) * 8;
  }

  const targetWater = Math.max(1, safeNum(targets.waterL, 3));
  const actualWater = Math.max(0, safeNum(dayObj.waterL, 0));
  if (c.water) fitnessBody += 4;
  else if (actualWater > 0)
    fitnessBody += Math.min(1, actualWater / targetWater) * 4;

  /* 3. Sleep & Screen Anchor (35 pts) */
  let sleepScreen = 0;
  if (c.wakeOnTime || safeNum(dayObj.sleepH, 0) >= 7) sleepScreen += 8;
  if (c.sunlight) sleepScreen += 8;
  const targetScreen = Math.max(0.5, safeNum(targets.screenH, 3));
  const actualScreen = Math.max(0, safeNum(dayObj.screenH, 0));
  if (c.screenUnderLimit) sleepScreen += 8;
  else if (actualScreen > 0 && actualScreen <= targetScreen) sleepScreen += 8;
  if (c.bedOnTime) sleepScreen += 6;
  if (c.phoneOutside) sleepScreen += 5;

  /* 4. Mind, Skincare & Social Care (13 pts) */
  let mindCare = 0;
  const skinStepsCount = dayObj.skinCareSteps
    ? Object.values(dayObj.skinCareSteps).filter(Boolean).length
    : 0;
  if (c.skincare || skinStepsCount >= 2) mindCare += 5;
  else if (skinStepsCount === 1) mindCare += 2.5;

  if (c.braveSocial) mindCare += 4;
  if (
    c.journal ||
    (typeof dayObj.journal === "string" && dayObj.journal.trim().length > 3)
  ) {
    mindCare += 4;
  }

  const total = Math.min(
    100,
    Math.round(coreClean + fitnessBody + sleepScreen + mindCare)
  );
  return {
    total,
    coreClean: Math.round(coreClean),
    fitnessBody: Math.round(fitnessBody),
    sleepScreen: Math.round(sleepScreen),
    mindCare: Math.round(mindCare),
  };
}

function calcDailyScore(dayObj, profile) {
  return calcDailyScoreBreakdown(dayObj, profile).total;
}

function calcStreaks(daysMap, profile, currentISO = todayISO()) {
  const map = daysMap || {};
  const startISO = (profile && profile.startDate) || currentISO;
  const allDates = Array.from(
    new Set([...Object.keys(map), currentISO])
  ).sort();
  const earliestISO = allDates[0] < startISO ? allDates[0] : startISO;
  const totalSpan = Math.max(0, dayDiff(earliestISO, currentISO));

  let totalClean = 0;
  let cleanLast30 = 0;
  let bestClean = 0;
  let runningClean = 0;
  let bestRoutine = 0;
  let runningRoutine = 0;
  let minDaysHitLast14 = 0;

  const scoresLast7 = [];
  const scoresLast30 = [];

  for (let i = 0; i <= totalSpan; i += 1) {
    const dISO = addDaysISO(earliestISO, i);
    const dObj = map[dISO];
    const isClean = Boolean(dObj && dObj.c && dObj.c.noPorn && !dObj.slip);
    const isSlip = Boolean(dObj && dObj.slip);
    const score = dObj ? calcDailyScore(dObj, profile) : 0;
    const daysAgo = dayDiff(dISO, currentISO);

    if (daysAgo >= 0 && daysAgo < 7) scoresLast7.push(score);
    if (daysAgo >= 0 && daysAgo < 30) scoresLast30.push(score);
    if (daysAgo >= 0 && daysAgo < 14 && dObj && dObj.c) {
      const minDone = MINIMUM_DAY_IDS.every((id) => Boolean(dObj.c[id]));
      if (minDone) minDaysHitLast14 += 1;
    }

    if (isClean) {
      totalClean += 1;
      runningClean += 1;
      if (runningClean > bestClean) bestClean = runningClean;
    } else if (isSlip || dISO < currentISO) {
      runningClean = 0;
    }
    if (daysAgo < 30 && isClean) cleanLast30 += 1;

    if (score >= 60) {
      runningRoutine += 1;
      if (runningRoutine > bestRoutine) bestRoutine = runningRoutine;
    } else if (dISO < currentISO) {
      runningRoutine = 0;
    }
  }

  const todayObj = map[currentISO];
  const todayLoggedClean = Boolean(
    todayObj && todayObj.c && todayObj.c.noPorn && !todayObj.slip
  );
  const todaySlipped = Boolean(todayObj && todayObj.slip);

  let currentClean = 0;
  if (!todaySlipped) {
    let cursor = todayLoggedClean ? currentISO : addDaysISO(currentISO, -1);
    while (cursor >= earliestISO) {
      const obj = map[cursor];
      if (obj && obj.c && obj.c.noPorn && !obj.slip) {
        currentClean += 1;
        cursor = addDaysISO(cursor, -1);
      } else break;
    }
  }

  let currentRoutine = 0;
  const todayScore = todayObj ? calcDailyScore(todayObj, profile) : 0;
  let rCursor = todayScore >= 60 ? currentISO : addDaysISO(currentISO, -1);
  while (rCursor >= earliestISO) {
    const obj = map[rCursor];
    const sc = obj ? calcDailyScore(obj, profile) : 0;
    if (sc >= 60) {
      currentRoutine += 1;
      rCursor = addDaysISO(rCursor, -1);
    } else break;
  }

  const avg7 = scoresLast7.length
    ? Math.round(scoresLast7.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / scoresLast7.length)
    : 0;
  const avg30 = scoresLast30.length
    ? Math.round(scoresLast30.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / scoresLast30.length)
    : 0;
  const momentumDelta = avg7 - avg30;

  return {
    currentClean,
    bestClean: Math.max(bestClean, currentClean),
    totalClean,
    cleanLast30,
    currentRoutine,
    bestRoutine: Math.max(bestRoutine, currentRoutine),
    avg7,
    avg30,
    momentumDelta,
    minDaysHitLast14,
  };
}

/**
 * Upgraded Sports-Science Body & Nutrition Engine
 * Includes Normalized FFMI (1.80m standard), complete macro split, creatine & electrolytes,
 * and Golden Ratio (1.618) vs Athletic (1.45) shoulder-to-waist targets.
 */
function calcBodyMetrics(profile, measures = []) {
  const heightCm = clamp(profile?.heightCm, 120, 230);
  const weightKg = clamp(profile?.weightKg, 30, 200);
  const age = clamp(profile?.age, 16, 80);
  const mult = clamp(profile?.activity, 1.2, 1.9);
  const requestedGoal = profile?.calcGoal || "gain";

  const heightM = heightCm / 100;
  const bmi = +(weightKg / (heightM * heightM)).toFixed(1);
  const isUnderweight = bmi < 18.5;

  let bmiCategory = "Normal weight";
  if (bmi < 18.5) bmiCategory = "Underweight (BMI below 18.5)";
  else if (bmi >= 25 && bmi < 30) bmiCategory = "Overweight";
  else if (bmi >= 30) bmiCategory = "Obese";

  // Mifflin-St Jeor equation (Male baseline)
  const bmr = Math.round(10 * weightKg + 6.25 * heightCm - 5 * age + 5);
  const tdee = Math.round(bmr * mult);

  const loseBlocked = isUnderweight && requestedGoal === "lose";
  const effectiveGoal = loseBlocked ? "gain" : requestedGoal;

  let calMin = tdee;
  let calMax = tdee;
  if (effectiveGoal === "gain") {
    calMin = tdee + 300;
    calMax = tdee + 450;
  } else if (effectiveGoal === "lose") {
    calMin = Math.max(1450, tdee - 500);
    calMax = Math.max(1550, tdee - 350);
  }

  let swRatio = null;
  let waistCm = 74;
  let neckCm = 35.5;
  let shoulderCm = 105;

  if (Array.isArray(measures) && measures.length > 0) {
    const latest = measures[measures.length - 1];
    const sh = safeNum(latest.shoulder, 0);
    const wa = safeNum(latest.waist, 0);
    if (sh > 0) shoulderCm = sh;
    if (wa > 0) waistCm = wa;
    if (safeNum(latest.neck, 0) > 0) neckCm = safeNum(latest.neck, 35.5);
    if (sh > 0 && wa > 0) swRatio = +(sh / wa).toFixed(2);
  }

  // US Navy Body Fat Formula
  const diff = Math.max(12, waistCm - neckCm);
  const rawBf =
    495 /
      (1.0324 -
        0.19077 * Math.log10(diff) +
        0.15456 * Math.log10(heightCm)) -
    450;
  const bodyFatPct = +clamp(rawBf, 5, 40).toFixed(1);
  const leanMassKg = +(weightKg * (1 - bodyFatPct / 100)).toFixed(1);
  const ffmi = +(leanMassKg / (heightM * heightM)).toFixed(1);
  // Normalized FFMI adjusted to 1.80m stature
  const normalizedFfmi = +(ffmi + 6.1 * (1.8 - heightM)).toFixed(1);

  const idealShoulderCm = +(waistCm * 1.45).toFixed(1);
  const goldenShoulderCm = +(waistCm * 1.618).toFixed(1);
  const shoulderGapCm = +(idealShoulderCm - shoulderCm).toFixed(1);

  const proteinMin = Math.round(weightKg * 1.6);
  const proteinMax = Math.round(weightKg * 2.0);
  const targetKcalMid = Math.round((calMin + calMax) / 2);
  const proteinTargetG = Math.round((proteinMin + proteinMax) / 2);
  const fatTargetG = Math.round((targetKcalMid * 0.25) / 9);
  const carbsTargetG = Math.max(
    100,
    Math.round((targetKcalMid - proteinTargetG * 4 - fatTargetG * 9) / 4)
  );

  const waterRecommendedL = +(weightKg * 0.042).toFixed(1);
  const creatineG = +(weightKg * 0.06).toFixed(1);

  return {
    heightCm,
    weightKg,
    age,
    bmi,
    bmiCategory,
    isUnderweight,
    loseBlocked,
    effectiveGoal,
    bmr,
    tdee,
    calMin,
    calMax,
    targetKcalMid,
    proteinMin,
    proteinMax,
    macros: {
      proteinG: proteinTargetG,
      carbsG: carbsTargetG,
      fatG: fatTargetG,
    },
    swRatio,
    bodyFatPct,
    leanMassKg,
    ffmi,
    normalizedFfmi,
    shoulderCm,
    waistCm,
    idealShoulderCm,
    goldenShoulderCm,
    shoulderGapCm,
    waterRecommendedL,
    creatineG,
  };
}

/**
 * Calculates optimal bedtimes based on 90-minute ultradian sleep cycles
 * and a 15-minute sleep onset latency.
 */
function calcSleepCycles(wakeTimeStr = "07:00") {
  const parts = String(wakeTimeStr || "07:00")
    .split(":")
    .map(Number);
  const wakeH = Number.isFinite(parts[0]) ? parts[0] : 7;
  const wakeM = Number.isFinite(parts[1]) ? parts[1] : 0;
  const wakeTotalMins = wakeH * 60 + wakeM;

  // 6 cycles (9h), 5 cycles (7.5h - Ideal), 4 cycles (6h - Minimum)
  return [6, 5, 4].map((cycles) => {
    const sleepDurationMins = cycles * 90;
    const fallAsleepBuffer = 15;
    let bedMins = wakeTotalMins - sleepDurationMins - fallAsleepBuffer;
    while (bedMins < 0) bedMins += 1440;
    const hh = String(Math.floor(bedMins / 60) % 24).padStart(2, "0");
    const mm = String(bedMins % 60).padStart(2, "0");
    return {
      cycles,
      hours: +(sleepDurationMins / 60).toFixed(1),
      bedtime: `${hh}:${mm}`,
      label:
        cycles === 5
          ? "Optimal (5 Cycles Â· 7.5h)"
          : cycles === 6
          ? "Deep Recovery (6 Cycles Â· 9h)"
          : "Minimum Anchor (4 Cycles Â· 6h)",
    };
  });
}

/**
 * Calculates CNS & Daily Readiness Score (0-100) from recent sleep, mood, energy & screen hygiene.
 */
function calcRecoveryReadiness(daysMap = {}, currentISO = todayISO()) {
  const todayObj = daysMap[currentISO] || {};
  const yestObj = daysMap[addDaysISO(currentISO, -1)] || {};

  const sleepH = safeNum(todayObj.sleepH || yestObj.sleepH, 7);
  const sleepScore = clamp((sleepH / 7.5) * 40, 10, 40);

  const mood = safeNum(todayObj.mood, 3);
  const energy = safeNum(todayObj.energy, 3);
  const stateScore = ((mood + energy) / 10) * 35;

  const yestPhoneOut = Boolean(yestObj?.c?.phoneOutside || todayObj?.c?.phoneOutside);
  const yestNoSlip = !yestObj?.slip && !todayObj?.slip;
  const nervousSystemScore = (yestPhoneOut ? 12 : 4) + (yestNoSlip ? 13 : 3);

  const readiness = Math.round(
    clamp(sleepScore + stateScore + nervousSystemScore, 15, 100)
  );

  let status = "Prime â€” Push Progressive Overload Today";
  let recommendation =
    "Nervous system is well-recovered. Aim to add +1 rep or slow down the eccentric on your main lifts.";
  if (readiness < 50) {
    status = "Low Recovery â€” Prioritize Minimum Day & Sunlight";
    recommendation =
      "High fatigue detected. Stick to the 3 Minimum Day anchors, get 20m sunlight, and sleep on time tonight.";
  } else if (readiness < 75) {
    status = "Steady â€” Standard Training & Clean Routine";
    recommendation =
      "Good baseline readiness. Complete your scheduled workout with clean form (stop 2 reps shy of failure).";
  }

  return { readiness, status, recommendation };
}

/**
 * Analyzes urge logs & slip days to detect the user's top trigger, location, and peak danger hour.
 */
function analyzeTriggerPatterns(urges = [], daysMap = {}) {
  const triggerCounts = {};
  const locationCounts = {};
  const hourBuckets = {
    "Morning (6amâ€“12pm)": 0,
    "Afternoon (12pmâ€“6pm)": 0,
    "Evening (6pmâ€“10pm)": 0,
    "Late Night (10pmâ€“6am)": 0,
  };

  urges.forEach((u) => {
    const trig = u.trigger || "Late night in bed";
    const loc = u.location || "Bedroom (on bed)";
    triggerCounts[trig] = (triggerCounts[trig] || 0) + 1;
    locationCounts[loc] = (locationCounts[loc] || 0) + 1;

    if (u.time || u.createdAt) {
      const dt = new Date(u.time || u.createdAt);
      const hr = Number.isNaN(dt.getHours()) ? 23 : dt.getHours();
      if (hr >= 6 && hr < 12) hourBuckets["Morning (6amâ€“12pm)"] += 1;
      else if (hr >= 12 && hr < 18) hourBuckets["Afternoon (12pmâ€“6pm)"] += 1;
      else if (hr >= 18 && hr < 22) hourBuckets["Evening (6pmâ€“10pm)"] += 1;
      else hourBuckets["Late Night (10pmâ€“6am)"] += 1;
    }
  });

  const topEntry = (obj, fallback) => {
    const entries = Object.entries(obj).sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1]);
    return entries.length ? entries[0][0] : fallback;
  };

  let lowSleepSlips = 0;
  let totalSlips = 0;
  Object.values(daysMap || {}).forEach((d) => {
    if (d && d.slip) {
      totalSlips += 1;
      if (safeNum(d.sleepH, 7) > 0 && safeNum(d.sleepH, 7) < 6.5) {
        lowSleepSlips += 1;
      }
    }
  });

  return {
    totalUrgesLogged: urges.length,
    totalSlips,
    topTrigger: topEntry(triggerCounts, "Late night in bed"),
    topLocation: topEntry(locationCounts, "Bedroom (on bed)"),
    peakWindow: topEntry(hourBuckets, "Late Night (10pmâ€“6am)"),
    lowSleepCorrelationPct: totalSlips
      ? Math.round((lowSleepSlips / totalSlips) * 100)
      : 0,
  };
}

/**
 * Evaluates recent workout logs and provides a Double-Progression coaching cue.
 */
function calcWorkoutProgression(daysMap = {}, weekday = getWeekdayShort()) {
  const plan = DAILY_HOME_WORKOUTS[weekday] || DAILY_HOME_WORKOUTS.Mon;
  const dates = Object.keys(daysMap || {}).sort().reverse();
  let lastSessionReps = 0;
  let lastSessionDate = null;

  for (let i = 0; i < dates.length; i += 1) {
    const dISO = dates[i];
    if (getWeekdayShort(dISO) === weekday) {
      const reps = safeNum(daysMap[dISO]?.homeWorkoutLog?.totalReps, 0);
      if (reps > 0) {
        lastSessionReps = reps;
        lastSessionDate = dISO;
        break;
      }
    }
  }

  const targetReps = safeNum(plan.targetReps, 140);
  const nextTargetReps =
    lastSessionReps > 0
      ? Math.max(targetReps, Math.round(lastSessionReps * 1.05))
      : targetReps;

  const overloadTip =
    lastSessionReps >= targetReps
      ? `Last ${plan.day} (${lastSessionDate}) you hit ${lastSessionReps} reps! Today aim for ${nextTargetReps} total reps OR add 1â€“2 books into your backpack.`
      : `Target ${targetReps} total clean reps today. Stop 2 reps before form breakdown on every set.`;

  return {
    weekday,
    planTitle: plan.title,
    lastSessionDate,
    lastSessionReps,
    targetReps,
    nextTargetReps,
    overloadTip,
  };
}

/* â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â• ZERO-OVERSHOOT MONOTONE CUBIC SPLINE & CHARTS â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â• */

/**
 * Fritsch-Carlson Monotone Cubic Hermite Interpolation.
 * Guarantees smooth curves that NEVER overshoot below 0 or above the local extrema.
 */
function buildSmoothSvgPath(points) {
  if (!points || points.length === 0) return "";
  if (points.length === 1) return `M ${points[0].x} ${points[0].y}`;
  if (points.length === 2) {
    return `M ${points[0].x} ${points[0].y} L ${points[1].x} ${points[1].y}`;
  }

  const n = points.length;
  const dx = [];
  const dy = [];
  const m = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < n - 1; i += 1) {
    dx[i] = points[i + 1].x - points[i].x;
    dy[i] = points[i + 1].y - points[i].y;
    m[i] = dx[i] !== 0 ? dy[i] / dx[i] : 0;
  }

  const tangents = new Array(n).fill(0);
  tangents[0] = m[0];
  tangents[n - 1] = m[n - 2];

  for (let i = 1; i < n - 1; i += 1) {
    if (m[i - 1] * m[i] <= 0) {
      tangents[i] = 0;
    } else {
      tangents[i] = (m[i - 1] + m[i]) / 2;
    }
  }

  for (let i = 0; i < n - 1; i += 1) {
    if (m[i] === 0) {
      tangents[i] = 0;
      tangents[i + 1] = 0;
    } else {
      const alpha = tangents[i] / m[i];
      const beta = tangents[i + 1] / m[i];
      const hypot = Math.hypot(alpha, beta);
      if (hypot > 3) {
        const tau = 3 / hypot;
        tangents[i] = tau * alpha * m[i];
        tangents[i + 1] = tau * beta * m[i];
      }
    }
  }

  let d = `M ${points[0].x.toFixed(1)} ${points[0].y.toFixed(1)}`;
  for (let i = 0; i < n - 1; i += 1) {
    const p0 = points[i];
    const p1 = points[i + 1];
    const segDx = dx[i] / 3;
    const cpx1 = p0.x + segDx;
    const cpy1 = p0.y + tangents[i] * segDx;
    const cpx2 = p1.x - segDx;
    const cpy2 = p1.y - tangents[i + 1] * segDx;
    d += ` C ${cpx1.toFixed(1)} ${cpy1.toFixed(1)}, ${cpx2.toFixed(
      1
    )} ${cpy2.toFixed(1)}, ${p1.x.toFixed(1)} ${p1.y.toFixed(1)}`;
  }
  return d;
}

function ModernAreaChart({
  data = [],
  maxVal = 100,
  unit = "",
  color = "#3f6952",
  secondaryData = null,
  secondaryUnit = "",
  secondaryColor = "#c89572",
  showEMA = true,
  height = 200,
}) {
  const [hoverIdx, setHoverIdx] = useState(null);
  const svgRef = useRef(null);
  const w = 620;
  const h = height;
  const padL = 36;
  const padR = 18;
  const padT = 22;
  const padB = 30;
  const plotW = w - padL - padR;
  const plotH = h - padT - padB;

  const safeMax = useMemo(() => {
    const vals = data.map((d) => safeNum(d.value, 0));
    return Math.max(maxVal || 10, ...vals, 1);
  }, [data, maxVal]);

  const coords = useMemo(
    () =>
      data.map((d, i) => {
        const val = Math.max(0, safeNum(d.value, 0));
        const x =
          data.length <= 1
            ? padL + plotW / 2
            : padL + (i / (data.length - 1)) * plotW;
        const y = padT + plotH - Math.min(1, val / safeMax) * plotH;
        return { ...d, x, y, val };
      }),
    [data, plotW, plotH, safeMax]
  );

  // 7-Point Exponential Moving Average (EMA) Trendline
  const emaCoords = useMemo(() => {
    if (!showEMA || coords.length < 3) return [];
    const k = 2 / (Math.min(7, coords.length) + 1);
    let ema = coords[0].val;
    return coords.map((pt, idx) => {
      ema = idx === 0 ? pt.val : pt.val * k + ema * (1 - k);
      const y = padT + plotH - Math.min(1, ema / safeMax) * plotH;
      return { x: pt.x, y, val: Math.round(ema * 10) / 10 };
    });
  }, [coords, showEMA, plotH, safeMax]);

  const avgY = useMemo(() => {
    if (!coords.length) return null;
    const sum = coords.reduce((acc, c) => acc + c.val, 0);
    const avg = sum / coords.length;
    if (avg <= 0) return null;
    return padT + plotH - Math.min(1, avg / safeMax) * plotH;
  }, [coords, plotH, safeMax]);

  const secCoords = useMemo(() => {
    if (!secondaryData || !secondaryData.length) return [];
    const vals = secondaryData.map((d) => safeNum(d.value, 0));
    const minS = Math.min(...vals, 0);
    const maxS = Math.max(...vals, 1);
    const span = maxS - minS || 1;
    return secondaryData.map((d, i) => {
      const val = safeNum(d.value, 0);
      const x =
        secondaryData.length <= 1
          ? padL + plotW / 2
          : padL + (i / (secondaryData.length - 1)) * plotW;
      const y = padT + plotH - ((val - minS) / span) * plotH;
      return { ...d, x, y, val };
    });
  }, [secondaryData, plotW, plotH]);

  const linePath = useMemo(() => buildSmoothSvgPath(coords), [coords]);
  const emaPath = useMemo(() => buildSmoothSvgPath(emaCoords), [emaCoords]);
  const secPath = useMemo(() => buildSmoothSvgPath(secCoords), [secCoords]);

  const areaPath = useMemo(() => {
    if (!coords.length) return "";
    const first = coords[0];
    const last = coords[coords.length - 1];
    return `${linePath} L ${last.x} ${padT + plotH} L ${first.x} ${padT + plotH} Z`;
  }, [coords, linePath, plotH]);

  const gradId = useMemo(
    () => `area-grad-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 7)}`,
    []
  );

  const handleTouchMove = useCallback(
    (e) => {
      if (!svgRef.current || !coords.length) return;
      const touch = e.touches && e.touches[0];
      if (!touch) return;
      const rect = svgRef.current.getBoundingClientRect();
      if (!rect.width) return;
      const relX = ((touch.clientX - rect.left) / rect.width) * w;
      let closestIdx = 0;
      let minDist = Infinity;
      coords.forEach((pt, idx) => {
        const dist = Math.abs(pt.x - relX);
        if (dist < minDist) {
          minDist = dist;
          closestIdx = idx;
        }
      });
      setHoverIdx(closestIdx);
    },
    [coords]
  );

  if (!data.length) {
    return <div className="empty-chart">No data points recorded yet.</div>;
  }

  const activePoint =
    hoverIdx !== null && coords[hoverIdx] ? coords[hoverIdx] : null;
  const activeEma =
    hoverIdx !== null && emaCoords[hoverIdx] ? emaCoords[hoverIdx] : null;
  const activeSec =
    hoverIdx !== null && secCoords[hoverIdx] ? secCoords[hoverIdx] : null;

  return (
    <div className="modern-chart-wrap">
      {activePoint && (
        <div className="modern-chart-tooltip">
          <span>{activePoint.fullLabel || activePoint.label}</span>
          <strong>
            {activePoint.val}
            {unit}
          </strong>
          {activeEma && (
            <small className="jsx-3b426722">
              Â· Trend: {activeEma.val}
              {unit}
            </small>
          )}
          {activeSec && (
            <em className="modern-chart-secondary">
              Â· {activeSec.val}
              {secondaryUnit}
            </em>
          )}
        </div>
      )}
      <svg
        ref={svgRef}
        viewBox={`0 0 ${w} ${h}`}
        className="modern-svg-chart"
        onMouseLeave={() => setHoverIdx(null)}
        onTouchStart={handleTouchMove}
        onTouchMove={handleTouchMove}
      >
        <defs>
          <linearGradient id={gradId} x1="0" y1="0" x2="0" y2="1">
            <stop offset="0%" stopColor={color} stopOpacity="0.24" />
            <stop offset="100%" stopColor={color} stopOpacity="0.01" />
          </linearGradient>
        </defs>

        {[0, 0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1].map((ratio, idx) => {
          const y = padT + plotH * (1 - ratio);
          const labelVal = Math.round(safeMax * ratio);
          return (
            <g key={idx}>
              <line
                x1={padL}
                y1={y}
                x2={w - padR}
                y2={y}
                stroke="var(--line)"
                strokeDasharray={ratio === 0 ? undefined : "4 4"}
              />
              <text
                x={padL - 8}
                y={y + 3}
                textAnchor="end"
                className="chart-axis-text"
              >
                {labelVal}
              </text>
            </g>
          );
        })}

        {avgY !== null && (
          <line
            x1={padL}
            y1={avgY}
            x2={w - padR}
            y2={avgY}
            stroke={color}
            strokeOpacity="0.22"
            strokeDasharray="2 4"
          />
        )}

        <path d={areaPath} fill={`url(#${gradId})`} />

        {emaCoords.length > 2 && (
          <path
            d={emaPath}
            fill="none"
            stroke={color}
            strokeOpacity="0.45"
            strokeWidth="1.75"
            strokeDasharray="3 3"
          />
        )}

        <path
          d={linePath}
          fill="none"
          stroke={color}
          strokeWidth="2.8"
          strokeLinecap="round"
        />

        {secCoords.length > 1 && (
          <path
            d={secPath}
            fill="none"
            stroke={secondaryColor}
            strokeWidth="2.2"
            strokeDasharray="5 4"
            strokeLinecap="round"
          />
        )}

        {activePoint && (
          <line
            x1={activePoint.x}
            y1={padT}
            x2={activePoint.x}
            y2={padT + plotH}
            stroke={color}
            strokeOpacity="0.35"
            strokeDasharray="3 3"
          />
        )}

        {coords.map((pt, i) => (
          <g
            key={pt.key || i}
            onMouseEnter={() => setHoverIdx(i)}
            onClick={() => setHoverIdx(i)}
            className="jsx-74fa97c2"
          >
            <rect
              x={pt.x - plotW / Math.max(2, coords.length) / 2}
              y={padT}
              width={plotW / Math.max(1, coords.length)}
              height={plotH}
              fill="transparent"
            />
            <circle
              cx={pt.x}
              cy={pt.y}
              r={hoverIdx === i ? 6 : 4}
              fill="var(--paper)"
              stroke={color}
              strokeWidth={hoverIdx === i ? 3 : 2}
            />
            {(coords.length <= 10 ||
              i % Math.ceil(coords.length / 8) === 0 ||
              i === coords.length - 1) && (
              <text
                x={pt.x}
                y={h - 8}
                textAnchor="middle"
                className="chart-axis-text"
              >
                {pt.label}
              </text>
            )}
          </g>
        ))}
      </svg>
    </div>
  );
}

function ModernDonutChart({ breakdown }) {
  const safeBreakdown = breakdown || {
    total: 0,
    coreClean: 0,
    fitnessBody: 0,
    sleepScreen: 0,
    mindCare: 0,
  };

  const segments = [
    {
      label: "Clean Streak Core",
      value: safeNum(safeBreakdown.coreClean, 0),
      max: 20,
      color: "#3f6952",
    },
    {
      label: "Workout, Steps & Diet",
      value: safeNum(safeBreakdown.fitnessBody, 0),
      max: 32,
      color: "#689677",
    },
    {
      label: "Sleep & Screen Anchor",
      value: safeNum(safeBreakdown.sleepScreen, 0),
      max: 35,
      color: "#c89572",
    },
    {
      label: "Skincare, Social & Journal",
      value: safeNum(safeBreakdown.mindCare, 0),
      max: 13,
      color: "#7d8ec4",
    },
  ];

  const size = 160;
  const stroke = 16;
  const center = size / 2;
  const radius = center - stroke;
  const circumference = 2 * Math.PI * radius;

  let accumulatedPercent = 0;

  return (
    <div
      className="modern-donut-layout"
      role="group"
      aria-label={`Today's score: ${safeNum(safeBreakdown.total, 0)} out of 100 points`}
    >
      <div className="donut-svg-box">
        <svg
          width={size}
          height={size}
          viewBox={`0 0 ${size} ${size}`}
          role="img"
          aria-label={`Score breakdown donut chart, ${safeNum(safeBreakdown.total, 0)} of 100 points`}
        >
          <circle
            cx={center}
            cy={center}
            r={radius}
            fill="transparent"
            stroke="var(--sage-soft)"
            strokeWidth={stroke}
          />
          {segments.map((seg, i) => {
            const pct = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, seg.value / 100));
            const dash = pct * circumference;
            const offset = circumference - accumulatedPercent * circumference;
            accumulatedPercent += pct;
            if (seg.value <= 0) return null;
            return (
              <circle
                key={i}
                cx={center}
                cy={center}
                r={radius}
                fill="transparent"
                stroke={seg.color}
                strokeWidth={stroke}
                strokeDasharray={`${dash} ${circumference}`}
                strokeDashoffset={offset}
                strokeLinecap="round"
                transform={`rotate(-90 ${center} ${center})`}
              />
            );
          })}
          <text
            x="50%"
            y="46%"
            textAnchor="middle"
            className="donut-score-big"
          >
            {safeNum(safeBreakdown.total, 0)}
          </text>
          <text
            x="50%"
            y="63%"
            textAnchor="middle"
            className="donut-score-sub"
          >
            / 100 PTS
          </text>
        </svg>
      </div>
      <div className="donut-legend-list">
        {segments.map((seg, i) => (
          <div key={i} className="donut-legend-item">
            <span className={`donut-dot donut-dot-${i}`} />
            <div className="donut-legend-copy">
              <div className="donut-legend-heading">
                <span>{seg.label}</span>
                <strong>{seg.value}/{seg.max} pts</strong>
              </div>
              <progress
                className={`donut-category-meter donut-category-meter-${i}`}
                value={Math.min(seg.max, Math.max(0, seg.value))}
                max={seg.max}
                aria-label={`${seg.label} progress`}
              />
              <small>
                {Math.max(0, seg.max - seg.value)} pts to target
              </small>
            </div>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function ModernRadarChart({ axes = [] }) {
  const size = 240;
  const center = size / 2;
  const radius = 76;
  const count = axes.length || 6;

  const getPoint = (idx, ratio) => {
    const angle = (Math.PI * 2 * idx) / count - Math.PI / 2;
    return {
      x: center + radius * ratio * Math.cos(angle),
      y: center + radius * ratio * Math.sin(angle),
    };
  };

  const polygonPoints = axes
    .map((ax, i) => {
      const ratio = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, safeNum(ax.value, 0) / 100));
      const pt = getPoint(i, ratio);
      return `${pt.x},${pt.y}`;
    })
    .join(" ");

  return (
    <div className="modern-radar-wrap">
      <svg
        width={size}
        height={size}
        viewBox={`0 0 ${size} ${size}`}
        role="img"
        aria-label={`Holistic balance radar: ${axes
          .map(
            (axis) =>
              `${axis.label} ${Math.round(safeNum(axis.value, 0))} percent`
          )
          .join(", ")}`}
      >
        {[0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1].map((lvl, idx) => (
          <g key={idx}>
            <polygon
              points={axes
                .map((_, i) => {
                  const p = getPoint(i, lvl);
                  return `${p.x},${p.y}`;
                })
                .join(" ")}
              fill="none"
              stroke="var(--line)"
              strokeDasharray={lvl === 1 ? undefined : "3 3"}
            />
            <text
              x={center + 3}
              y={center - radius * lvl - 3}
              className="radar-scale-text"
            >
              {Math.round(lvl * 100)}
            </text>
          </g>
        ))}
        {axes.map((ax, i) => {
          const outer = getPoint(i, 1);
          const labelPt = getPoint(i, 1.24);
          return (
            <g key={i}>
              <line
                x1={center}
                y1={center}
                x2={outer.x}
                y2={outer.y}
                stroke="var(--line)"
              />
              <text
                x={labelPt.x}
                y={labelPt.y + 3}
                textAnchor="middle"
                className="radar-label-text"
              >
                {ax.label} ({Math.round(safeNum(ax.value, 0))}%)
              </text>
            </g>
          );
        })}
        <polygon
          points={polygonPoints}
          fill="rgba(63, 105, 82, 0.24)"
          stroke="var(--sage)"
          strokeWidth="2.2"
        />
        {axes.map((ax, i) => {
          const ratio = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, safeNum(ax.value, 0) / 100));
          const pt = getPoint(i, ratio);
          return (
            <circle key={i} cx={pt.x} cy={pt.y} r="3.8" fill="var(--sage)" />
          );
        })}
      </svg>
    </div>
  );
}

/* â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â• BASE UI PRIMITIVES â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â• */

function Icon({ children }) {
  return (
    <span className="icon" aria-hidden="true">
      {children}
    </span>
  );
}

function Button({ children, kind = "secondary", className = "", ...props }) {
  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      className={`btn ${kind} ${className}`.trim()}
      {...props}
    >
      {children}
    </button>
  );
}

function Card({ children, className = "", ...props }) {
  return (
    <section className={`card ${className}`.trim()} {...props}>
      {children}
    </section>
  );
}

function Heading({ eyebrow, title, description, action }) {
  return (
    <div className="page-heading">
      <div>
        <div className="eyebrow">{eyebrow}</div>
        <h1>{title}</h1>
        {description && <p>{description}</p>}
      </div>
      {action && <div className="heading-action">{action}</div>}
    </div>
  );
}

function Empty({ icon = "âœ³", title, description, action }) {
  return (
    <div className="empty">
      <div className="empty-icon">{icon}</div>
      <h3>{title}</h3>
      <p>{description}</p>
      {action}
    </div>
  );
}

function Progress({ value = 0 }) {
  const clamped = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, safeNum(value, 0)));
  return <progress className="progress-meter" value={clamped} max={100} />;
}

function Field({ label, children, hint }) {
  return (
    <label className="field">
      <span>{label}</span>
      {children}
      {hint && <small>{hint}</small>}
    </label>
  );
}

function Modal({ title, onClose, children }) {
  useEffect(() => {
    const onKey = (e) => {
      if (e.key === "Escape" && typeof onClose === "function") {
        onClose();
      }
    };
    window.addEventListener("keydown", onKey);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey);
  }, [onClose]);

  return (
    <div
      className="modal-backdrop"
      onMouseDown={(e) => e.target === e.currentTarget && onClose()}
    >
      <div className="modal" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label={title}>
        <div className="modal-head">
          <h2>{title}</h2>
          <button
            type="button"
            className="icon-button"
            onClick={onClose}
            aria-label="Close"
          >
            Ã—
          </button>
        </div>
        {children}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function Stat({ icon, label, value, detail, color }) {
  return (
    <Card className={`stat-card ${color || ""}`.trim()}>
      <div className="stat-top">
        <span className="stat-icon">{icon}</span>
        <span className="stat-label">{label}</span>
      </div>
      <div className="stat-value">{value}</div>
      <div className="stat-detail">{detail}</div>
    </Card>
  );
}

function Accordion({ title, badge, defaultOpen = false, children }) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(defaultOpen);
  return (
    <div className={`accordion-box ${open ? "open" : ""}`}>
      <button
        type="button"
        className="accordion-head"
        onClick={() => setOpen((o) => !o)}
        aria-expanded={open}
      >
        <strong>{title}</strong>
        <div className="accordion-right">
          {badge && <span className="status-pill complete">{badge}</span>}
          <span>{open ? "âˆ’" : "+"}</span>
        </div>
      </button>
      {open && <div className="accordion-body">{children}</div>}
    </div>
  );
}

function CountdownTimer({
  defaultSeconds = 600,
  label = "Timer",
  onComplete,
}) {
  const [secondsLeft, setSecondsLeft] = useState(defaultSeconds);
  const [running, setRunning] = useState(false);
  const completeRef = useRef(onComplete);

  useEffect(() => {
    completeRef.current = onComplete;
  }, [onComplete]);

  useEffect(() => {
    setSecondsLeft(defaultSeconds);
    setRunning(false);
  }, [defaultSeconds]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!running) return undefined;
    const id = window.setInterval(() => {
      setSecondsLeft((prev) => (prev > 0 ? prev - 1 : 0));
    }, 1000);
    return () => window.clearInterval(id);
  }, [running]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (running && secondsLeft === 0) {
      setRunning(false);
      triggerCompletionFeedback("success");
      if (typeof completeRef.current === "function") completeRef.current();
    }
  }, [secondsLeft, running]);

  const mins = String(Math.floor(secondsLeft / 60)).padStart(2, "0");
  const secs = String(secondsLeft % 60).padStart(2, "0");
  const elapsedPct =
    defaultSeconds > 0
      ? Math.round(((defaultSeconds - secondsLeft) / defaultSeconds) * 100)
      : 0;

  return (
    <div className="timer-strip">
      <div className="jsx-1bbd7a1c">
        <small className="eyebrow">{label}</small>
        <div className="timer-readout">
          {mins}:{secs}
        </div>
        {running && (
          <div className="jsx-a5595d11">
            <Progress value={elapsedPct} />
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
      <div className="timer-btns">
        <Button kind="primary" onClick={() => setRunning((r) => !r)}>
          {running ? "Pause" : "Start"}
        </Button>
        {running && (
          <Button
            onClick={() => setSecondsLeft((s) => s + 60)}
            title="Add 1 minute"
          >
            +1m
          </Button>
        )}
        <Button
          onClick={() => {
            setRunning(false);
            setSecondsLeft(defaultSeconds);
          }}
        >
          Reset
        </Button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function Heatmap90({
  startDate,
  daysMap,
  profile,
  selectedDate,
  onSelectDate,
}) {
  const cells = useMemo(() => {
    const start = startDate || todayISO();
    return Array.from({ length: 90 }, (_, i) => {
      const iso = addDaysISO(start, i);
      const dayObj = daysMap && daysMap[iso];
      const score = dayObj ? calcDailyScore(dayObj, profile) : 0;
      let lvl = 0;
      if (score >= 75) lvl = 3;
      else if (score >= 50) lvl = 2;
      else if (score > 0) lvl = 1;
      return {
        dayNum: i + 1,
        iso,
        score,
        lvl,
        slip: Boolean(dayObj && dayObj.slip),
      };
    });
  }, [startDate, daysMap, profile]);

  return (
    <div>
      <div
        className="heatmap-grid"
        role="group"
        aria-label="90-day comeback heatmap"
      >
        {cells.map((cell) => (
          <button
            key={cell.iso}
            type="button"
            className={`heat-cell lvl-${cell.lvl} ${
              cell.slip ? "slip-day" : ""
            } ${selectedDate === cell.iso ? "selected" : ""}`}
            onClick={() => onSelectDate(cell.iso)}
            title={`Day ${cell.dayNum} (${cell.iso}): ${cell.score}/100 pts`}
          >
            {cell.dayNum}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>
      <div className="heat-legend">
        <span className="status-pill">0 pts</span>
        <span className="status-pill complete">1â€“49 pts</span>
        <span className="status-pill complete">50â€“74 pts</span>
        <span className="status-pill complete">75â€“100 pts</span>
        <span className="status-pill peach-pill">
          Orange border = slip logged
        </span>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

/* â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â• NEW ADVANCED INTERACTIVE COMPONENTS â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â• */

const BREATH_PROTOCOLS = {
  sigh: {
    label: "Physiological Sigh (Fastest Urge & Anxiety Drop)",
    phases: [
      { name: "Deep Nose Inhale + Top-up Sip", sec: 4, scale: 1.35 },
      { name: "Brief Hold", sec: 1, scale: 1.35 },
      { name: "Long Slow Mouth Exhale", sec: 8, scale: 0.75 },
    ],
  },
  relax478: {
    label: "4-7-8 Parasympathetic Sleep & Urge Reset",
    phases: [
      { name: "Inhale Quietly Through Nose", sec: 4, scale: 1.32 },
      { name: "Hold Breath Calmly", sec: 7, scale: 1.32 },
      { name: "Slow Audible Exhale", sec: 8, scale: 0.75 },
    ],
  },
  box: {
    label: "Box Breathing 4-4-4-4 (Pre-Study Focus)",
    phases: [
      { name: "Inhale (Nose)", sec: 4, scale: 1.3 },
      { name: "Hold Full", sec: 4, scale: 1.3 },
      { name: "Exhale", sec: 4, scale: 0.78 },
      { name: "Hold Empty", sec: 4, scale: 0.78 },
    ],
  },
};

function BreathingPacer({ onCycleComplete }) {
  const [mode, setMode] = useState("sigh");
  const [active, setActive] = useState(false);
  const [phaseIdx, setPhaseIdx] = useState(0);
  const [secInPhase, setSecInPhase] = useState(0);
  const [cyclesDone, setCyclesDone] = useState(0);

  const protocol = BREATH_PROTOCOLS[mode] || BREATH_PROTOCOLS.sigh;
  const currentPhase = protocol.phases[phaseIdx] || protocol.phases[0];

  useEffect(() => {
    setPhaseIdx(0);
    setSecInPhase(0);
    setActive(false);
  }, [mode]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!active) return undefined;
    const timer = window.setInterval(() => {
      setSecInPhase((prev) => {
        if (prev + 1 < currentPhase.sec) {
          return prev + 1;
        }
        const nextIdx = (phaseIdx + 1) % protocol.phases.length;
        setPhaseIdx(nextIdx);
        if (nextIdx === 0) {
          setCyclesDone((c) => {
            const nextC = c + 1;
            if (typeof onCycleComplete === "function") onCycleComplete(nextC);
            return nextC;
          });
          triggerCompletionFeedback("breatheIn");
        } else if (
          protocol.phases[nextIdx].name.toLowerCase().includes("exhale")
        ) {
          triggerCompletionFeedback("breatheOut");
        }
        return 0;
      });
    }, 1000);
    return () => window.clearInterval(timer);
  }, [active, phaseIdx, currentPhase, protocol, onCycleComplete]);

  const remainingSec = Math.max(0, currentPhase.sec - secInPhase);

  return (
    <Card className="breathing-pacer-card">
      <div
        className="jsx-a76f9757"
      >
        <div>
          <div className="eyebrow">NERVOUS SYSTEM VAGAL RESET</div>
          <h3 className="jsx-d59d3084">{protocol.label}</h3>
        </div>
        <div className="jsx-454c221e">
          {Object.keys(BREATH_PROTOCOLS).map((k) => (
            <Button
              key={k}
              kind={mode === k ? "primary" : "secondary"}
              onClick={() => setMode(k)}
            >
              {k === "sigh" ? "Physiological Sigh" : k === "relax478" ? "4-7-8" : "Box 4-4-4-4"}
            </Button>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="breathing-pacer-stage">
        <div
          className={`breathing-pacer-orb ${active ? "is-active" : ""} ${currentPhase.scale > 1 ? "phase-expand" : "phase-contract"}`}
          data-duration={currentPhase.sec}
        >
          <strong className="jsx-df273fd7">{remainingSec}s</strong>
          <small className="jsx-c55e460c">
            Cycle #{cyclesDone + 1}
          </small>
        </div>
        <p className="jsx-710f531a">
          {active ? currentPhase.name : "Press Start and follow the expanding circle"}
        </p>
      </div>

      <div className="jsx-f120801a">
        <span className="status-pill complete">Completed: {cyclesDone} cycles</span>
        <div className="jsx-b9b7b537">
          <Button
            kind="primary"
            onClick={() => {
              if (!active) triggerCompletionFeedback("breatheIn");
              setActive((a) => !a);
            }}
          >
            {active ? "Pause Pacer" : "Start Breathing"}
          </Button>
          <Button
            onClick={() => {
              setActive(false);
              setPhaseIdx(0);
              setSecInPhase(0);
              setCyclesDone(0);
            }}
          >
            Reset
          </Button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </Card>
  );
}

/**
 * Deep Work Pomodoro & Ultradian Studio with Offline Brown Noise & Distraction Catchpad.
 */
function PomodoroFocusStudio({ onSessionComplete }) {
  const [presetMins, setPresetMins] = useState(25);
  const [secondsLeft, setSecondsLeft] = useState(25 * 60);
  const [running, setRunning] = useState(false);
  const [brownNoiseOn, setBrownNoiseOn] = useState(false);
  const [distractionInput, setDistractionInput] = useState("");
  const [distractions, setDistractions] = useState([]);

  useEffect(() => {
    setSecondsLeft(presetMins * 60);
    setRunning(false);
  }, [presetMins]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!running) {
      AmbientSoundEngine.stop();
      setBrownNoiseOn(false);
      return undefined;
    }
    const id = window.setInterval(() => {
      setSecondsLeft((prev) => (prev > 0 ? prev - 1 : 0));
    }, 1000);
    return () => window.clearInterval(id);
  }, [running]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (running && secondsLeft === 0) {
      setRunning(false);
      AmbientSoundEngine.stop();
      setBrownNoiseOn(false);
      triggerCompletionFeedback("levelUp");
      if (typeof onSessionComplete === "function") {
        onSessionComplete({
          id: uid(),
          durationMins: presetMins,
          completedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
          distractionsCaught: distractions.length,
        });
      }
    }
  }, [running, secondsLeft, presetMins, distractions.length, onSessionComplete]);

  useEffect(() => () => AmbientSoundEngine.stop(), []);

  const toggleNoise = () => {
    if (brownNoiseOn) {
      AmbientSoundEngine.stop();
      setBrownNoiseOn(false);
    } else {
      const started = AmbientSoundEngine.startBrownNoise(0.08);
      setBrownNoiseOn(started);
    }
  };

  const addDistraction = (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!distractionInput.trim()) return;
    setDistractions((prev) => [
      { id: uid(), text: distractionInput.trim(), time: new Date().toLocaleTimeString() },
      ...prev,
    ]);
    setDistractionInput("");
    triggerCompletionFeedback("tick");
  };

  const mins = String(Math.floor(secondsLeft / 60)).padStart(2, "0");
  const secs = String(secondsLeft % 60).padStart(2, "0");
  const pct = Math.round(((presetMins * 60 - secondsLeft) / (presetMins * 60)) * 100);

  return (
    <Card className="pomodoro-studio-card">
      <div className="jsx-08c2f232">
        <div>
          <div className="eyebrow">APNA KAAM Â· DEEP FOCUS ENGINE</div>
          <h3 className="jsx-d5945cb3">
            {presetMins === 25
              ? "25m Sprint (Pomodoro)"
              : presetMins === 50
              ? "50m Deep Block (GATE / Coding)"
              : "90m Ultradian Peak Block"}
          </h3>
        </div>
        <div className="jsx-fabd50d3">
          {[25, 50, 90].map((m) => (
            <Button
              key={m}
              kind={presetMins === m ? "primary" : "secondary"}
              onClick={() => setPresetMins(m)}
            >
              {m}m
            </Button>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="jsx-a90da117">
        <div className="timer-readout jsx-239fead9" >
          {mins}:{secs}
        </div>
        <Progress value={pct} />
      </div>

      <div className="jsx-21a5d782">
        <Button kind="primary" onClick={() => setRunning((r) => !r)}>
          {running ? "Pause Session" : "Start Focus Block"}
        </Button>
        <Button onClick={toggleNoise}>
          {brownNoiseOn ? "ðŸ”Š Brown Noise: ON" : "ðŸ”‡ Brown Noise: OFF"}
        </Button>
        <Button
          onClick={() => {
            setRunning(false);
            AmbientSoundEngine.stop();
            setBrownNoiseOn(false);
            setSecondsLeft(presetMins * 60);
          }}
        >
          Reset
        </Button>
      </div>

      <form onSubmit={addDistraction} className="jsx-b9b7b537">
        <input
          type="text"
          value={distractionInput}
          onChange={(e) => setDistractionInput(e.target.value)}
          placeholder="Urge or random thought? Dump it here & return to work..."
          className="jsx-634a28be"
        />
        <Button type="submit">Catch</Button>
      </form>

      {distractions.length > 0 && (
        <div className="jsx-bbf384f4">
          <small className="eyebrow">
            Parked Thoughts ({distractions.length}) â€” Deal with these AFTER the timer:
          </small>
          <ul className="jsx-d8d1425a">
            {distractions.slice(0, 4).map((d) => (
              <li key={d.id}>
                {d.text} <small className="jsx-31127fe2">({d.time})</small>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        </div>
      )}
    </Card>
  );
}

/**
 * Keyboard-first Command Palette (Ctrl+K or Cmd+K) for instant navigation & logging.
 */
function CommandPalette({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  onNavigate,
  onQuickAction,
}) {
  const [query, setQuery] = useState("");
  const inputRef = useRef(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isOpen && inputRef.current) {
      inputRef.current.focus();
    }
  }, [isOpen]);

  const actions = useMemo(
    () => [
      ...NAV.map(([tab, icon, desc]) => ({
        id: `nav-${tab}`,
        title: `Go to ${tab}`,
        sub: desc,
        icon,
        run: () => onNavigate && onNavigate(tab),
      })),
      {
        id: "act-water",
        title: "Log +0.5L Water Intake",
        sub: "Instant hydration increment for today",
        icon: "â—Œ",
        run: () => onQuickAction && onQuickAction("addWater", 0.5),
      },
      {
        id: "act-steps",
        title: "Log +1,000 Steps",
        sub: "Add 1,000 steps to today's pedometer count",
        icon: "â†—",
        run: () => onQuickAction && onQuickAction("addSteps", 1000),
      },
      {
        id: "act-minDay",
        title: "Mark Minimum Day Complete (Sunlight + Bed + Phone Out)",
        sub: "Check off the 3 core biological anchors",
        icon: "âœ¦",
        run: () => onQuickAction && onQuickAction("completeMinDay"),
      },
      {
        id: "act-sos",
        title: "Emergency Urge SOS Reset",
        sub: "Jump straight to the 7-step urge surfer & breathing pacer",
        icon: "â†»",
        run: () => onNavigate && onNavigate("Reset"),
      },
    ],
    [onNavigate, onQuickAction]
  );

  const filtered = useMemo(() => {
    const q = query.trim().toLowerCase();
    if (!q) return actions;
    return actions.filter(
      (a) =>
        a.title.toLowerCase().includes(q) || a.sub.toLowerCase().includes(q)
    );
  }, [actions, query]);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  return (
    <Modal title="Quick Command Launcher (Ctrl + K)" onClose={onClose}>
      <div className="jsx-90543b1c">
        <input
          ref={inputRef}
          type="search"
          value={query}
          onChange={(e) => setQuery(e.target.value)}
          placeholder="Type a tab name or action (e.g. Yoga, Water, SOS)..."
        />
        <div
          className="jsx-5592537a"
        >
          {filtered.map((item) => (
            <button
              key={item.id}
              type="button"
              className="btn secondary jsx-95d0747e"
              
              onClick={() => {
                item.run();
                onClose();
              }}
            >
              <span>
                <strong>
                  {item.icon} {item.title}
                </strong>
                <small className="jsx-c9f78508">
                  {item.sub}
                </small>
              </span>
              <span>â†µ</span>
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
    </Modal>
  );
}

/**
 * Data Vault Component for JSON Backup Export & Restore.
 */
function DataBackupCard({ storeData, onRestore }) {
  const [statusMsg, setStatusMsg] = useState("");

  const handleDownload = () => {
    try {
      const jsonStr = exportStoreBackup(storeData);
      const blob = new Blob([jsonStr], { type: "application/json" });
      const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
      const a = document.createElement("a");
      a.href = url;
      a.download = `comeback90-backup-${todayISO()}.json`;
      a.click();
      URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
      setStatusMsg("Backup JSON downloaded successfully.");
      triggerCompletionFeedback("success");
    } catch {
      setStatusMsg("Could not export backup.");
    }
  };

  const handleFileChange = (e) => {
    const file = e.target.files && e.target.files[0];
    if (!file) return;
    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = (ev) => {
      const res = importStoreBackup(String(ev.target?.result || ""));
      if (res.ok) {
        setStatusMsg("Backup restored! Reloading state...");
        triggerCompletionFeedback("levelUp");
        if (typeof onRestore === "function") onRestore(res.data);
      } else {
        setStatusMsg(res.error || "Invalid backup file.");
      }
    };
    reader.readAsText(file);
  };

  return (
    <Card>
      <div className="eyebrow">CLOUD DATA VAULT</div>
      <h3 className="jsx-0d966aa3">JSON Backup & Restore</h3>
      <p className="jsx-6f020693">
        Your app data is saved to the connected database. Keep a JSON backup in
        case you need to restore it later.
      </p>
      <div className="jsx-49f60068">
        <Button kind="primary" onClick={handleDownload}>
          Export JSON Backup
        </Button>
        <label className="btn secondary jsx-74fa97c2" >
          Import JSON File
          <input
            type="file"
            accept="application/json"
            onChange={handleFileChange}
            className="jsx-5790ffba"
          />
        </label>
      </div>
      {statusMsg && (
        <small className="jsx-88f62893">
          {statusMsg}
        </small>
      )}
    </Card>
  );
}

function UrgeSOSModal({ data, setData, today, dm, updateDay, onClose, notify }) {
  const [mode, setMode] = useState("sos"); // sos | slip
  const [beforeRating, setBeforeRating] = useState(7);
  const [afterRating, setAfterRating] = useState(3);
  const [trigger, setTrigger] = useState(URGE_TRIGGERS[0]);
  const [location, setLocation] = useState(URGE_LOCATIONS[0]);
  const [breathTick, setBreathTick] = useState(0);

  useEffect(() => {
    const id = window.setInterval(() => setBreathTick((t) => t + 1), 1000);
    return () => window.clearInterval(id);
  }, []);

  const phase = (() => {
    const c = breathTick % 14;
    return c < 4 ? "Breathe In (4s)" : c < 8 ? "Hold Gently (4s)" : "Slow Exhale (6s)";
  })();

  const saveOutcome = (isSlip) => {
    const entry = {
      id: uid(),
      ts: Date.now(),
      trigger,
      location,
      before: beforeRating,
      after: afterRating,
      slip: isSlip,
    };
    setData((old) => ({ ...old, urges: [entry, ...(old.urges || [])] }));
    if (isSlip) {
      updateDay(today, (prev) => ({
        ...prev,
        slip: true,
        c: { ...(prev.c || {}), noPorn: false },
      }));
      notify("Reset logged honestly. Drink water and start fresh.");
    } else {
      notify("Urge surfed! Proud of you, bhai.");
    }
    onClose();
  };

  return (
    <Modal title={dm("ðŸ›¡ Urge SOS â€” 10-Minute Rescue")} onClose={onClose}>
      <div className="record-form">
        <div className="tabs jsx-a0d4a037" >
          <button
            type="button"
            className={mode === "sos" ? "selected" : ""}
            onClick={() => setMode("sos")}
          >
            Ride Out the Wave
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            className={mode === "slip" ? "selected" : ""}
            onClick={() => setMode("slip")}
          >
            {dm("I Slipped (No-Shame Reset)")}
          </button>
        </div>

        {mode === "sos" ? (
          <div className="stack-col">
            <CountdownTimer
              defaultSeconds={600}
              label="10-Minute Urge Surfing Timer"
            />
            <div className="breath-box">
              <div className="breath-orb">
                <strong>{phase}</strong>
                <small>4-4-6 Calming Rhythm</small>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="habit-list">
              {URGE_STEPS.slice(0, 5).map((s) => (
                <div className="habit-row" key={s.step}>
                  <span className="status-pill complete">0{s.step}</span>
                  <div className="habit-info">
                    <strong>{dm(s.title)}</strong>
                    <small>{dm(s.detail)}</small>
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
            <div className="form-grid">
              <Field label="Trigger">
                <select value={trigger} onChange={(e) => setTrigger(e.target.value)}>
                  {URGE_TRIGGERS.map((t) => (
                    <option key={t} value={t}>
                      {t}
                    </option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              </Field>
              <Field label="Location">
                <select value={location} onChange={(e) => setLocation(e.target.value)}>
                  {URGE_LOCATIONS.map((l) => (
                    <option key={l} value={l}>
                      {l}
                    </option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              </Field>
              <Field label={`Intensity Before (${beforeRating}/10)`}>
                <input
                  type="range"
                  min="1"
                  max="10"
                  value={beforeRating}
                  onChange={(e) => setBeforeRating(Number(e.target.value))}
                />
              </Field>
              <Field label={`Intensity Now (${afterRating}/10)`}>
                <input
                  type="range"
                  min="1"
                  max="10"
                  value={afterRating}
                  onChange={(e) => setAfterRating(Number(e.target.value))}
                />
              </Field>
            </div>
            <div className="form-actions">
              <Button onClick={onClose}>Close</Button>
              <Button kind="primary" onClick={() => saveOutcome(false)}>
                âœ“ I Survived the Urge (Save Win)
              </Button>
            </div>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="stack-col">
            <p className="muted-text">
              One slip never deletes your total clean days. Avoid the &ldquo;chaser effect&rdquo;
              â€” leave the room, drink water, and log the trigger honestly.
            </p>
            <Field label="What triggered the slip?">
              <select value={trigger} onChange={(e) => setTrigger(e.target.value)}>
                {URGE_TRIGGERS.map((t) => (
                  <option key={t} value={t}>
                    {t}
                  </option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </Field>
            <div className="form-actions">
              <Button onClick={onClose}>Cancel</Button>
              <Button kind="primary" onClick={() => saveOutcome(true)}>
                Log Reset & Begin Again
              </Button>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </Modal>
  );
}

function ProfileModal({ profile, onClose, onSave }) {
  const [form, setForm] = useState({
    name: profile.name || "Bhai",
    startDate: profile.startDate || todayISO(),
    age: profile.age || 22,

    weightKg: profile.weightKg || 56,
    discreet: Boolean(profile.discreet),

    waterL: profile.targets?.waterL || 3,
    screenH: profile.targets?.screenH || 3,
  });

  return (
    <Modal title="Your personal space & 90-day setup" onClose={onClose}>
      <form
        className="record-form"
        onSubmit={(e) => {
          e.preventDefault();
          onSave({
            name: form.name.trim() || "Bhai",
            startDate: form.startDate,
            age: safeNum(form.age, 22),
            heightCm: safeNum(form.heightCm, 172),
            weightKg: safeNum(form.weightKg, 56),
            discreet: form.discreet,
            targets: {
              steps: safeNum(form.steps, 8000),
              waterL: safeNum(form.waterL, 3),
              screenH: safeNum(form.screenH, 3),
            },
          });
        }}
      >
        <div className="form-grid">
          <Field label="Your name or nickname">
            <input
              value={form.name}
              onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, name: e.target.value })}
            />
          </Field>
          <Field label="90-Day Start Date">
            <input
              type="date"
              value={form.startDate}
              onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, startDate: e.target.value })}
            />
          </Field>
          <Field label="Height (cm)">
            <input
              type="number"
              value={form.heightCm}
              onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, heightCm: e.target.value })}
            />
          </Field>
          <Field label="Weight (kg)">
            <input
              type="number"
              value={form.weightKg}
              onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, weightKg: e.target.value })}
            />
          </Field>
          <Field label="Daily Steps Target">
            <input
              type="number"
              value={form.steps}
              onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, steps: e.target.value })}
            />
          </Field>
          <Field label="Daily Water Target (L)">
            <input
              type="number"
              step="0.5"
              value={form.waterL}
              onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, waterL: e.target.value })}
            />
          </Field>
        </div>
        <label className="archive-toggle jsx-dffc4db2" >
          <input
            type="checkbox"
            checked={form.discreet}
            onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, discreet: e.target.checked })}
          />{" "}
          Enable Discreet Mode (Hides sensitive words across the app)
        </label>
        <p className="form-note">
          Saved to the connected database for this browser session.
        </p>
        <div className="form-actions">
          <Button onClick={onClose}>Cancel</Button>
          <Button kind="primary" type="submit">
            Save profile
          </Button>
        </div>
      </form>
    </Modal>
  );
}

function LifestyleLogModal({ item, onClose, onSave }) {

    <Modal
      title={item.kind === "routine" ? `Log Â· ${item.name}` : `Measure Â· ${item.name}`}
      onClose={onClose}
    >
      <form
        className="record-form"
        onSubmit={(e) => {
          e.preventDefault();
          onSave({ id: uid(), date, value, status, notes });
        }}
      >
        {item.kind === "metric" && (
          <Field label={`Value (${item.unit})`}>
            <input
              type="number"
              step="any"
              required
              value={value}
              onChange={(e) => setValue(e.target.value)}
            />
          </Field>
        )}
        <Field label="Date">
          <input
            type="date"
            required
            value={date}
            onChange={(e) => setDate(e.target.value)}
          />
        </Field>
        {item.kind === "routine" && (
          <Field label="Check-in status">
            <select value={status} onChange={(e) => setStatus(e.target.value)}>
              <option value="completed">Completed</option>
              <option value="partial">Partially completed</option>
              <option value="skipped">Skipped today</option>
            </select>
          </Field>
        )}
        <Field label="Notes">
          <textarea
            value={notes}
            onChange={(e) => setNotes(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="Optional noteâ€¦"
          />
        </Field>
        <div className="form-actions">
          <Button onClick={onClose}>Cancel</Button>
          <Button kind="primary" type="submit">
            Save activity
          </Button>
        </div>
      </form>
    </Modal>
  );
}

function RecordForm({ title, fields, initial, submitLabel, onCancel, onSubmit }) {
  const [values, setValues] = useState(() =>
    Object.fromEntries(
      fields.map((f) => [f.name, initial[f.name] ?? f.defaultValue ?? ""])
    )
  );
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const change = (key, value) => setValues((v) => ({ ...v, [key]: value }));

  return (
    <Modal title={title} onClose={onCancel}>
      <form
        className="record-form"
        onSubmit={(e) => {
          e.preventDefault();
          for (const f of fields) {
            if (f.required && !String(values[f.name] ?? "").trim()) {
              setError(`${f.label} is required.`);
              return;
            }
            if (
              f.type === "number" &&
              values[f.name] !== "" &&
              !Number.isFinite(Number(values[f.name]))
            ) {
              setError(`${f.label} must be a number.`);
              return;
            }
          }
          setError("");
          onSubmit(values);
        }}
      >
        <div className="form-grid">
          {fields.map((f) => (
            <Field key={f.name} label={f.label} hint={f.hint}>
              {f.type === "select" ? (
                <select
                  required={f.required}
                  value={values[f.name]}
                  onChange={(e) => change(f.name, e.target.value)}
                >
                  {(f.options || []).map(([v, l]) => (
                    <option value={v} key={v}>
                      {l}
                    </option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              ) : f.type === "textarea" ? (
                <textarea
                  required={f.required}
                  rows={3}
                  value={values[f.name]}
                  placeholder={f.placeholder || ""}
                  onChange={(e) => change(f.name, e.target.value)}
                />
              ) : (
                <input
                  required={f.required}
                  type={f.type || "text"}
                  step={f.type === "number" ? "any" : undefined}
                  value={values[f.name]}
                  placeholder={f.placeholder || ""}
                  onChange={(e) => change(f.name, e.target.value)}
                />
              )}
            </Field>
          ))}
        </div>
        {error && <p className="form-error">{error}</p>}
        <p className="form-note">
          Saved to the connected database for this browser session.
        </p>
        <div className="form-actions">
          <Button onClick={onCancel}>Cancel</Button>
          <Button kind="primary" type="submit">
            {submitLabel}
          </Button>
        </div>
      </form>
    </Modal>
  );
}

export { UrgeSOSModal, ProfileModal, LifestyleLogModal, RecordForm };


===== FILE: C:\Users\tejas\OneDrive\Desktop\LastChance\Frontend\src\hooks\usePageNav.js =====

import { useEffect, useRef, useSyncExternalStore } from "react";

/**
 * Page navigation that talks to the browser history.
 *
 * Why Back used to leave the site: when pages are just React state (setPage),
 * the browser only ever sees ONE history entry, so Back goes straight out.
 * Here every navigate() pushes a real history entry (with the URL hash, e.g.
 * #/quest), so phone Back / Android back-gesture / browser Back return to the
 * previous page, and a refresh stays on the same page.
 *
 *   const { page, direction, navigate } = usePageNav(PAGES);
 *   navigate("Quest");                       // push  -> Back returns here
 *   navigate("Quest", { replace: true });    // replace -> no extra Back step
 */

const hashOf = (page) => "#/" + encodeURIComponent(page.toLowerCase());

// Plain (non-React) store so it is easy to test and safe under StrictMode.
export function createPageNav(pages, initial = pages[0], win = window) {
  const idx = (p) => pages.indexOf(p);

  const read = () => {
    const st = win.history.state;
    if (st && pages.includes(st.page)) return st.page;
    let h = "";
    try {
      h = decodeURIComponent((win.location.hash || "").replace(/^#\/?/, ""));
    } catch (e) {
      h = "";
    }
    return pages.find((p) => p.toLowerCase() === h.toLowerCase()) || initial;
  };

  let page = read();
  let snapshot = { page, direction: 1 };
  const listeners = new Set();

  const commit = (next, direction) => {
    page = next;
    snapshot = { page, direction };
    listeners.forEach((l) => l());
  };

  // make the very first entry carry state, so coming Back to it works
  win.history.replaceState({ page }, "", hashOf(page));

  const navigate = (next, { replace = false } = {}) => {
    if (!pages.includes(next) || next === page) return;
    const direction = Math.sign(idx(next) - idx(page)) || 1;
    win.history[replace ? "replaceState" : "pushState"]({ page: next }, "", hashOf(next));
    commit(next, direction);
  };

  const onPop = (e) => {
    const next = e.state && pages.includes(e.state.page) ? e.state.page : read();
    if (next === page) return;
    commit(next, Math.sign(idx(next) - idx(page)) || -1);
  };

  // listener lives with the subscription, so StrictMode's mount/unmount/mount is fine
  const subscribe = (listener) => {
    listeners.add(listener);
    if (listeners.size === 1) win.addEventListener("popstate", onPop);
    return () => {
      listeners.delete(listener);
      if (listeners.size === 0) win.removeEventListener("popstate", onPop);
    };
  };

  return { navigate, subscribe, getSnapshot: () => snapshot };
}

export function usePageNav(pages, initial) {
  const ref = useRef(null);
  if (!ref.current) ref.current = createPageNav(pages, initial);
  const store = ref.current;

  const { page, direction } = useSyncExternalStore(
    store.subscribe,
    store.getSnapshot,
    store.getSnapshot
  );

  // every page starts at the top
  useEffect(() => {
    window.scrollTo(0, 0);
  }, [page]);

  return { page, direction, navigate: store.navigate };
}

===== FILE: C:\Users\tejas\OneDrive\Desktop\LastChance\Frontend\src\hooks\useSwipeNav.js =====

import { useEffect, useRef } from "react";

/**
 * Swipe left  -> next page,  swipe right -> previous page (order = `pages`).
 *
 * Guard rails so it never fights normal phone use:
 *  - ignores swipes that start within 24px of either screen edge
 *    (that zone belongs to Android's system Back gesture)
 *  - needs a clearly horizontal move (not a diagonal scroll)
 *  - ignores touches on inputs/sliders, inside horizontally-scrollable areas,
 *    inside anything marked data-no-swipe, and while text is selected
 */

const MIN_DIST = 70; // px
const EDGE = 24; // px
const RATIO = 1.8; // horizontal must beat vertical by this much
const SLOW_MS = 700; // slow drags must travel further
const SLOW_DIST = 140; // px

// Pure decision function: returns 1 (next), -1 (previous) or 0 (ignore)
export function swipeDirection({ dx, dy, dt, startX, vw }) {
  if (startX < EDGE || startX > vw - EDGE) return 0;
  const ax = Math.abs(dx);
  const ay = Math.abs(dy);
  if (ax < MIN_DIST || ax < ay * RATIO) return 0;
  if (dt > SLOW_MS && ax < SLOW_DIST) return 0;
  return dx < 0 ? 1 : -1;
}

function inBlockedArea(target) {
  for (let n = target; n && n !== document.body; n = n.parentElement) {
    if (n.nodeType !== 1) continue;
    if (n.hasAttribute("data-no-swipe")) return true;
    const tag = n.tagName;
    if (tag === "INPUT" || tag === "TEXTAREA" || tag === "SELECT" || n.isContentEditable) {
      return true;
    }
    const ox = getComputedStyle(n).overflowX;
    if ((ox === "auto" || ox === "scroll") && n.scrollWidth > n.clientWidth + 1) {
      return true;
    }
  }
  return false;
}

export function useSwipeNav({ page, pages, navigate, enabled = true }) {
  // always read the latest values without re-attaching listeners
  const latest = useRef({ page, pages, navigate });
  latest.current = { page, pages, navigate };

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!enabled) return undefined;
    let start = null;

    const onStart = (e) => {
      const sel = window.getSelection && window.getSelection().toString();
      if (e.touches.length !== 1 || sel || inBlockedArea(e.target)) {
        start = null;
        return;
      }
      const t = e.touches[0];
      start = { x: t.clientX, y: t.clientY, time: Date.now() };
    };

    const onEnd = (e) => {
      if (!start) return;
      const t = e.changedTouches[0];
      const dir = swipeDirection({
        dx: t.clientX - start.x,
        dy: t.clientY - start.y,
        dt: Date.now() - start.time,
        startX: start.x,
        vw: window.innerWidth,
      });
      start = null;
      if (!dir) return;
      const { page: cur, pages: list, navigate: go } = latest.current;
      const next = list[list.indexOf(cur) + dir];
      // replace: swiping through tabs doesn't pile up Back steps
      if (next) go(next, { replace: true });
    };

    const onCancel = () => {
      start = null;
    };

    document.addEventListener("touchstart", onStart, { passive: true });
    document.addEventListener("touchend", onEnd, { passive: true });
    document.addEventListener("touchcancel", onCancel, { passive: true });
    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener("touchstart", onStart);
      document.removeEventListener("touchend", onEnd);
      document.removeEventListener("touchcancel", onCancel);
    };
  }, [enabled]);
}

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";

const DAILY_GOALS = [
  {
    id: "recall",
    title: "Recall yesterday's English",
    detail: "Say 3 useful words or phrases from memory before checking notes.",
    time: "2 min",
  },
  {
    id: "shadow",
    title: "Listen, pause, and shadow",
    detail:
      "Use a short English clip. Pause after one sentence and repeat it aloud, copying the rhythmâ€”not the accent.",
    time: "5 min",
  },
  {
    id: "speak",
    title: "Speak on today's prompt",

  },

  "Describe a challenge you handled. What did you try, and what did you learn?",

  "Explain something you know how to do, as if teaching a beginner.",
  "Which is better for learning: studying alone or with others? Give an example.",
  "Talk about a person you admire and one quality you would like to develop.",
  "What would you improve in your daily routine? Why would that help?",
  "Describe a memorable conversation. What made it easy to keep talking?",
];

const EXPOSURE_STEPS = [
  {
    title: "Speak alone",
    detail: "Read a few lines aloud, then explain one idea to yourself.",
  },
  {
    title: "Make a private voice note",
    detail: "Record 30â€“60 seconds. Listen once and notice one thing you did well.",
  },
  {
    title: "Send a voice note",
    detail: "Share a short English voice message with someone you trust.",
