const WEEKLY_FOCUS = [
  "Easy start",
  "Everyday stories",
  "Opinions",
  "Ask & follow up",
  "Explain an idea",
  "Real conversation",
  "Review & repeat",
];

function dayIndex(date) {
  return Math.floor(Date.parse(`${date}T00:00:00Z`) / 86400000);
}

function CommunicationPage({ data, setData, today, notify }) {
  const [recording, setRecording] = useState(false);
  const [requestingMic, setRequestingMic] = useState(false);
  const [recordingSeconds, setRecordingSeconds] = useState(0);
  const [speechAudioUrl, setSpeechAudioUrl] = useState("");
  const [speechDuration, setSpeechDuration] = useState(0);
  const mediaRecorderRef = useRef(null);
  const streamRef = useRef(null);
  const chunksRef = useRef([]);
  const startedAtRef = useRef(0);
  const audioUrlRef = useRef("");
  const mountedRef = useRef(true);
  const micRequestRef = useRef(false);

  const practice = data.communicationPractice || {};
  const days = practice.days || {};
  const entry = days[today] || { completed: [] };
  const completed = Array.isArray(entry.completed) ? entry.completed : [];
  const level = Math.min(
    Math.max(Number(practice.exposureLevel) || 0, 0),
    EXPOSURE_STEPS.length - 1
  );
  const focusIndex = ((dayIndex(today) % WEEKLY_FOCUS.length) + WEEKLY_FOCUS.length) % WEEKLY_FOCUS.length;
  const prompt = PROMPTS[((dayIndex(today) % PROMPTS.length) + PROMPTS.length) % PROMPTS.length];
  const recentDates = Array.from({ length: 7 }, (_, index) =>
    addDaysISO(today, index - 6)
  );
  const successfulDays = recentDates.filter((date) => {
    const saved = days[date];
    return (
      saved &&
      Array.isArray(saved.completed) &&
      DAILY_GOALS.every((goal) => saved.completed.includes(goal.id))
    );
  }).length;
  const progress = Math.round((completed.length / DAILY_GOALS.length) * 100);

  useEffect(() => {
    mountedRef.current = true;
    return () => {
      mountedRef.current = false;
      if (mediaRecorderRef.current?.state === "recording") {
        mediaRecorderRef.current.stop();
      }
      streamRef.current?.getTracks().forEach((track) => track.stop());
      if (audioUrlRef.current) URL.revokeObjectURL(audioUrlRef.current);
    };
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!recording) return undefined;
    const timerId = window.setInterval(() => {
      const elapsed = Math.floor((Date.now() - startedAtRef.current) / 1000);
      setRecordingSeconds(Math.min(60, elapsed));
      if (elapsed >= 60 && mediaRecorderRef.current?.state === "recording") {
        mediaRecorderRef.current.stop();
      }
    }, 250);
    return () => window.clearInterval(timerId);
  }, [recording]);

  const startSpeechRecording = async () => {
    if (micRequestRef.current) return;
    if (!navigator.mediaDevices?.getUserMedia || !window.MediaRecorder) {
      notify("Audio recording is not supported in this browser.");
      return;
    }

    micRequestRef.current = true;
    setRequestingMic(true);
    try {
      const stream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({ audio: true });
      if (!mountedRef.current) {
        stream.getTracks().forEach((track) => track.stop());
        return;
      }
      streamRef.current = stream;
      chunksRef.current = [];
      const supportedType = [
        "audio/webm;codecs=opus",
        "audio/webm",
        "audio/mp4",
      ].find((type) => window.MediaRecorder.isTypeSupported(type));
      const recorder = new window.MediaRecorder(
        stream,
        supportedType ? { mimeType: supportedType } : undefined
      );
      recorder.ondataavailable = (event) => {
        if (event.data.size) chunksRef.current.push(event.data);
      };
      recorder.onerror = () => {
        notify("Recording stopped because the microphone reported an error.");
      };
      recorder.onstop = () => {
        stream.getTracks().forEach((track) => track.stop());
        streamRef.current = null;
        if (!mountedRef.current) return;
        setRecording(false);
        const recordingBlob = new Blob(chunksRef.current, {
          type: recorder.mimeType || "audio/webm",
        });
        if (!recordingBlob.size) {
          notify("No audio was captured. Check the microphone and try again.");
          return;
        }
        const nextUrl = URL.createObjectURL(recordingBlob);
        if (audioUrlRef.current) URL.revokeObjectURL(audioUrlRef.current);
        audioUrlRef.current = nextUrl;
        setSpeechAudioUrl(nextUrl);
        setSpeechDuration(
          Math.min(60, Math.max(1, Math.round((Date.now() - startedAtRef.current) / 1000)))
        );
        notify("Recording ready. It stays in this tab only.");
      };
      mediaRecorderRef.current = recorder;
      startedAtRef.current = Date.now();
      setRecordingSeconds(0);
      recorder.start();
      setRecording(true);
    } catch (error) {
      streamRef.current?.getTracks().forEach((track) => track.stop());
      streamRef.current = null;
      notify(
        error?.name === "NotAllowedError"
          ? "Microphone permission was denied. Allow access in browser settings to record."
          : "Could not start recording. Check that a microphone is available."
      );
    } finally {
      micRequestRef.current = false;
      if (mountedRef.current) setRequestingMic(false);
    }
  };

  const stopSpeechRecording = () => {
    if (mediaRecorderRef.current?.state === "recording") {
      mediaRecorderRef.current.stop();
    }
  };

  const deleteSpeechRecording = () => {
    if (audioUrlRef.current) URL.revokeObjectURL(audioUrlRef.current);
    audioUrlRef.current = "";
    setSpeechAudioUrl("");
    setSpeechDuration(0);
  };

  const updateToday = (update) => {
    setData((previous) => {
      const currentPractice = previous.communicationPractice || {};
      const currentDays = currentPractice.days || {};
      const currentEntry = currentDays[today] || { completed: [] };
      return {
        ...previous,
        communicationPractice: {
          ...currentPractice,
          days: {
            ...currentDays,
            [today]: update(currentEntry),
          },
        },
      };
    });
  };

  const toggleGoal = (goalId) => {
    updateToday((current) => {
      const done = Array.isArray(current.completed) ? current.completed : [];
      return {
        ...current,
        completed: done.includes(goalId)
          ? done.filter((id) => id !== goalId)
          : [...done, goalId],
      };
    });
  };

  const saveRating = (key, value) => {
    updateToday((current) => ({ ...current, [key]: value }));
  };

  const completeExposure = () => {
    if (entry.exposureDone) return;
    setData((previous) => {
      const currentPractice = previous.communicationPractice || {};
      const currentDays = currentPractice.days || {};
      const currentEntry = currentDays[today] || { completed: [] };
      if (currentEntry.exposureDone) return previous;
      return {
        ...previous,
        communicationPractice: {
          ...currentPractice,
          days: {
            ...currentDays,
            [today]: { ...currentEntry, exposureDone: true },
          },
        },
      };
    });
    notify("Speaking challenge done â€” repeat this step until it feels workable.");
  };

  const adjustExposureLevel = (direction) => {
    setData((previous) => {
      const currentPractice = previous.communicationPractice || {};
      const currentLevel = Math.min(
        Math.max(Number(currentPractice.exposureLevel) || 0, 0),
        EXPOSURE_STEPS.length - 1
      );
      return {
        ...previous,
        communicationPractice: {
          ...currentPractice,
          exposureLevel: Math.min(
            Math.max(currentLevel + direction, 0),
            EXPOSURE_STEPS.length - 1
          ),
        },
      };
    });
    notify(
      direction > 0
        ? "Moved up one step â€” only continue if it feels manageable."
        : "Moved back one step. Repeating an easier rep is progress too."
    );
  };

  return (
    <div className="speaking-page stack-col">
      <Heading
        title="Speak with more confidence"
        description="A practical English + communication routine. Progress comes from saying things out loud, not waiting until you feel perfect."
      />

      <section className="speaking-hero">
        <div>
          <h2>
            {WEEKLY_FOCUS[focusIndex] === "Ask & follow up" ? (
              <>
                Ask & <em>follow up</em>
              </>
            ) : (
              WEEKLY_FOCUS[focusIndex]
            )}
          </h2>
          <p>
            Short recall, clear speaking, and one manageable speaking rep.
            Keep your own accent; aim to be understood.
          </p>
          <p className="speaking-hinglish">
            Atak jao? Simple sentence bolo, pause lo, phir continue â€” perfect
            accent zaroori nahi.
          </p>
          <div className="speaking-progress">
            <div className="row-between">
              <strong>Today's goals</strong>
              <span>{completed.length}/{DAILY_GOALS.length} done</span>
            </div>
            <Progress value={progress} />
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="speaking-hero-stat">
          <strong>{successfulDays}<small>/7</small></strong>
          <span>complete days this week</span>
        </div>
      </section>

      <div className="speaking-layout">
        <Card className="speaking-goals-card">
          <div className="section-head">
            <div>
              <h2>Today's practice</h2>
              <p>Tick each goal when you actually say it out loud.</p>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div className="speaking-goals">
            {DAILY_GOALS.map((goal, index) => {
              const isDone = completed.includes(goal.id);
              return (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  className={`speaking-goal ${isDone ? "done" : ""}`}
                  key={goal.id}
                  onClick={() => toggleGoal(goal.id)}
                  aria-pressed={isDone}
                >
                  <span className="speaking-goal-check" aria-hidden="true">
                    {isDone ? "âœ“" : index + 1}
                  </span>
                  <span className="speaking-goal-copy">
                    <strong>{goal.title}</strong>
                    <small>{goal.detail}</small>
                  </span>
                  <span className="speaking-time">{goal.time}</span>
                </button>
              );
            })}
          </div>
          <div className="speaking-prompt">
            <span className="speaking-tag">SPEAKING PROMPT</span>
            <p>{prompt}</p>
            <div className="speaking-prep">
              <strong>Stuck? Use PREP:</strong>
              <span>Point â†’ Reason â†’ Example â†’ Point again</span>
            </div>
          </div>
          <details className="speaking-example">
            <summary>Example opening (make it your own)</summary>
            <p>
              â€œOne small win for me was ___. It mattered because ___. For
              example, ___. So I learned ___.â€
            </p>
          </details>
        </Card>

        <div className="speaking-side stack-col">
          <Card>
            <div>
              <h2 className="speaking-card-title">Start a 12-minute session</h2>
              <p className="muted-text">
                Use the timer for today's four practice goals. Pause or stop
                whenever you need.
              </p>
            </div>
            <CountdownTimer defaultSeconds={720} label="Speak, don't just study" />
          </Card>

          <Card className="speaking-reflection">
            <div>
              <h2 className="speaking-card-title">Confidence check-in</h2>
              <p className="muted-text">
                A quick self-rating helps you notice change over time. It is
                not a test of your English.
              </p>
            </div>
            <label>
              <span>Before practice</span>
              <select
                value={entry.confidenceBefore || ""}
                onChange={(event) =>
                  saveRating("confidenceBefore", event.target.value)
                }
              >
                <option value="">Choose 1â€“5</option>
                {[1, 2, 3, 4, 5].map((value) => (
                  <option value={value} key={value}>
                    {value} â€” {["very nervous", "nervous", "okay", "calm", "confident"][value - 1]}
                  </option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </label>
            <label>
              <span>After practice</span>
              <select
                value={entry.confidenceAfter || ""}
                onChange={(event) =>
                  saveRating("confidenceAfter", event.target.value)
                }

                  <option value={value} key={value}>
                    {value} â€” {["very nervous", "nervous", "okay", "calm", "confident"][value - 1]}

      <Card className="speech-recorder-card">

            <p className="muted-text">
              Answer today&apos;s prompt, listen once, and notice one strength.
              Audio stays in this tab and is never uploaded or saved.
            </p>
          </div>
          <span className={`speech-recording-indicator ${recording ? "on" : ""}`}>
            {recording ? "â— Recording" : speechAudioUrl ? "Ready to review" : "Not recording"}
          </span>
        </div>
        <div className="speech-recorder-controls">
          <strong>{recording ? `${recordingSeconds}s / 60s` : "Keep it natural; pauses are okay."}</strong>
          {recording ? (
            <Button kind="primary" onClick={stopSpeechRecording}>
              Finish &amp; review
            </Button>
          ) : (
            <Button
              kind="primary"
              onClick={startSpeechRecording}
              disabled={requestingMic}
            >
              {requestingMic
                ? "Waiting for microphoneâ€¦"
                : speechAudioUrl
                ? "Record again"
                : "Start recording"}
            </Button>
          )}
        </div>
        {speechAudioUrl && !recording && (
          <div className="speech-playback">
            <audio controls src={speechAudioUrl} aria-label="Your speaking practice recording" />
            <span>{speechDuration}s recording</span>
            <Button onClick={deleteSpeechRecording}>Delete recording</Button>
          </div>
        )}
      </Card>

      <Card className="speaking-ladder-card">
        <div className="section-head">
          <div>
            <h2>Speaking comfort ladder</h2>
            <p>
              Start at a manageable step, repeat it, and move up only when it
              feels workable. No need to force a big audience.
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <ol className="speaking-ladder">
          {EXPOSURE_STEPS.map((step, index) => (
            <li
              key={step.title}
              className={`${index === level ? "current" : ""} ${index < level ? "passed" : ""}`}
            >
              <span className="speaking-ladder-number">
                {index < level ? "âœ“" : index + 1}
              </span>
              <span>
                <strong>{step.title}</strong>
                <small>{step.detail}</small>
              </span>
              {index === level && (
                <span className="speaking-current-label">YOUR STEP</span>
              )}
            </li>
          ))}
        </ol>
        <div className="speaking-ladder-action">
          <p>
            <strong>Today's small courage rep:</strong>{" "}
            {EXPOSURE_STEPS[level].detail}
          </p>
          <div className="heading-btn-group">
            <Button
              kind="primary"
              onClick={completeExposure}
              disabled={Boolean(entry.exposureDone)}
            >
              {entry.exposureDone ? "Today's rep complete" : "I practised this step"}
            </Button>
            {level > 0 && (
              <Button onClick={() => adjustExposureLevel(-1)}>
                Make this step easier
              </Button>
            )}
            {level < EXPOSURE_STEPS.length - 1 && (
              <Button
                onClick={() => adjustExposureLevel(1)}
                disabled={!entry.exposureDone}
              >
                Move up when ready
              </Button>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
        <p className="speaking-safety-note">
          Feeling nervous is normal. Take a pause, exhale slowly, and make the
          step smaller if needed. If anxiety is intense or affecting everyday
          life, a qualified mental-health professional can help.
        </p>
      </Card>

      <Card className="speaking-science-card">
        <div className="section-head">
          <div>
            <h2>Evidence-informed, not a magic fix</h2>
            <p>
              These studies support the practice ingredients; they do not
              guarantee a specific result for every person.
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <ul className="speaking-evidence">
          <li>
            <strong>Recall beats only re-reading.</strong> Try to remember
            phrases and speak before looking at notes; a meta-analysis found
            benefits of testing/retrieval for later retention.
            <a
              href="https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/25150680/"
              target="_blank"
              rel="noreferrer"
            >
              Rowland, 2014 Â· Psychological Bulletin
            </a>
          </li>
          <li>
            <strong>Space practice across days.</strong> Brief repeated
            sessions are more useful for long-term recall than cramming; the
            cited review focused on verbal recall tasks, not specifically
            English fluency.
            <a
              href="https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/16719566/"
              target="_blank"
              rel="noreferrer"
            >
              Cepeda et al., 2006 Â· Psychological Bulletin
            </a>
          </li>
          <li>
            <strong>Build up exposure in steps.</strong> A 2025 systematic
            review/meta-analysis found psychological interventions can reduce
            public-speaking anxiety, but studies and outcomes varied.
            <a
              href="https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/39970240/"
              target="_blank"
              rel="noreferrer"
            >
              McWilliam et al., 2025 Â· Journal of Psychology
            </a>
          </li>
        </ul>
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
}

function GoalsAndRoadmapPage({
  data,
  setData,
  onAdd,
  onEdit,
  onMilestone,
  onMeasure,
  onDelete,
  notify,
}) {
  const [subTab, setSubTab] = useState("goals"); // goals | roadmap | review
  const [showArchivedGoals, setShowArchivedGoals] = useState(false);
  const [rev, setRev] = useState({ q1: "", q2: "", q3: "" });

  const today = todayISO();
  const allGoals = data.goals || [];
  const goals = allGoals.filter((g) => !!g.archived === showArchivedGoals);
  const weeklyReviews = data.weeklyReviews || [];
  const roadmapProgress = data.roadmapProgress || {};

  const handleSaveReview = () => {
    if (!rev.q1.trim() && !rev.q3.trim()) return;
    const entry = {
      id: `rev-${uid()}`,
      week: weeklyReviews.length + 1,
      date: today,
      ...rev,
    };
    setData((old) => ({
      ...old,
      weeklyReviews: [entry, ...(old.weeklyReviews || [])],
    }));
    setRev({ q1: "", q2: "", q3: "" });
    notify("Sunday weekly review saved");
  };

  const handleDeleteReview = (id) => {
    setData((old) => ({
      ...old,
      weeklyReviews: (old.weeklyReviews || []).filter((r) => r.id !== id),
    }));
    notify("Weekly review deleted");
  };

  const toggleRoadmapTask = (phaseId, taskIdx) => {
    const key = `${phaseId}-${taskIdx}`;
    setData((old) => ({
      ...old,
      roadmapProgress: {
        ...(old.roadmapProgress || {}),
        [key]: !((old.roadmapProgress || {})[key]),
      },
    }));
  };

  /* Automated Weekly KPI Snapshot for Sunday Review */
  const weeklySnapshot = useMemo(() => {
    const daysMap = data.days || {};
    let totalScore7d = 0;
    let workouts7d = 0;
    let cleanDays7d = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < 7; i += 1) {
      const iso = addDaysISO(today, -i);
      const d = daysMap[iso];
      if (d) {
        totalScore7d += calcDailyScore(d, data.profile);
        if (d.c?.workout) workouts7d += 1;
        if (d.c?.noPorn && !d.slip) cleanDays7d += 1;
      }
    }

    return {
      avgScore: Math.round(totalScore7d / 7),
      workouts: workouts7d,
      cleanDays: cleanDays7d,
    };
  }, [data.days, data.profile, today]);

  return (
    <>
      <Heading
        title="Goals & 90-day roadmap"
        description="Turn what matters to you into milestones, phased 90-day checkpoints with interactive task tracking, and Sunday weekly reviews."
        action={
          <div className="heading-btn-group">
            <Button kind="primary" onClick={onAdd}>
              ï¼‹ Create goal
            </Button>
          </div>
        }
      />

      <div className="tabs large-tabs">
        <button
          type="button"
          className={subTab === "goals" ? "selected" : ""}
          onClick={() => setSubTab("goals")}
        >
          Personal Goals &amp; Milestones <span>{goals.length}</span>
        </button>
        <button
          type="button"
          className={subTab === "roadmap" ? "selected" : ""}
          onClick={() => setSubTab("roadmap")}
        >
          90-Day Phased Roadmap <span>3 Phases</span>
        </button>
        <button
          type="button"
          className={subTab === "review" ? "selected" : ""}
          onClick={() => setSubTab("review")}
        >
          Sunday Weekly Review <span>{weeklyReviews.length}</span>
        </button>
      </div>

      {subTab === "goals" && (
        <>
          <div className="goals-summary">
            <div>
              <span>{allGoals.filter((g) => !g.archived).length}</span>
              <small>Active goals</small>
            </div>
            <div>
              <span>
                {(data.milestones || []).filter((m) => m.done).length}/
                {(data.milestones || []).length}
              </span>
              <small>Milestones complete</small>
            </div>
            <div>
              <span>{(data.measurements || []).length}</span>
              <small>Progress entries</small>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="toolbar jsx-29b7980a" >
            <div className="tabs">
              <button
                type="button"
                className={!showArchivedGoals ? "selected" : ""}
                onClick={() => setShowArchivedGoals(false)}
              >
                Active Goals ({allGoals.filter((g) => !g.archived).length})
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                className={showArchivedGoals ? "selected" : ""}
                onClick={() => setShowArchivedGoals(true)}
              >
                Archived ({allGoals.filter((g) => g.archived).length})
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="record-grid goal-grid">
            {goals.map((g) => {
              const ms = (data.milestones || []).filter(
                (m) => m.goalId === g.id
              );
              const latest = (data.measurements || [])
                .filter((m) => m.goalId === g.id)
                .sort((a, b) => (b.date || "").localeCompare(a.date || ""))[0];

              const currentVal = Number(latest?.value || g.progress || 0);
              const targetVal = Number(g.target || 100);
              const pct = g.target ? percent(currentVal, targetVal) : g.progress || 0;

              /* Velocity / Required Daily Pace */
              let paceText = "";
              if (g.due && targetVal > 0) {
                const daysRemaining = dayDiff(today, g.due);
                const unitsLeft = targetVal - currentVal;
                if (daysRemaining > 0 && unitsLeft > 0) {
                  const dailyPace = (unitsLeft / daysRemaining).toFixed(1);
                  paceText = `Pace needed: ~${dailyPace} ${
                    g.unit || "units"
                  }/day (${daysRemaining}d left)`;
                } else if (daysRemaining <= 0) {
                  paceText = "Target date reached";
                }
              }

              return (
                <Card className="goal-card" key={g.id}>
                  <div className="goal-card-head">
                    <span className="record-symbol sage">âš‘</span>

                    {g.archived
                      ? "ARCHIVED"
                      : `IN PROGRESS${
                          g.due
                            ? ` Â· DUE ${fmtDate(g.due).toUpperCase()}`
                            : ""
                        }`}
                  </span>
                  <h3>{g.title}</h3>
                  <p>{g.why || "Keep taking the next useful step."}</p>

                  {g.target && (
                    <div className="goal-progress-detail">

                        <strong>
                          {latest
                            ? `${latest.value} / ${g.target} ${g.unit || ""}`
                            : `0 / ${g.target} ${g.unit || ""}`}
                        </strong>
                      </div>
                      <Progress value={pct} />
                      {paceText && (
                        <small className="muted-text jsx-6b1f0d97" >
                          {paceText}
                        </small>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  )}

                  <div className="milestone-head">
                    <strong>Milestones</strong>
                    <span>
                      {ms.filter((m) => m.done).length}/{ms.length}
                    </span>
                  </div>

                  {ms.length ? (
                    ms.map((m) => (
                      <label className="milestone-row" key={m.id}>
                        <input
                          type="checkbox"
                          checked={!!m.done}
                          onChange={() => onDelete("milestones", m.id, "toggle")}
                        />
                        <span className={m.done ? "struck" : ""}>
                          {m.title}

                    <p className="muted-text milestone-empty">
                      Add meaningful steps to break this goal down.
                    </p>

                          ï¼‹ Milestone
                        </Button>
                        <Button onClick={() => onMeasure(g)}>Log progress</Button>
                      </>
                    )}
                    <Button onClick={() => onEdit(g)}>Edit</Button>
                    <Button
                      onClick={() =>
                        onDelete(
                          "goals",
                          g.id,
                          g.archived ? "restore" : "archive"
                        )
                      }
                    >
                      {g.archived ? "Restore" : "Archive"}
                    </Button>
                  </div>
                </Card>
              );
            })}
          </div>

          {!goals.length && (
            <Empty
              icon="âš‘"
              title={
                showArchivedGoals ? "No archived goals" : "No active goals yet"

import React, { useState, useMemo, useEffect } from "react";
import {
  uid,
  addDaysISO,
  getWeekdayShort,
  fmtDate,
  percent,
  safeNum,
  MINIMUM_DAY_IDS,
  SCORED_CHECKS,
  LIFE_BASICS,
  CONTROL_LOOPS,
  FACE_HABITS,
  DEFAULT_SCHEDULE,
  MOOD_ACTIVITIES,
  createEmptyDay,
  calcDailyScoreBreakdown,
  calcDailyScore,
  Button,
  Card,
  Heading,
  Empty,
  Progress,
  Field,
  Stat,
} from "../shared";

const HABIT_STACK_PRESETS = [
  {
    anchor: "Subah uth kar paani peene ke turant baad",
    action: "Main bistar saaf karunga aur 15 min dhoop mein walk karunga",
    category: "Morning Anchor",
  },
  {
    anchor: "Dopahar ka khana khane ke baad",
    action: "Main bina phone ke 15 min family ke saath baithunga",
    category: "Social & Mind",
  },
  {
    anchor: "Shaam ko study/kaam khatam karne ke baad",
    action: "Main turant workout kapde pehnunga aur 1st warm-up set shuru karunga",
    category: "Fitness",
  },
  {
    anchor: "Raat ko 10:00 PM alarm bajte hi",
    action: "Main phone bedroom ke bahar charge par lagaunga aur 2-line journal likhunga",
    category: "Sleep Shield",
  },
];

function HabitsAndTrackPage({
  data,
  setData,
  today,
  selectedISO,
  setSelectedISO,
  updateDay,
  dm,
  onAdd,
  onEdit,
  onToggle,
  onDelete,
  showArchived,
  setShowArchived,
  query,
  setQuery,
}) {
  const [viewMode, setViewMode] = useState("tracker"); // tracker | custom | matrix | schedule
  const [filter, setFilter] = useState("all");
  const [newWin, setNewWin] = useState("");
  const [onlyMinDay, setOnlyMinDay] = useState(false);
  const [stackInput, setStackInput] = useState({
    anchor: "",
    action: "",
    category: "Morning Anchor",
  });
  const [currentTimeStr, setCurrentTimeStr] = useState(() => {
    const now = new Date();
    return `${String(now.getHours()).padStart(2, "0")}:${String(
      now.getMinutes()
    ).padStart(2, "0")}`;
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    const timer = window.setInterval(() => {
      const now = new Date();
      setCurrentTimeStr(
        `${String(now.getHours()).padStart(2, "0")}:${String(
          now.getMinutes()
        ).padStart(2, "0")}`
      );
    }, 60000);
    return () => window.clearInterval(timer);
  }, []);

  const p = data.profile || {};
  const dayObj = (data.days && data.days[selectedISO]) || createEmptyDay();

  const breakdown = useMemo(
    () => calcDailyScoreBreakdown(dayObj, p),
    [dayObj, p]
  );

  /* 7-Day Window for Matrix & Trend */
  const last7Dates = useMemo(() => {
    return Array.from({ length: 7 }, (_, idx) => {
      const iso = addDaysISO(selectedISO, -(6 - idx));
      const d = (data.days && data.days[iso]) || createEmptyDay();
      return {
        iso,
        shortDay: getWeekdayShort(iso),
        dayNum: iso.slice(8),
        score: calcDailyScore(d, p),
        dayObj: d,
      };
    });
  }, [selectedISO, data.days, p]);

  const avg7DayScore = useMemo(() => {
    if (!last7Dates.length) return 0;
    const sum = last7Dates.reduce((acc, item) => acc + item.score, 0);
    return Math.round(sum / last7Dates.length);
  }, [last7Dates]);

  const list = (data.habits || []).filter(
    (h) =>
      !!h.archived === showArchived &&
      (filter === "all" || h.kind === filter) &&
      (h.name || "").toLowerCase().includes((query || "").toLowerCase())
  );

  const builds = (data.habits || []).filter(
    (h) => !h.archived && h.kind !== "quit"
  ).length;

  const quits = (data.habits || []).filter(
    (h) => !h.archived && h.kind === "quit"
  ).length;

  const toggleGroup = (groupKey, itemId, val) => {
    updateDay(selectedISO, (prev) => ({
      ...prev,
      [groupKey]: {
        ...(prev[groupKey] || {}),
        [itemId]: val,
      },
    }));
  };

  const toggleCoreCheck = (checkId) => {
    const currentVal = Boolean(dayObj.c && dayObj.c[checkId]);
    const nextVal = !currentVal;
    updateDay(selectedISO, (prev) => ({
      ...prev,
      slip: checkId === "noPorn" && nextVal ? false : prev.slip,
      c: {
        ...(prev.c || {}),
        [checkId]: nextVal,
      },
    }));
  };

  const markMinimumDayDone = () => {
    updateDay(selectedISO, (prev) => {
      const nextC = { ...(prev.c || {}) };
      MINIMUM_DAY_IDS.forEach((id) => {
        nextC[id] = true;
      });
      return { ...prev, c: nextC };
    });
  };

  const handleScheduleChange = (idx, field, val) => {
    setData((old) => {
      const next = [...(old.schedule || DEFAULT_SCHEDULE)];
      next[idx] = { ...next[idx], [field]: val };
      return { ...old, schedule: next };
    });
  };

  const addScheduleBlock = () => {
    setData((old) => {
      const next = [
        ...(old.schedule || DEFAULT_SCHEDULE),
        {
          id: `sch-${uid()}`,
          time: "16:30",
          label: "New focused block / revision session",
        },
      ].sort((a, b) => (a.time || "").localeCompare(b.time || ""));
      return { ...old, schedule: next };
    });
  };

  const removeScheduleBlock = (idx) => {
    setData((old) => {
      const next = [...(old.schedule || DEFAULT_SCHEDULE)].filter(
        (_, i) => i !== idx
      );
      return { ...old, schedule: next };
    });
  };

  const resetDefaultSchedule = () => {
    setData((old) => ({
      ...old,
      schedule: DEFAULT_SCHEDULE.map((x) => ({ ...x })),
    }));
  };

  const toggleScheduleDoneForDay = (blockId) => {
    updateDay(selectedISO, (prev) => {
      const curMap = prev.scheduleDone || {};
      return {
        ...prev,
        scheduleDone: {
          ...curMap,
          [blockId]: !curMap[blockId],
        },
      };
    });
  };

  /* Habit Stacks (stored in data.habitStacks) */
  const habitStacks = data.habitStacks || [];

  const addHabitStack = (anchorText, actionText, categoryText = "Routine") => {
    const a = (anchorText || "").trim();
    const b = (actionText || "").trim();
    if (!a || !b) return;
    const newStack = {
      id: `stk-${uid()}`,
      anchor: a,
      action: b,
      category: categoryText,
    };
    setData((old) => ({
      ...old,
      habitStacks: [newStack, ...(old.habitStacks || [])],
    }));
    setStackInput({ anchor: "", action: "", category: "Morning Anchor" });
  };

  const removeHabitStack = (stackId) => {
    setData((old) => ({
      ...old,
      habitStacks: (old.habitStacks || []).filter((s) => s.id !== stackId),
    }));
  };

  /* Helper to calculate custom habit streak & 7-day history */
  const getHabitStats = (habitId) => {
    const checkins = (data.checkins || []).filter(
      (c) => c.habitId === habitId && c.done
    );
    const doneDatesSet = new Set(checkins.map((c) => c.date));
    const hObj = (data.habits || []).find((h) => h.id === habitId);
    if (hObj?.doneDate) doneDatesSet.add(hObj.doneDate);

    let streak = 0;
    let cursor = doneDatesSet.has(today) ? today : addDaysISO(today, -1);
    while (doneDatesSet.has(cursor)) {
      streak += 1;
      cursor = addDaysISO(cursor, -1);
    }

    const missedYesterday =
      !doneDatesSet.has(addDaysISO(today, -1)) && !doneDatesSet.has(today);

    const last7 = last7Dates.map((d) => ({
      iso: d.iso,
      shortDay: d.shortDay,
      done: doneDatesSet.has(d.iso),
    }));

    const rate7d = percent(
      last7.filter((x) => x.done).length,
      last7.length
    );

    return { streak, missedYesterday, last7, rate7d };
  };

  const activeLoopsList = CONTROL_LOOPS.filter((loop) =>
    (data.settings?.activeLoops || []).includes(loop.id)
  );

  const visibleScoredChecks = onlyMinDay
    ? SCORED_CHECKS.filter((item) => MINIMUM_DAY_IDS.includes(item.id))
    : SCORED_CHECKS;

  const minDayDoneCount = MINIMUM_DAY_IDS.filter(
    (id) => dayObj.c && dayObj.c[id]
  ).length;

  const targetSteps = p.targets?.steps || 8000;
  const targetWater = p.targets?.waterL || 3;
  const targetScreen = p.targets?.screenH || 3;
  const recommendedWaterByWeight = +(
    Math.max(45, safeNum(p.weightKg, 56)) * 0.04
  ).toFixed(1);

  /* Determine active schedule block index for today */
  const scheduleRows = data.schedule || DEFAULT_SCHEDULE;
  const activeScheduleIdx = useMemo(() => {
    if (selectedISO !== today) return -1;
    let found = -1;
    for (let i = 0; i < scheduleRows.length; i += 1) {
      if ((scheduleRows[i].time || "00:00") <= currentTimeStr) {
        found = i;
      }
    }
    return found;
  }, [scheduleRows, currentTimeStr, selectedISO, today]);

  return (
    <>
      <Heading
        title="Habits & daily tracker"
        description="Track your 100-point Comeback core, custom habits, 7-day consistency matrix, habit stacks, and daily time blocks."
        action={
          <div className="heading-btn-group">
            <Button
              onClick={() => setSelectedISO(addDaysISO(selectedISO, -1))}
              title="Previous Day"
            >
              â† Prev
            </Button>

            <input
              type="date"
              className="date-picker-input"
              value={selectedISO}
              max={today}
              onChange={(e) => setSelectedISO(e.target.value || today)}
            />

            {selectedISO !== today && (
              <Button onClick={() => setSelectedISO(today)}>
                Jump to Today
              </Button>
            )}

            <Button kind="primary" onClick={onAdd}>
              ï¼‹ Create habit
            </Button>
          </div>
        }
      />

      <div className="mini-stat-row">
        <div>
          <span className="mini-stat-number">{breakdown.total}/100</span>
          <span>Score on {fmtDate(selectedISO)}</span>
        </div>

        <div>
          <span className="mini-stat-number">{avg7DayScore} pts</span>
          <span>7-Day avg score</span>
        </div>

        <div>
          <span className="mini-stat-number">{builds}</span>
          <span>Building habits</span>
        </div>

        <div>
          <span className="mini-stat-number">{quits}</span>
          <span>Letting go</span>
        </div>

        <div>
          <span className="mini-stat-number">
            {(data.checkins || []).filter((c) => c.done).length}
          </span>
          <span>Total check-ins</span>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="tabs large-tabs">
        <button
          type="button"
          className={viewMode === "tracker" ? "selected" : ""}
          onClick={() => setViewMode("tracker")}
        >
          Comeback 90 Daily Log <span>{breakdown.total} pts</span>
        </button>

        <button
          type="button"
          className={viewMode === "custom" ? "selected" : ""}
          onClick={() => setViewMode("custom")}
        >
          Custom Habits &amp; Stacks <span>{(data.habits || []).length}</span>
        </button>

        <button
          type="button"
          className={viewMode === "matrix" ? "selected" : ""}
          onClick={() => setViewMode("matrix")}
        >
          7-Day Consistency Matrix <span>{avg7DayScore}%</span>
        </button>

        <button
          type="button"
          className={viewMode === "schedule" ? "selected" : ""}
          onClick={() => setViewMode("schedule")}
        >
          Daily Time-Block Schedule <span>{scheduleRows.length}</span>
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* ================= TRACKER TAB ================= */}

      {viewMode === "tracker" && (
        <div className="tracker-layout stack-col">
          {/* 4-PILLAR SCORE BREAKDOWN & MINIMUM DAY ANCHOR */}
          <Card>
            <div className="section-head">
              <div>
                <div className="eyebrow">
                  100-POINT COMEBACK SCORE ENGINE ({fmtDate(selectedISO)})
                </div>
                <h2>Live 4-Pillar Score Breakdown: {breakdown.total}/100 pts</h2>
                <p>
                  Low energy ya exam load wale din sirf 3 Minimum Day basics poore
                  karo taaki momentum kabhi zero na ho.
                </p>
              </div>
              <div className="heading-btn-group">
                <Button
                  kind={onlyMinDay ? "primary" : "secondary"}
                  onClick={() => setOnlyMinDay((v) => !v)}
                >
                  {onlyMinDay
                    ? "Showing 3 Minimum Basics"
                    : `Minimum Day Filter (${minDayDoneCount}/3)`}
                </Button>
                {minDayDoneCount < 3 && (
                  <Button onClick={markMinimumDayDone}>
                    âœ“ Lock 3 Minimum Basics (+19 pts)
                  </Button>
                )}
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="stats-grid jsx-29b7980a" >
              <Stat
                icon="ðŸ›¡"
                label="1. Clean Streak Core"
                value={`${breakdown.coreClean}/20 pts`}
                detail={
                  dayObj.c?.noPorn && !dayObj.slip
                    ? "Clean day locked in"
                    : "Tap 'No porn today' below"
                }
                color="sage"
              />
              <Stat
                icon="ðŸ’ª"
                label="2. Workout, Steps & Fuel"
                value={`${breakdown.fitnessBody}/32 pts`}
                detail="Workout (12) Â· Steps (8) Â· Protein (8) Â· Water (4)"
                color="peach"
              />
              <Stat
                icon="â˜¾"
                label="3. Sleep & Screen Anchor"
                value={`${breakdown.sleepScreen}/35 pts`}
                detail="Wake (8) Â· Sun (8) Â· Screen (8) Â· Bed (6) Â· Phone (5)"
                color="blue"
              />
              <Stat
                icon="âœ¦"
                label="4. Mind, Skincare & Social"
                value={`${breakdown.mindCare}/13 pts`}
                detail="Skincare (5) Â· Brave Social (4) Â· Journal (4)"
                color="lavender"
              />
            </div>

            <div className="habit-list">
              {visibleScoredChecks.map((item) => {
                const checked = Boolean(dayObj.c && dayObj.c[item.id]);
                const isMin = MINIMUM_DAY_IDS.includes(item.id);
                return (
                  <div
                    className={`habit-row ${checked ? "checked" : ""}`}
                    key={item.id}
                  >
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      className="check-circle"
                      aria-label={`${checked ? "Uncheck" : "Complete"} ${
                        item.title
                      }`}
                      aria-pressed={checked}
                      onClick={() => toggleCoreCheck(item.id)}
                    >
                      {checked ? "âœ“" : ""}
                    </button>

                    <div className="habit-info">
                      <strong>
                        {dm(item.title)}{" "}
                        {isMin && (
                          <span
                            className="status-pill peach-pill jsx-8cdcd88b"
                            
                          >
                            Minimum Day Basic
                          </span>
                        )}
                      </strong>
                      <small>{dm(item.sub)}</small>
                    </div>

                    <span
                      className={`status-pill ${checked ? "complete" : ""}`}

                      +{item.weight} pts
                    </span>
                  </div>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </Card>

          {/* Daily metrics with Quick-Increment Buttons */}
          <Card>
            <div className="section-head">
              <div>
                <div className="eyebrow">DAILY NUMBERS &amp; BIOMETRICS</div>
                <h2>
                  Steps, hydration, screen &amp; sleep ({fmtDate(selectedISO)})

                  Partial progress automatically calculates towards your
                  100-point score. Use the quick-tap buttons for instant logging.
                </p>
              </div>
              <span className="status-pill complete">
                Weight-based water guide: ~{recommendedWaterByWeight}L/day
              </span>
            </div>

            <div className="form-grid four-col-grid tracker-metrics">
              <div>
                <Field label={`Steps (Target: ${targetSteps})`}>
                  <input
                    type="number"
                    min="0"
                    step="500"
                    value={dayObj.steps}
                    onChange={(e) => {
                      const v = Math.max(0, safeNum(e.target.value, 0));
                      updateDay(selectedISO, {
                        steps: v,
                        c: {
                          ...(dayObj.c || {}),
                          steps: v >= targetSteps,
                        },
                      });
                    }}
                  />
                </Field>
                <div className="jsx-0d69e4b1">
                  <Progress value={percent(dayObj.steps || 0, targetSteps)} />
                  <div className="category-pills jsx-0d69e4b1" >
                    {[1000, 2500, targetSteps].map((val) => (
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        key={val}
                        onClick={() => {
                          const nextSteps =
                            val === targetSteps
                              ? targetSteps
                              : Math.max(0, safeNum(dayObj.steps, 0) + val);
                          updateDay(selectedISO, {
                            steps: nextSteps,
                            c: {
                              ...(dayObj.c || {}),
                              steps: nextSteps >= targetSteps,
                            },
                          });
                        }}
                      >
                        {val === targetSteps ? `Hit ${targetSteps}` : `+${val}`}
                      </button>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div>
                <Field label={`Water in Litres (Target: ${targetWater}L)`}>
                  <input
                    type="number"
                    min="0"
                    step="0.25"
                    value={dayObj.waterL}
                    onChange={(e) => {
                      const v = Math.max(0, safeNum(e.target.value, 0));
                      updateDay(selectedISO, {
                        waterL: v,
                        c: {
                          ...(dayObj.c || {}),
                          water: v >= targetWater,
                        },
                      });
                    }}
                  />
                </Field>
                <div className="jsx-0d69e4b1">
                  <Progress value={percent(dayObj.waterL || 0, targetWater)} />
                  <div className="category-pills jsx-0d69e4b1" >
                    {[
                      [0.25, "+250ml"],
                      [0.5, "+500ml"],
                      [targetWater, `Hit ${targetWater}L`],
                    ].map(([amt, lbl]) => (
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        key={lbl}
                        onClick={() => {
                          const nextW =
                            amt === targetWater
                              ? targetWater
                              : +(safeNum(dayObj.waterL, 0) + Number(amt)).toFixed(
                                  2
                                );
                          updateDay(selectedISO, {
                            waterL: nextW,
                            c: {
                              ...(dayObj.c || {}),
                              water: nextW >= targetWater,
                            },
                          });
                        }}
                      >
                        {lbl}
                      </button>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div>
                <Field
                  label={`Recreational Screen (Limit â‰¤ ${targetScreen}h)`}
                >
                  <input
                    type="number"
                    min="0"
                    step="0.5"
                    value={dayObj.screenH}
                    onChange={(e) => {
                      const v = Math.max(0, safeNum(e.target.value, 0));
                      updateDay(selectedISO, {
                        screenH: v,
                        c: {
                          ...(dayObj.c || {}),
                          screenUnderLimit: v > 0 && v <= targetScreen,
                        },
                      });
                    }}
                  />
                </Field>
                <div className="jsx-0d69e4b1">
                  <Progress
                    value={
                      dayObj.screenH > 0
                        ? Math.min(100, percent(dayObj.screenH, targetScreen))
                        : 0
                    }
                  />
                  <div className="category-pills jsx-0d69e4b1" >
                    {[1, 2, targetScreen].map((hr) => (
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        key={hr}
                        onClick={() =>
                          updateDay(selectedISO, {
                            screenH: hr,
                            c: {
                              ...(dayObj.c || {}),
                              screenUnderLimit: hr <= targetScreen,
                            },
                          })
                        }
                      >
                        {hr}h logged
                      </button>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div>
                <Field label="Last Night's Sleep (Hours)">
                  <input
                    type="number"
                    min="0"
                    max="16"
                    step="0.5"
                    value={dayObj.sleepH}
                    onChange={(e) =>
                      updateDay(selectedISO, {
                        sleepH: Math.max(0, safeNum(e.target.value, 0)),
                      })
                    }
                  />
                </Field>
                <div className="jsx-0d69e4b1">
                  <Progress value={percent(dayObj.sleepH || 0, 8)} />
                  <div className="category-pills jsx-0d69e4b1" >
                    {[6.5, 7.5, 8].map((sh) => (
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        key={sh}
                        onClick={() =>
                          updateDay(selectedISO, {
                            sleepH: sh,
                            c: {
                              ...(dayObj.c || {}),
                              wakeOnTime: sh >= 7 || Boolean(dayObj.c?.wakeOnTime),
                            },
                          })
                        }
                      >
                        {sh}h
                      </button>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </Card>

          {/* Two main columns */}
          <div className="tracker-sections">
            {/* LEFT COLUMN */}
            <div className="tracker-life-column">
              <Card>
                <div className="section-head">
                  <div>
                    <div className="eyebrow">LIFE BASICS &amp; FACE POSTURE</div>
                    <h2>Daily grounding &amp; jaw care</h2>
                  </div>
                  <span className="status-pill">
                    {
                      [...LIFE_BASICS, ...FACE_HABITS].filter((item) => {
                        const isFace = FACE_HABITS.some((f) => f.id === item.id);
                        const grp = isFace ? "face" : "life";
                        return Boolean(dayObj[grp] && dayObj[grp][item.id]);
                      }).length

                    /{LIFE_BASICS.length + FACE_HABITS.length} done
                  </span>
                </div>

                <div className="habit-list">
                  {[...LIFE_BASICS, ...FACE_HABITS].map((item) => {
                    const isFace = FACE_HABITS.some((f) => f.id === item.id);
                    const group = isFace ? "face" : "life";

                      dayObj[group] && dayObj[group][item.id]

                        <button

                          aria-label={`${
                            checked ? "Uncheck" : "Complete"
                          } ${item.title}`}

                        >
                          {checked ? "âœ“" : ""}

                        }

                      onKeyDown={(e) => {
                        if (e.key === "Enter" && newWin.trim()) {

                      ï¼‹ Add win

                        : "Not checked in today"}

                          After: {st.anchor} â†’ <em>{st.action}</em>

                        type="button"

                        Remove
                      </button>
                    </div>

              </div>
            )}

                      <tr key={chk.id}>

                            (+{chk.weight}p)
                          </small>

                            d.dayObj.c && d.dayObj.c[chk.id]

                                type="button"
                                className={`jsx-2d92f77e ${`check-circle ${
                                  isChecked ? "checked" : ""
                                }`}`}
                                
                                onClick={() => {
                                  updateDay(d.iso, (prev) => ({
                                    ...prev,
                                    c: {
                                      ...(prev.c || {}),
                                      [chk.id]: !isChecked,
                                    },
                                  }));
                                }}
                                title={`${chk.title} on ${d.iso}`}
                              >
                                {isChecked ? "âœ“" : ""}
                              </button>
                            </td>
                          );
                        })}
                        <td className="jsx-65f9d5a3">
                          <strong>{percent(doneCount, 7)}%</strong>
                        </td>
                      </tr>
                    );
                  })}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
          </Card>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* ================= SCHEDULE TAB ================= */}

      {viewMode === "schedule" && (
        <Card>
          <div className="section-head">
            <div>
              <div className="eyebrow">STRUCTURED DAY TEMPLATE</div>
              <h2>
                Editable daily schedule (includes Apna Kaam deep-focus blocks)
              </h2>

              </p>
            </div>
            <div className="heading-btn-group">
              <Button onClick={addScheduleBlock}>ï¼‹ Add time block</Button>
              <Button onClick={resetDefaultSchedule}>Reset default</Button>
            </div>
          </div>

            {scheduleRows.map((row, idx) => {
              const blockId = row.id || `idx-${idx}`;
              const isDone = Boolean(
                dayObj.scheduleDone && dayObj.scheduleDone[blockId]
              );
              const isCurrentNow = idx === activeScheduleIdx;

              return (
                <div
                  key={blockId}
                  className={`schedule-edit-row ${isDone ? "checked" : ""} ${isCurrentNow ? "current-time-block" : ""}`}
                >
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    className="check-circle"
                    aria-label={`Mark ${row.label} complete`}
                    onClick={() => toggleScheduleDoneForDay(blockId)}
                  >
                    {isDone ? "âœ“" : ""}
                  </button>

                  <input
                    type="time"
                    className="clean-input time-w"
                    aria-label={`Schedule time for ${row.label}`}
                    value={row.time}
                    onChange={(e) =>
                      handleScheduleChange(idx, "time", e.target.value)
                    }
                  />

                  <input
                    type="text"
                    className="clean-input flex-1"
                    aria-label="Schedule activity"
                    value={row.label}
                    onChange={(e) =>
                      handleScheduleChange(idx, "label", e.target.value)
                    }
                  />

                  {isCurrentNow && (
                    <span className="status-pill complete">LIVE NOW</span>
                  )}

                  <button
                    type="button"
                    className="danger-text"
                    onClick={() => removeScheduleBlock(idx)}
                    title="Remove block"
                  >
                    Ã—
                  </button>
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        </Card>
      )}
    </>
  );
}

const dateFromOffset = (today, offset) => {
  const date = new Date(`${today}T12:00:00`);
  date.setDate(date.getDate() + offset);
  return `${date.getFullYear()}-${String(date.getMonth() + 1).padStart(
    2,
    "0"
  )}-${String(date.getDate()).padStart(2, "0")}`;
};

function getDailyXP(game, date) {
  const checkInXP = Array.isArray(game.dailyWinRewards?.[date])
    ? game.dailyWinRewards[date].length * 10
    : 0;
  const mainQuest = QUESTS.find((quest) => quest.id === game.completed?.[date]);
  const sideQuestXP = Array.isArray(game.sideCompleted?.[date])
    ? game.sideCompleted[date].reduce((total, id) => {
        const quest = QUESTS.find((item) => item.id === id);
        return total + (quest?.xp || 0);
      }, 0)
    : 0;
  return checkInXP + (mainQuest?.xp || 0) + sideQuestXP;
}

function HunterRankPage({ game, setGame, today, notify }) {
  const xp = Math.max(0, Number(game.xp) || 0);
  const progress = getLevelProgress(xp);
  const currentClass = getClassForLevel(progress.level);
  const nextClass = getNextClassForLevel(progress.level);
  const CurrentClassIcon = CLASS_ICONS[currentClass.rank] || Sprout;

  const [selectedRankKey, setSelectedRankKey] = useState(null);

  const rewardPlans = game.levelRewards || {};
  const nextRewardLevel =
    REWARD_LEVELS.find((level) => !rewardPlans[level]?.claimedAt) ||
    progress.level + 1;
  const nextRewardXP = xpForLevel(nextRewardLevel);
  const nextReward = rewardPlans[nextRewardLevel] || {};
  const rewardPlan =
    nextReward.plan ?? REWARD_IDEAS[nextRewardLevel] ?? "";
  const rewardUnlocked = xp >= nextRewardXP;

  const dates = Array.from({ length: 14 }, (_, index) =>
    dateFromOffset(today, index - 13)
  );
  const dailyXP = dates.map((date) => getDailyXP(game, date));
  const hasActivity = dailyXP.some((value) => value > 0);
  const maxDailyXP = Math.max(1, ...dailyXP);
  const last7XP = dailyXP.slice(-7).reduce((total, value) => total + value, 0);
  const activeDays = dailyXP.slice(-7).filter((value) => value > 0).length;
  const completedDays = Object.values(game.days || {}).filter(Boolean).length;

  const currentArchetype =
    RANK_ARCHETYPES[currentClass.rank] || RANK_ARCHETYPES.E;
  const inspectedRankKey = selectedRankKey || currentClass.rank;
  const inspectedTier =
    CLASS_TIERS.find((tier) => tier.rank === inspectedRankKey) || currentClass;
  const inspectedArchetype =
    RANK_ARCHETYPES[inspectedRankKey] || RANK_ARCHETYPES.E;
  const isInspectedUnlocked = progress.level >= inspectedTier.minLevel;

  const saveRewardPlan = (level, value) => {
    setGame((previous) => ({
      ...previous,
      levelRewards: {
        ...(previous.levelRewards || {}),
        [level]: {
          ...(previous.levelRewards?.[level] || {}),
          plan: value,
        },
      },
    }));
  };

  const claimReward = () => {
    if (!rewardUnlocked || !rewardPlan.trim() || nextReward.claimedAt) {
      return;
    }
    setGame((previous) => ({
      ...previous,
      levelRewards: {
        ...(previous.levelRewards || {}),
        [nextRewardLevel]: {
          ...(previous.levelRewards?.[nextRewardLevel] || {}),
          plan: rewardPlan.trim(),
          claimedAt: today,
        },
      },
    }));
    notify(`Reward enjoyed: ${rewardPlan.trim()}`);
  };

  return (
    <div className="hunter-rank-page">
      <Heading
        title="Your Hunter journey"
        description="Build your rank one real-life win at a time. Every step countsâ€”even on quiet days."
      />

      {/* HERO BANNER PANEL */}
      <section className="hunter-rank-banner" aria-labelledby="hunter-rank-title">
        <div className="hunter-rank-copy">
          <div className="hr-banner-head">
            <span className="hunter-class-label tint-blue">
              <CurrentClassIcon size={15} aria-hidden="true" />
              CURRENT RANK
            </span>
            <span className="hr-mono-tag">{currentArchetype.label}</span>
          </div>

          <h2 id="hunter-rank-title">{currentClass.title} Hunter</h2>

          <div className="hunter-rank-meta" aria-label="Current class and level">
            <span className="hr-pill tint-blue">
              {currentClass.rank}-RANK
            </span>
            <span className="hr-pill">LEVEL {progress.level}</span>
          </div>

          <blockquote className="hr-quote-box">
            <p>&ldquo;{currentArchetype.mantra}&rdquo;</p>
            <footer>{currentArchetype.focus}</footer>
          </blockquote>

          {nextClass ? (
            <div className="hunter-next-rank">
              <Award size={17} aria-hidden="true" />
              <span>
                Next rank Â·{" "}
                <strong>
                  {nextClass.rank}-Rank {nextClass.title}
                </strong>{" "}
              </span>
              <small>LEVEL {nextClass.minLevel}</small>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="hunter-next-rank">
              <Sparkles size={17} aria-hidden="true" />
              <span>
                <strong>Highest rank reached</strong> Â· Your journey continues

            </div>
          )}

                {progress.levelProgressXP.toLocaleString()} /{" "}
                {progress.levelRequirementXP.toLocaleString()} XP

            {dates.map((date, index) => {

              const height = value ? Math.max(10, (value / maxDailyXP) * 100) : 4;

      {/* HUNTER RANK LADDER */}
      <Card className="hunter-class-card">
        <div className="section-head">
          <div>
            <h2>Hunter rank ladder</h2>
            <p>
              Every rank is earned over time. Click any rank card to preview its
              focus and description.
            </p>
          </div>
          <span className="hunter-ladder-count">
            {CLASS_TIERS.filter((tier) => progress.level >= tier.minLevel).length}
            <span> / {CLASS_TIERS.length} RANKS</span>
          </span>
        </div>

        {/* SELECTED RANK INSPECTOR ROW */}
        <div className="hr-inspector-row">
          <div className="hr-inspector-content">
            <div className="hr-inspector-tags">
              <span className="hr-mono-tag">{inspectedTier.rank}-RANK</span>
              <span
                className={`hr-chip ${
                  isInspectedUnlocked ? "tint-green" : "tint-lavender"
                }`}
              >
                {isInspectedUnlocked
                  ? "UNLOCKED"
                  : `UNLOCKS AT LEVEL ${inspectedTier.minLevel}`}
              </span>
            </div>
            <h3>
              {inspectedTier.title} Hunter
            </h3>
            <p>{inspectedArchetype.focus}</p>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="hunter-class-ladder">
          {CLASS_TIERS.map((tier) => {
            const unlocked = progress.level >= tier.minLevel;
            const active = tier.rank === currentClass.rank;
            const inspected = tier.rank === inspectedRankKey;

            return (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setSelectedRankKey(tier.rank)}
                className={[
                  "hunter-class-step",
                  unlocked ? "unlocked" : "locked",
                  active ? "active" : "",
                  inspected ? "inspected" : "",
                ]
                  .filter(Boolean)
                  .join(" ")}
                key={tier.rank}
                aria-label={`Preview ${tier.rank}-Rank ${tier.title}, ${
                  unlocked
                    ? active
                      ? "current rank"
                      : "unlocked"
                    : `unlocks at level ${tier.minLevel}`
                }`}
                aria-pressed={inspected}
                aria-current={active ? "step" : undefined}
              >
                <div className="hunter-class-info">
                  <div className="hr-step-title-line">
                    <strong>{tier.rank}-Rank</strong>
                    <small>LVL {tier.minLevel}</small>
                  </div>
                  <span className="hr-step-class-title">{tier.title}</span>
                  <small className={`hr-step-archetype ${active ? "is-current" : ""}`}>
                    {active ? "CURRENT RANK" : unlocked ? "UNLOCKED" : "LOCKED"}
                  </small>
                </div>
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </Card>

      {/* MILESTONE REWARD CARD */}
      <Card className="hunter-reward-card">
        <div className="section-head">
          <div>
            <h2>
              {rewardUnlocked
                ? `Level ${nextRewardLevel} reached`
                : `Plan a reward for Level ${nextRewardLevel}`}
            </h2>
            <p>
              Pick something enjoyable and comfortable for your budget. A
              reward is optional; your progress already counts.
            </p>
          </div>
          <span className={`status-pill ${rewardUnlocked ? "complete" : ""}`}>
            {rewardUnlocked
              ? nextReward.claimedAt
                ? "ENJOYED"
                : "UNLOCKED"
              : `${Math.max(0, nextRewardXP - xp).toLocaleString()} XP TO GO`}
          </span>
        </div>

        <label className="hunter-reward-field">
          <span>Your reward idea</span>
          <input
            className="clean-input"
            value={rewardPlan}
            onChange={(event) =>
              saveRewardPlan(nextRewardLevel, event.target.value)
            }
            maxLength={120}
            placeholder="Choose a treat that feels right for you"
          />
        </label>

        {rewardUnlocked ? (
          <Button
            kind="primary"
            onClick={claimReward}
            disabled={Boolean(nextReward.claimedAt) || !rewardPlan.trim()}
          >
            {nextReward.claimedAt
              ? `âœ“ Enjoyed on ${nextReward.claimedAt}`
              : "Mark my reward as enjoyed"}
          </Button>
        ) : (
          <small className="hunter-reward-hint">
            Suggested: {REWARD_IDEAS[nextRewardLevel] || REWARD_IDEAS[25]}
          </small>
        )}
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
}

export { HunterRankPage };
export default HunterRankPage;
