function ModernWorkoutBarLineChart({ data = [], height = 210 }) {
  const [hoverIdx, setHoverIdx] = useState(null);
  const w = 640;
  const h = height;
  const padL = 38;
  const padR = 20;
  const padT = 24;
  const padB = 30;
  const plotW = w - padL - padR;
  const plotH = h - padT - padB;

  const maxReps = useMemo(() => {
    const vals = data.map((d) => safeNum(d.reps, 0));
    return Math.max(160, ...vals, 1);
  }, [data]);

  const points = useMemo(() => {
    const step = plotW / Math.max(1, data.length);
    return data.map((d, i) => {
      const reps = Math.max(0, safeNum(d.reps, 0));
      const sets = Math.max(0, safeNum(d.sets, 0));
      const x = padL + i * step + step / 2;
      const barH = Math.min(plotH, (reps / maxReps) * plotH);
      const y = padT + plotH - barH;
      const targetY =
        padT + plotH - Math.min(1, safeNum(d.target, 120) / maxReps) * plotH;
      return {
        ...d,
        x,
        y,
        barH,
        reps,
        sets,
        targetY,
        barW: Math.min(26, step * 0.58),
      };
    });
  }, [data, plotW, plotH, maxReps]);

  const linePath = useMemo(() => {
    if (!points.length) return "";
    return points
      .map((p, idx) => `${idx === 0 ? "M" : "L"} ${p.x} ${p.y}`)
      .join(" ");
  }, [points]);

  const active = hoverIdx !== null && points[hoverIdx] ? points[hoverIdx] : null;

  return (
    <div className="modern-chart-wrap">
      {active && (
        <div className="modern-chart-tooltip">
          <span>{active.fullLabel}</span>
          <strong>{active.reps} reps</strong>
          <em>Â· {active.sets} sets</em>
        </div>
      )}
      <svg
        viewBox={`0 0 ${w} ${h}`}
        className="modern-svg-chart"
        onMouseLeave={() => setHoverIdx(null)}
      >
        <defs>
          <linearGradient id="workoutBarGrad" x1="0" y1="0" x2="0" y2="1">
            <stop offset="0%" stopColor="var(--sage)" stopOpacity="0.88" />
            <stop offset="100%" stopColor="var(--sage)" stopOpacity="0.25" />
          </linearGradient>
        </defs>

        {[0, 0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1].map((ratio, idx) => {
          const y = padT + plotH * (1 - ratio);
          const val = Math.round(maxReps * ratio);
          return (
            <g key={idx}>
              <line
                x1={padL}
                y1={y}
                x2={w - padR}
                y2={y}
                stroke="var(--line)"
                strokeDasharray={ratio === 0 ? undefined : "4 4"}
              />
              <text
                x={padL - 8}
                y={y + 3}
                textAnchor="end"
                className="chart-axis-text"
              >
                {val}
              </text>
            </g>
          );
        })}

        {points.map((pt, i) => (
          <g
            key={pt.key || i}
            onMouseEnter={() => setHoverIdx(i)}
            className="jsx-74fa97c2"
          >
            <rect
              x={pt.x - pt.barW / 2}
              y={padT}
              width={pt.barW}
              height={plotH}
              rx="6"
              fill="var(--canvas)"
              opacity="0.55"
            />
            <rect
              x={pt.x - pt.barW / 2}
              y={pt.y}
              width={pt.barW}
              height={Math.max(4, pt.barH)}
              rx="6"
              fill={pt.reps > 0 ? "url(#workoutBarGrad)" : "var(--line)"}
            />
            <text
              x={pt.x}
              y={h - 8}
              textAnchor="middle"
              className="chart-axis-text"
            >
              {pt.label}
            </text>
          </g>
        ))}

        {points.length > 1 && (
          <path
            d={linePath}
            fill="none"
            stroke="#c89572"
            strokeWidth="2.4"
            strokeLinecap="round"
          />
        )}

        {points.map((pt, i) => (
          <circle
            key={`dot-${i}`}
            cx={pt.x}
            cy={pt.y}
            r={hoverIdx === i ? 5.5 : 3.5}
            fill="var(--paper)"
            stroke="#c89572"
            strokeWidth="2.2"
            pointerEvents="none"
          />
        ))}
      </svg>
    </div>
  );
}

function LifestyleAndBodyPage({
  data,
  setData,
  updateProfile,
  onAddRoutine,
  onAddMetric,
  onAddStep,
  onLog,
  onEdit,
  onDelete,
  notify,
}) {
  const [tab, setTab] = useState("workouts"); // workouts | calc | diet | frame | routines | metrics
  const [category, setCategory] = useState("all");

  const today = todayISO();
  const currentWeekday = getWeekdayShort(today);
  const [selectedDayKey, setSelectedDayKey] = useState(
    DAILY_HOME_WORKOUTS[currentWeekday] ? currentWeekday : "Mon"
  );
  const [restSeconds, setRestSeconds] = useState(90);
  const [measureChartKey, setMeasureChartKey] = useState("weight"); // weight | shoulder | waist | chest | arm | ratio

  /* 1RM & Overload Calculator State */
  const [liftInput, setLiftInput] = useState({ weight: 20, reps: 10 });

  /* Custom Food Input State */
  const [customFood, setCustomFood] = useState({
    name: "",
    protein: "",
    kcal: "",
  });

  /* Interactive Calcium Check State */
  const [calciumChecks, setCalciumChecks] = useState({});

  const p = data.profile || {};
  const measures = data.measures || [];
  const bodyMetrics = useMemo(() => calcBodyMetrics(p, measures), [p, measures]);

  /* Advanced Body Composition (US Navy Body Fat %, Lean Mass, FFMI, Ideal Shoulder Target) */
  const advancedBodyComp = useMemo(() => {
    const latest =
      measures.length > 0
        ? measures[measures.length - 1]
        : {
            weight: p.weightKg || 56,
            waist: 74,
            neck: 35.5,
            shoulder: 105,
            chest: 87,
            arm: 27.5,
          };

    const h = Math.max(140, safeNum(p.heightCm, 172));
    const wKg = Math.max(35, safeNum(latest.weight, p.weightKg || 56));
    const waist = Math.max(55, safeNum(latest.waist, 74));
    const neck = Math.max(28, safeNum(latest.neck, 35.5));
    const shoulder = Math.max(80, safeNum(latest.shoulder, 105));

    // US Navy Male Body Fat Formula (metric)
    const diff = Math.max(12, waist - neck);
    const rawBf =
      495 /
        (1.0324 -
          0.19077 * Math.log10(diff) +
          0.15456 * Math.log10(h)) -
      450;
    const bodyFatPct = +Math.max(5, Math.min(40, rawBf)).toFixed(1);
    const fatMassKg = +((wKg * bodyFatPct) / 100).toFixed(1);
    const leanMassKg = +(wKg - fatMassKg).toFixed(1);
    const heightM = h / 100;
    const ffmi = +(leanMassKg / (heightM * heightM)).toFixed(1);

    const athleticShoulderTarget = +(waist * 1.45).toFixed(1);
    const goldenShoulderTarget = +(waist * 1.618).toFixed(1);
    const cmToAthletic = +Math.max(0, athleticShoulderTarget - shoulder).toFixed(
      1
    );

    // Daily Macro Split based on target calories
    const targetKcal = Math.round((bodyMetrics.calMin + bodyMetrics.calMax) / 2);
    const proteinG = Math.round(
      (bodyMetrics.proteinMin + bodyMetrics.proteinMax) / 2
    );
    const fatG = Math.round((targetKcal * 0.25) / 9);
    const carbG = Math.max(
      100,
      Math.round((targetKcal - proteinG * 4 - fatG * 9) / 4)
    );

    return {
      bodyFatPct,
      fatMassKg,
      leanMassKg,
      ffmi,
      athleticShoulderTarget,
      goldenShoulderTarget,
      cmToAthletic,
      targetKcal,
      proteinG,
      fatG,
      carbG,
    };
  }, [measures, p.heightCm, p.weightKg, bodyMetrics]);

  const activeDayPlan =
    DAILY_HOME_WORKOUTS[selectedDayKey] || DAILY_HOME_WORKOUTS.Mon;
  const todayDayObj = (data.days && data.days[today]) || {};
  const savedWorkoutLog = todayDayObj.homeWorkoutLog || {
    setsDone: {},
    repsByEx: {},
    loadByEx: {},
    completed: false,
  };

  const getExReps = (ex) =>
    savedWorkoutLog.repsByEx && savedWorkoutLog.repsByEx[ex.id] !== undefined
      ? savedWorkoutLog.repsByEx[ex.id]
      : ex.defaultReps;

  const getExLoad = (ex) =>
    savedWorkoutLog.loadByEx && savedWorkoutLog.loadByEx[ex.id] !== undefined
      ? savedWorkoutLog.loadByEx[ex.id]
      : 0;

  const isSetChecked = (exId, setIdx) =>
    Boolean(
      savedWorkoutLog.setsDone && savedWorkoutLog.setsDone[`${exId}-${setIdx}`]
    );

  const toggleWorkoutSet = (ex, setIdx) => {
    const key = `${ex.id}-${setIdx}`;
    const nextSetsDone = {
      ...(savedWorkoutLog.setsDone || {}),
      [key]: !isSetChecked(ex.id, setIdx),
    };
    const nextRepsByEx = {
      ...(savedWorkoutLog.repsByEx || {}),
      [ex.id]: getExReps(ex),
    };
    const nextLoadByEx = {
      ...(savedWorkoutLog.loadByEx || {}),
      [ex.id]: getExLoad(ex),
    };
    recalcAndSaveTodayWorkout(
      nextSetsDone,
      nextRepsByEx,
      nextLoadByEx,
      savedWorkoutLog.completed
    );
  };

  const updateExerciseReps = (ex, newReps) => {
    const cleanReps = Math.max(1, safeNum(newReps, ex.defaultReps));
    const nextRepsByEx = {
      ...(savedWorkoutLog.repsByEx || {}),
      [ex.id]: cleanReps,
    };
    recalcAndSaveTodayWorkout(
      savedWorkoutLog.setsDone || {},
      nextRepsByEx,
      savedWorkoutLog.loadByEx || {},
      savedWorkoutLog.completed
    );
  };

  const updateExerciseLoad = (ex, newLoad) => {
    const cleanLoad = Math.max(0, safeNum(newLoad, 0));
    const nextLoadByEx = {
      ...(savedWorkoutLog.loadByEx || {}),
      [ex.id]: cleanLoad,
    };
    recalcAndSaveTodayWorkout(
      savedWorkoutLog.setsDone || {},
      savedWorkoutLog.repsByEx || {},
      nextLoadByEx,
      savedWorkoutLog.completed
    );
  };

  const recalcAndSaveTodayWorkout = (
    setsDoneMap,
    repsMap,
    loadMap = {},
    markCompleted = false
  ) => {
    let totalSets = 0;
    let totalReps = 0;
    let totalTonnageKg = 0;
    Object.values(DAILY_HOME_WORKOUTS).forEach((dayPlan) => {
      dayPlan.exercises.forEach((ex) => {
        const r =
          repsMap[ex.id] !== undefined ? repsMap[ex.id] : ex.defaultReps;
        const loadKg = safeNum(loadMap[ex.id], 0);
        for (let s = 1; s <= ex.sets; s += 1) {
          if (setsDoneMap[`${ex.id}-${s}`]) {
            totalSets += 1;
            totalReps += Number(r) || 0;
            totalTonnageKg += (Number(r) || 0) * loadKg;
          }
        }
      });
    });

    setData((old) => {
      const prevDay = (old.days && old.days[today]) || {};
      return {
        ...old,
        days: {
          ...(old.days || {}),
          [today]: {
            ...prevDay,
            c: {
              ...(prevDay.c || {}),
              workout:
                markCompleted ||
                totalSets >= 3 ||
                Boolean(prevDay.c?.workout),
            },
            homeWorkoutLog: {
              dayKey: selectedDayKey,
              setsDone: setsDoneMap,
              repsByEx: repsMap,
              loadByEx: loadMap,
              totalSets,
              totalReps,
              totalTonnageKg,
              completed: markCompleted || totalSets >= 6,
              updatedAt: Date.now(),
            },
          },
        },
      };
    });
  };

  const completeAllSetsForToday = () => {
    const nextSetsDone = { ...(savedWorkoutLog.setsDone || {}) };
    const nextRepsByEx = { ...(savedWorkoutLog.repsByEx || {}) };
    const nextLoadByEx = { ...(savedWorkoutLog.loadByEx || {}) };
    activeDayPlan.exercises.forEach((ex) => {
      if (nextRepsByEx[ex.id] === undefined) {
        nextRepsByEx[ex.id] = ex.defaultReps;
      }
      for (let s = 1; s <= ex.sets; s += 1) {
        nextSetsDone[`${ex.id}-${s}`] = true;
      }
    });
    recalcAndSaveTodayWorkout(nextSetsDone, nextRepsByEx, nextLoadByEx, true);
    notify(`${activeDayPlan.day} Home Workout logged! (+12 Comeback pts)`);
  };

  /* 14-Day Workout Graph Data */
  const workoutGraphSeries = useMemo(() => {
    return Array.from({ length: 14 }, (_, idx) => {
      const iso = addDaysISO(today, -(13 - idx));
      const dObj = (data.days && data.days[iso]) || {};
      const wkName = getWeekdayShort(iso);
      const planForDay = DAILY_HOME_WORKOUTS[wkName] || DAILY_HOME_WORKOUTS.Mon;
      const log = dObj.homeWorkoutLog;

      let reps = safeNum(log?.totalReps, 0);
      let sets = safeNum(log?.totalSets, 0);
      if (reps === 0 && dObj.c?.workout) {
        reps = planForDay.targetReps;
        sets = 14;
      }

      return {
        key: iso,
        label: wkName.slice(0, 2),
        fullLabel: `${fmtDate(iso)} (${wkName})`,
        reps,
        sets,
        target: planForDay.targetReps,
      };
    });
  }, [data.days, today]);

  const weeklyWorkoutStats = useMemo(() => {
    const last7 = workoutGraphSeries.slice(-7);
    const totalReps7d = last7.reduce((acc, d) => acc + d.reps, 0);
    const totalSets7d = last7.reduce((acc, d) => acc + d.sets, 0);
    const activeDays7d = last7.filter((d) => d.reps > 0).length;
    const estKcal = Math.round(totalSets7d * 14 + totalReps7d * 0.45);
    return { totalReps7d, totalSets7d, activeDays7d, estKcal };
  }, [workoutGraphSeries]);

  /* Diet Live Macro & Protein Tracker State (Stored per day) */
  const todayDietLog = todayDayObj.dietItems || [];
  const loggedProteinToday = todayDietLog.reduce(
    (s, item) => s + safeNum(item.protein, 0),
    0
  );
  const loggedKcalToday = todayDietLog.reduce(
    (s, item) => s + safeNum(item.kcal, 0),
    0
  );

  const addQuickFoodToday = (food) => {
    setData((old) => {
      const prevDay = (old.days && old.days[today]) || {};
      const nextList = [
        ...(prevDay.dietItems || []),
        { ...food, uid: uid(), time: new Date().toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: "2-digit", minute: "2-digit" }) },
      ];
      const newProt = nextList.reduce((s, x) => s + safeNum(x.protein, 0), 0);
      return {
        ...old,
        days: {
          ...(old.days || {}),
          [today]: {
            ...prevDay,
            dietItems: nextList,
            c: {
              ...(prevDay.c || {}),
              protein:
                newProt >= bodyMetrics.proteinMin ||
                Boolean(prevDay.c?.protein),
            },
          },
        },
      };
    });
    notify(`Added ${food.name} (+${food.protein}g protein)`);
  };

  const handleAddCustomFood = () => {
    const name = customFood.name.trim();
    const prot = Math.max(0, safeNum(customFood.protein, 0));
    const kcal = Math.max(0, safeNum(customFood.kcal, prot * 4));
    if (!name || (prot === 0 && kcal === 0)) return;
    addQuickFoodToday({
      id: `cust-${uid()}`,
      name,
      protein: prot,
      kcal,
    });
    setCustomFood({ name: "", protein: "", kcal: "" });
  };

  const removeFoodItemToday = (itemUid) => {
    setData((old) => {
      const prevDay = (old.days && old.days[today]) || {};
      const nextList = (prevDay.dietItems || []).filter(
        (x) => x.uid !== itemUid
      );
      return {
        ...old,
        days: {
          ...(old.days || {}),
          [today]: { ...prevDay, dietItems: nextList },
        },
      };
    });
  };

  const clearTodayDiet = () => {
    setData((old) => {
      const prevDay = (old.days && old.days[today]) || {};
      return {
        ...old,
        days: {
          ...(old.days || {}),
          [today]: { ...prevDay, dietItems: [] },
        },
      };
    });
  };

  /* Measurements Form & Multi-Metric Chart */
  const [newMeas, setNewMeas] = useState({
    date: today,
    weight: p.weightKg || 56,
    waist: 74,
    chest: 87,
    arm: 27.5,
    neck: 35.5,
    shoulder: 105,
  });

  const handleSaveMeasurement = () => {
    const entry = {
      date: newMeas.date || today,
      weight: safeNum(newMeas.weight, p.weightKg),
      waist: safeNum(newMeas.waist, 74),
      chest: safeNum(newMeas.chest, 87),
      arm: safeNum(newMeas.arm, 27.5),
      neck: safeNum(newMeas.neck, 35.5),
      shoulder: safeNum(newMeas.shoulder, 105),
    };
    setData((old) => ({
      ...old,
      measures: [...(old.measures || []), entry].sort((a, b) =>
        String(a.date).localeCompare(String(b.date))
      ),
      profile: { ...old.profile, weightKg: entry.weight },
    }));
    notify("Body measurements logged");
  };

function parseAvgRupeeRange(str = "") {
  const nums = String(str)
    .replace(/,/g, "")
    .match(/\d+/g);
  if (!nums || !nums.length) return 800;
  if (nums.length === 1) return Number(nums[0]);
  return Math.round((Number(nums[0]) + Number(nums[1])) / 2);
}

  const [subTab, setSubTab] = useState("face"); // face | height | hair | skin | groom

    lengthVsWidth: "balanced",

    foreheadVsJaw: "equal",
  });

    sittingHours: "high", // low | med | high
    aptLevel: "moderate", // none | moderate | high

  });

  const today = todayISO();
  const p = data.profile || {};
  const groom = data.groom || {};
  const auditAnswers = (data.faceAudit && data.faceAudit.answers) || {};

    morningCm: groom.morningHeightCm || +(safeNum(p.heightCm, 172) + 1.5).toFixed(1),
    eveningCm: groom.eveningHeightCm || safeNum(p.heightCm, 172),
  });

    setData((old) => ({
      ...old,
      groom: { ...(old.groom || {}), ...patch },
    }));

    setData((old) => ({
      ...old,
      faceAudit: {
        answers: {
          ...((old.faceAudit && old.faceAudit.answers) || {}),
          [qId]: val,
        },
        updatedAt: todayISO(),
      },
    }));
  };

  const selectedSkinType = SKIN_ROUTINES[groom.skinType]
    ? groom.skinType

    ? groom.faceShape
    : "oval";

      if (ans) {
        answeredCount += 1;
        if (ans === q.options[0].value) {
          healthyCount += 1;
        }
        if (q.dentistOn && ans === q.dentistOn) {
          dentistAlerts.push(q);
        }
      }
    });

    return { healthyCount, answeredCount, dentistAlerts };
  }, [auditAnswers]);

  /* Sync Face Routine with Daily Tracker */
  const isFaceRoutineLoggedToday = Boolean(
    todayDayObj.face && todayDayObj.face.faceRoutineDone
  );

  const toggleFaceStepDone = (stepId) => {
    const next = { ...doneRoutineSteps, [stepId]: !doneRoutineSteps[stepId] };
    setDoneRoutineSteps(next);
    const completedCount = Object.values(next).filter(Boolean).length;
    if (completedCount >= FACE_ROUTINE_STEPS.length) {
      markFullFaceRoutineToday(true);
    }
  };

  const markFullFaceRoutineToday = (val = true) => {
    const nextSteps = {};
    if (val) {
      FACE_ROUTINE_STEPS.forEach((s) => {
        nextSteps[s.id] = true;
      });
    }
    setDoneRoutineSteps(nextSteps);

    setData((old) => {
      const prevDay = (old.days && old.days[today]) || createEmptyDay();
      return {
        ...old,
        days: {
          ...(old.days || {}),
          [today]: {
            ...prevDay,
            face: {
              ...(prevDay.face || {}),
              faceRoutineDone: val,
              lipsTogether: val ? true : prevDay.face?.lipsTogether,
            },
          },
        },
      };
    });
    if (val && typeof notify === "function") {
      notify("5â€“8 min Face & Posture routine logged for today!");
    }
  };

  /* Height Decompression Routine Sync */
  const isHeightRoutineDoneToday = Boolean(
    todayDayObj.life && todayDayObj.life.eyePosture && groom.heightDoneDate === today

    const next = { ...doneHeightSteps, [stepId]: !doneHeightSteps[stepId] };
    setDoneHeightSteps(next);
    const doneCount = Object.values(next).filter(Boolean).length;
    if (doneCount >= 3) {
      markFullHeightRoutineToday(true, next);
    }
  };

  const markFullHeightRoutineToday = (val = true, customMap = null) => {
    const nextMap = customMap || {};
    if (val && !customMap) {
      HEIGHT_DECOMPRESSION_STEPS.forEach((s) => {
        nextMap[s.id] = true;
      });
    }
    setDoneHeightSteps(nextMap);

    setData((old) => {
      const prevDay = (old.days && old.days[today]) || createEmptyDay();
      return {
        ...old,
        groom: {
          ...(old.groom || {}),
          heightDoneDate: val ? today : "",
        },
        days: {
          ...(old.days || {}),
          [today]: {
            ...prevDay,
            life: {
              ...(prevDay.life || {}),
              eyePosture: val ? true : prevDay.life?.eyePosture,
            },
          },

    if (val && typeof notify === "function") {
      notify("Spinal Decompression & Height Protocol logged for today!");

  /* Reclaimable Height Calculation (Age 22 Biomechanics) */
  const heightPotential = useMemo(() => {
    let discGain = 0.6;
    if (heightAudit.sittingHours === "high") discGain = 1.1;
    else if (heightAudit.sittingHours === "med") discGain = 0.8;

    let aptGain = 0.2;
    if (heightAudit.aptLevel === "high") aptGain = 1.0;
    else if (heightAudit.aptLevel === "moderate") aptGain = 0.6;

    const neckGain = heightAudit.neckSlouch === "yes" ? 0.6 : 0.2;

    const totalReclaimCm = +(discGain + aptGain + neckGain).toFixed(1);
    const baseHeight = safeNum(p.heightCm, 172);
    const potentialHeight = +(baseHeight + totalReclaimCm).toFixed(1);

    return {
      discGain,
      aptGain,
      neckGain,
      totalReclaimCm,
      baseHeight,
      potentialHeight,
    };
  }, [heightAudit, p.heightCm]);

  const handleSaveHeightLog = () => {
    const mCm = Math.max(130, Math.min(220, safeNum(heightForm.morningCm, 173.5)));
    const eCm = Math.max(130, Math.min(220, safeNum(heightForm.eveningCm, 172)));
    const entry = {
      id: uid(),
      date: today,
      morningCm: mCm,
      eveningCm: eCm,
      dropCm: +Math.max(0, mCm - eCm).toFixed(1),
    };

    setData((old) => ({
      ...old,
      profile: {
        ...(old.profile || {}),
        heightCm: eCm,
      },
      groom: {
        ...(old.groom || {}),
        morningHeightCm: mCm,
        eveningHeightCm: eCm,
        heightLogs: [entry, ...(old.groom?.heightLogs || []).slice(0, 14)],

      notify(`Height logged: Morning ${mCm} cm Â· Evening ${eCm} cm`);

    if (shapeQuiz.lengthVsWidth === "long") {
      rec = "oblong";
    } else if (shapeQuiz.foreheadVsJaw === "widerForehead") {
      rec = "heart";
    } else if (shapeQuiz.jawShape === "angular") {
      rec = "square";
    } else if (
      shapeQuiz.lengthVsWidth === "wide" &&
      shapeQuiz.jawShape === "soft"

      rec = "round";

    setGroom({ faceShape: rec });
    if (typeof notify === "function") {
      notify(`Matched face shape: ${FACE_SHAPES_GUIDE[rec].label}`);
    }

  const haircutStatus = useMemo(() => {
    if (!groom.lastHaircutDate) return null;
    const daysSince = Math.max(0, dayDiff(groom.lastHaircutDate, today));
    const nextIdealISO = addDaysISO(groom.lastHaircutDate, 28);
    const daysLeft = dayDiff(today, nextIdealISO);
    return { daysSince, nextIdealISO, daysLeft };
  }, [groom.lastHaircutDate, today]);

    const text = `Barber Notes (${guide.label}):\nâ€¢ Haircut: ${guide.haircut}\nâ€¢ Sides: Low/Mid taper fade (#1.5 to #3 guard), keep upper-side structure.\nâ€¢ Beard: ${guide.beard} (Neckline 1 finger above Adam's apple).`;
    try {

function parseTimeMins(timeStr = "07:00") {
  const parts = String(timeStr).split(":");
  const h = safeNum(parts[0], 7);
  const m = safeNum(parts[1], 0);
  return h * 60 + m;
}

function minsToTimeStr(totalMins) {
  const norm = ((totalMins % 1440) + 1440) % 1440;
  const h = Math.floor(norm / 60);
  const m = norm % 60;
  return `${String(h).padStart(2, "0")}:${String(m).padStart(2, "0")}`;
}

function MoreAndSettingsPage({
  data,
  setData,
  updateProfile,
  exportData,
  importData,
  resetAll,
  notify,
}) {
  const [subTab, setSubTab] = useState("sleep"); // sleep | control | health | help
  const [taskName, setTaskName] = useState("Apna Kaam (25m Deep Focus)");
  const [selectedPreset, setSelectedPreset] = useState(POMODORO_PRESETS[0]);
  const [manualMins, setManualMins] = useState(45);
  const [ifThenForm, setIfThenForm] = useState({ trigger: "", action: "" });

  const today = todayISO();
  const p = data.profile || {};
  const activeLoops = data.settings?.activeLoops || [];
  const focusSessions = data.focusSessions || [];
  const labValues = data.labResults || {};

  const toggleLoop = (loopId) => {
    setData((old) => {
      const cur = old.settings?.activeLoops || [];
      const next = cur.includes(loopId)
        ? cur.filter((x) => x !== loopId)
        : [...cur, loopId];
      return {
        ...old,
        settings: { ...(old.settings || {}), activeLoops: next },
      };
    });
  };

  const setAllLoops = (mode) => {
    const next =
      mode === "all"
        ? CONTROL_LOOPS.map((x) => x.id)
        : ["junk", "lateCaff", "doomscroll"];
    setData((old) => ({
      ...old,
      settings: { ...(old.settings || {}), activeLoops: next },
    }));
    notify(
      mode === "all"
        ? "All 13 control loops activated"
        : "Reset to top 3 core loops"
    );
  };

  /* Log completed or manual focus session */
  const logFocusSession = (minsToLog, labelToLog) => {
    const cleanMins = Math.max(5, safeNum(minsToLog, 25));
    const entry = {
      id: uid(),
      date: today,
      task: (labelToLog || taskName || "Deep Work Block").trim(),
      mins: cleanMins,
      ts: Date.now(),
    };
    setData((old) => ({
      ...old,
      focusSessions: [entry, ...(old.focusSessions || [])],
    }));
    notify(`${cleanMins}-min focus block logged!`);
  };

  const deleteFocusSession = (id) => {
    setData((old) => ({
      ...old,
      focusSessions: (old.focusSessions || []).filter((s) => s.id !== id),
    }));
  };

  /* Focus Analytics (Today, 7-Day Chart & All-time) */
  const focusAnalytics = useMemo(() => {
    const todayMins = focusSessions
      .filter((s) => s.date === today)
      .reduce((acc, s) => acc + safeNum(s.mins, 0), 0);

    const series7d = Array.from({ length: 7 }, (_, idx) => {
      const iso = addDaysISO(today, -(6 - idx));
      const dayTotal = focusSessions
        .filter((s) => s.date === iso)
        .reduce((acc, s) => acc + safeNum(s.mins, 0), 0);
      return {
        key: iso,
        label: getWeekdayShort(iso).slice(0, 2),
        fullLabel: fmtDate(iso),
        value: dayTotal,
      };
    });

    const weekMins = series7d.reduce((acc, d) => acc + d.value, 0);
    const totalAllTimeMins = focusSessions.reduce(
      (acc, s) => acc + safeNum(s.mins, 0),
      0
    );

    return {
      todayMins,
      weekMins,
      totalAllTimeHours: +(totalAllTimeMins / 60).toFixed(1),
      series7d,
    };
  }, [focusSessions, today]);

  /* Circadian Sleep & 90-Min Cycle Calculations */
  const sleepMetrics = useMemo(() => {
    const wakeMins = parseTimeMins(p.wakeTime || "07:00");
    const bedMins = parseTimeMins(p.bedtime || "23:00");

    let durationMins = wakeMins - bedMins;
    if (durationMins <= 0) durationMins += 1440;

    const durationHours = +(durationMins / 60).toFixed(1);
    const cycles = +(durationMins / 90).toFixed(1);

    const caffeineCutoff = minsToTimeStr(bedMins - 480); // 8 hrs before bed
    const screenSunset = minsToTimeStr(bedMins - 90); // 90 mins before bed
    const sunlightWindowEnd = minsToTimeStr(wakeMins + 45);

    // Ideal bedtimes for 5 cycles (7.5h) and 6 cycles (9h) + 15m fall-asleep buffer
    const fiveCycleBed = minsToTimeStr(wakeMins - 465);
    const sixCycleBed = minsToTimeStr(wakeMins - 555);

    return {
      durationHours,
      cycles,
      caffeineCutoff,
      screenSunset,
      sunlightWindowEnd,
      fiveCycleBed,
      sixCycleBed,
    };
  }, [p.wakeTime, p.bedtime]);

  /* If-Then Implementation Intentions */
  const handleAddIfThen = () => {
    const t = ifThenForm.trigger.trim();
    const a = ifThenForm.action.trim();
    if (!t || !a) return;
    setData((old) => ({
      ...old,
      ifThen: [{ id: `ift-${uid()}`, trigger: t, action: a }, ...(old.ifThen || [])],
    }));
    setIfThenForm({ trigger: "", action: "" });
    notify("If-Then rule saved");
  };

  const handleDeleteIfThen = (id) => {
    setData((old) => ({
      ...old,
      ifThen: (old.ifThen || []).filter((x) => x.id !== id),
    }));
  };

  /* Lab Biomarker Evaluators */
  const updateLabValue = (markerId, val) => {
    setData((old) => ({
      ...old,
      labResults: {
        ...(old.labResults || {}),
        [markerId]: val,
        updatedAt: today,
      },
    }));
  };

  const getMarkerStatus = (m, rawVal) => {
    if (rawVal === "" || rawVal === undefined || rawVal === null) {
      return { label: "Not logged", cls: "" };
    }
    const v = Number(rawVal);
    if (!Number.isFinite(v) || v <= 0) return { label: "Not logged", cls: "" };
    if (m.highCut && v > m.highCut) {
      return { label: "High Â· Consult Doctor", cls: "peach-pill" };
    }
    if (v < m.lowCut) {
      return { label: "Deficient / Low", cls: "peach-pill" };
    }
    if (v >= m.minOpt && v <= m.maxOpt) {
      return { label: "Optimal Range âœ“", cls: "complete" };
    }
    return { label: "Sub-optimal / Borderline", cls: "" };
  };

  return (
    <>
      <Heading
        title="Sleep, Pomodoro, health & settings"
        description="Circadian sleep anchors, multi-block focus timer with analytics, blood biomarker tracker, crisis support, and data backup."
      />

      <div className="tabs large-tabs">
        {[
          ["sleep", "Sleep & Deep Work Pomodoro"],
          ["control", "Dopamine Control & If-Then"],
          ["health", "Blood Tests & Lab Tracker"],
          ["help", "Helplines & System Vault"],
        ].map(([k, lbl]) => (
          <button
            type="button"
            key={k}
            className={subTab === k ? "selected" : ""}
            onClick={() => setSubTab(k)}
          >
            {lbl}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>

      {/* =====================================================================
          TAB 1: SLEEP & DEEP WORK POMODORO
          ===================================================================== */}
      {subTab === "sleep" && (
        <div className="stack-col">
          <div className="stats-grid">
            <Stat
              icon="â—ˆ"
              label="Today's Deep Focus"
              value={`${focusAnalytics.todayMins} mins`}
              detail={`${(focusAnalytics.todayMins / 60).toFixed(1)} hrs logged today`}
              color="sage"
            />
            <Stat
              icon="â†—"
              label="7-Day Focus Volume"
              value={`${focusAnalytics.weekMins} mins`}
              detail={`All-time: ${focusAnalytics.totalAllTimeHours} hrs`}
              color="blue"
            />
            <Stat
              icon="â˜¾"
              label="Planned Sleep Window"
              value={`${sleepMetrics.durationHours} hrs`}
              detail={`~${sleepMetrics.cycles} REM/Deep cycles (90m)`}
              color="peach"
            />
            <Stat
              icon="â˜•"
              label="Caffeine & Screen Cutoffs"
              value={sleepMetrics.caffeineCutoff}
              detail={`Grayscale ON at ${sleepMetrics.screenSunset}`}
              color="lavender"
            />
          </div>

          <div className="dashboard-grid">
            <Card>
              <div className="eyebrow">DEEP WORK POMODORO</div>
              <h2>Distraction-free focus sprint</h2>

              <div className="category-pills jsx-e5a6d3c3" >
                {POMODORO_PRESETS.map((pr) => (
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    key={pr.id}
                    className={selectedPreset.id === pr.id ? "selected" : ""}
                    onClick={() => {
                      setSelectedPreset(pr);
                      setTaskName(`Apna Kaam (${pr.label})`);
                    }}
                  >
                    {pr.label}
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>

              <Field label="Current Focus Task">
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={taskName}
                  onChange={(e) => setTaskName(e.target.value)}
                />
              </Field>

              <div className="jsx-dffc4db2">
                <CountdownTimer
                  defaultSeconds={selectedPreset.sec}
                  label={taskName}
                  onComplete={() => {
                    logFocusSession(selectedPreset.mins, taskName);
                  }}
                />
              </div>

              {/* Manual offline study logger */}
              <div
                className="inline-add-row jsx-d18d96ac"

                <input

                  max="240"
                  step="5"

                  onChange={(e) => setManualMins(safeNum(e.target.value, 45))}
                />
                <Button

                >
                  ï¼‹ Log {manualMins}m Offline Study Block

              </div>
            </Card>

            <Card>

              <h2>Sleep window &amp; 90-min cycle planner</h2>
              <div className="form-grid jsx-4bb90035" >
                <Field label="Fixed Wake Time">
                  <input

                    onChange={(e) =>
                      updateProfile({ wakeTime: e.target.value })
                    }
                  />
                </Field>
                <Field label="Planned Bedtime">
                  <input
                    type="time"

                  />
                </Field>
              </div>

              <div

                <button

                    updateProfile({ bedtime: sleepMetrics.fiveCycleBed })
                  }
                >
                  Set 5 Cycles (7.5h): {sleepMetrics.fiveCycleBed}
                </button>
                <button

                    updateProfile({ bedtime: sleepMetrics.sixCycleBed })

                  Set 6 Cycles (9h): {sleepMetrics.sixCycleBed}
                </button>
              </div>

              <p className="jsx-e5a6d3c3">
                â€¢ <strong>Morning Sunlight:</strong> Step outside for 10â€“20 mins
                before <strong>{sleepMetrics.sunlightWindowEnd}</strong>.
                <br />â€¢ <strong>Caffeine Cutoff:</strong> Stop tea, coffee, and
                energy drinks by <strong>{sleepMetrics.caffeineCutoff}</strong>{" "}
                (8 hrs before sleep).
                <br />â€¢ <strong>Digital Sunset:</strong> Turn on Grayscale &amp;
                keep phone outside bedroom by{" "}
                <strong>{sleepMetrics.screenSunset}</strong>.

                <span className="status-pill complete">
                  {focusAnalytics.weekMins}m this week
                </span>
              </div>
              <ModernAreaChart
                data={focusAnalytics.series7d}
                maxVal={Math.max(
                  120,
                  ...focusAnalytics.series7d.map((d) => d.value)

                unit="m"
                height={180}
              />
            </Card>

                  {focusSessions.length} sessions
                </span>
              </div>
              {focusSessions.length === 0 ? (
                <Empty
                  icon="â—ˆ"
                  title="No focus sessions logged yet"
                  description="Complete a Pomodoro sprint or log an offline study block above."
                />
              ) : (
                <div className="habit-list">

                    <div className="habit-row checked" key={s.id}>
                      <div className="habit-info">

                        <small>{fmtDate(s.date)}</small>
                      </div>

              {CONTROL_LOOPS.map((item) => {

                    Delete

      )}

      {subTab === "health" && (

                  <input

                  />
                </Field>
                <Field label="Screen Limit (Hours)">
                  <input
                    type="number"

                    value={p.targets?.screenH || 3}
                    onChange={(e) =>
                      updateProfile({
                        targets: {

                        },
                      })
                    }
                  />
                </Field>
              </div>
            </Card>

            <Card>
              <div className="eyebrow">CLOUD DATA VAULT</div>
              <h2>Backup, restore or reset your data</h2>
              <p className="jsx-0d69e4b1">
                Your habits, logs, journals, Quest progress, and settings are

              </p>

              <div className="record-actions jsx-4c0544d5" >

===== FILE: C:\Users\tejas\OneDrive\Desktop\LastChance\Frontend\src\pages\QuestPage.jsx =====

import { useRef } from "react";
import {
  addDaysISO,
  getDayIndex,
  getWeekdayShort,
  QUESTS,
  Button,
  Card,
  Heading,
  Progress,
  Stat,
} from "../shared";
import { getClassForLevel, getLevelProgress } from "../progression.js";

function QuestPage({ data, game, setGame, today, notify }) {
  const claimLockRef = useRef(new Set());

  const startDate = (data.profile && data.profile.startDate) || today;
  const dayNumber = getDayIndex(startDate, today);
  const quest = QUESTS[(dayNumber - 1) % QUESTS.length];
  const completedToday = Boolean(game.completed && game.completed[today]);
  const completedSideQuests = Array.isArray(game.sideCompleted?.[today])
    ? game.sideCompleted[today]
    : [];
  const xp = Number(game.xp) || 0;
  const levelProgress = getLevelProgress(xp);
  const currentClass = getClassForLevel(levelProgress.level);
  const completedDays = Object.keys(game.days || {}).filter(
    (d) => game.days[d]
  ).length;

  const recentDays = Array.from({ length: 7 }, (_, index) => {
    const iso = addDaysISO(today, -(6 - index));
    return {
      key: iso,
      label: getWeekdayShort(iso).slice(0, 1),
      done: Boolean(game.days && game.days[iso]),
    };
  });

  const completeQuest = (qItem = quest) => {
    const isMain = qItem.id === quest.id;
    const claimKey = `${today}:${qItem.id}`;
    if (completedToday && isMain) {
      notify("Main mission already claimed for today!");
      return;
    }
    if (
      claimLockRef.current.has(claimKey) ||
      (!isMain && completedSideQuests.includes(qItem.id))
    ) {
      notify("This side quest has already been claimed today.");
      return;
    }
    claimLockRef.current.add(claimKey);

    setGame((prev) => {
      const previousSideQuests =
        prev.sideCompleted &&
        typeof prev.sideCompleted === "object" &&
        !Array.isArray(prev.sideCompleted)
          ? prev.sideCompleted
          : {};
      const previousSideList = Array.isArray(previousSideQuests[today])
        ? previousSideQuests[today]
        : [];
      return {
        ...prev,
        xp: (Number(prev.xp) || 0) + qItem.xp,
        completed: isMain
          ? { ...(prev.completed || {}), [today]: qItem.id }
          : prev.completed,
        days: { ...(prev.days || {}), [today]: true },
        sideCompleted: isMain
          ? previousSideQuests
          : {
              ...previousSideQuests,
              [today]: [...previousSideList, qItem.id],
            },
      };
    });
    notify(`+${qItem.xp} XP earned! Mission cleared: ${qItem.title}`);
  };

  return (
    <>
      <Heading
        title="Comeback Quest"
        description="Small real-world missions. Earn XP, level up, and build a life you love waking up to."
        action={
          <Button
            onClick={() => {
              if (
                window.confirm(
                  "Reset Quest XP, class progress, and reward history?"
                )
              ) {
                setGame({
                  xp: 0,
                  completed: {},
                  days: {},
                  sideCompleted: {},
                  dailyWinRewards: {},
                  levelRewards: {},
                });
                notify("Quest progress reset");
              }
            }}
          >
            Reset XP
          </Button>
        }
      />

      <div className="lifestyle-banner">
        <div>
          <span className="eyebrow">
            CLASS {currentClass.rank} Â· {currentClass.title.toUpperCase()} Â·

            </div>

                <strong>{d.done ? "âœ“" : "Â·"}</strong>
                <span>{d.key.slice(8)}</span>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
          <div className="quote-strip">
            <span>â€œ</span>
            <p>Missing one day never erases your XP. Just return today.</p>
          </div>
        </Card>
      </div>

      <Card>
        <div className="section-head">
          <div>
            <div className="eyebrow">SIDE QUESTS</div>
            <h2>Bonus actions you can do anytime</h2>
            <p>Each side quest can earn XP once per day.</p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="record-grid">
          {QUESTS.filter((q) => q.id !== quest.id).map((item) => (
            <div className="side-quest-card" key={item.id}>
              <span className="record-symbol sage">{item.icon}</span>
              <div className="habit-info">
                <strong>{item.title}</strong>
                <small>
                  {item.detail}
                  {completedSideQuests.includes(item.id)
                    ? " Â· Claimed today"
                    : ""}
                </small>
              </div>
              <Button
                disabled={completedSideQuests.includes(item.id)}
                onClick={() => completeQuest(item)}
              >
                {completedSideQuests.includes(item.id)
                  ? "âœ“ Claimed"
                  : `+${item.xp} XP`}
              </Button>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </Card>
    </>
  );
}

import React, { useState, useEffect, useMemo, useCallback, useRef } from "react";
import {

function ReflectionAndMindPage({
  data,
  setData,
  today,
  onJournal,
  onEditJournal,
  onDelete,
  onMood,
  notify,
}) {
  const [subTab, setSubTab] = useState("insights"); // insights | ladder | cbt | screening
  const [range, setRange] = useState(14);
  const [journalSearch, setJournalSearch] = useState("");
  const [onlyGratitude, setOnlyGratitude] = useState(false);

  const [thoughtInput, setThoughtInput] = useState({
    situation: "",
    thought: "",
    distortion: "spotlight",
    intensity: 7,
    afterIntensity: 3,
    balanced: "",
  });

  const [activeScreen, setActiveScreen] = useState("phq9");
  const [phqAnswers, setPhqAnswers] = useState(Array(9).fill(0));
  const [gadAnswers, setGadAnswers] = useState(Array(7).fill(0));

  const moods = useMemo(
    () => [...(data.moods || [])].sort((a, b) => a.date.localeCompare(b.date)),
    [data.moods]
  );

  const recent = useMemo(
    () =>
      moods.filter(
        (m) => dayDiff(m.date, today) >= 0 && dayDiff(m.date, today) < range
      ),
    [moods, today, range]
  );

  const average = recent.length
    ? (
        recent.reduce((s, m) => s + Number(m.score || 0), 0) / recent.length
      ).toFixed(1)
    : "â€”";

  const avgEnergy = recent.length
    ? (
        recent.reduce((s, m) => s + Number(m.energy || 3), 0) / recent.length
      ).toFixed(1)
    : "â€”";

  const avgStress = recent.length
    ? (
        recent.reduce((s, m) => s + Number(m.stress || 3), 0) / recent.length
      ).toFixed(1)
    : "â€”";

  const moodChartData = useMemo(() => {
    return Array.from({ length: range }, (_, i) => {
      const iso = addDaysISO(today, -(range - 1 - i));
      const m = moods.find((x) => x.date === iso);
      const dObj = data.days && data.days[iso];
      return {
        key: iso,
        label: new Date(`${iso}T12:00:00`).toLocaleDateString(undefined, {
          weekday: "narrow",
        }),
        fullLabel: fmtDate(iso),
        value: m ? Number(m.score) : dObj?.mood || 0,
      };
    });
  }, [range, today, moods, data.days]);

  const energyChartData = useMemo(() => {
    return Array.from({ length: range }, (_, i) => {
      const iso = addDaysISO(today, -(range - 1 - i));
      const m = moods.find((x) => x.date === iso);
      const dObj = data.days && data.days[iso];
      return {
        key: iso,
        label: new Date(`${iso}T12:00:00`).toLocaleDateString(undefined, {
          weekday: "narrow",
        }),
        fullLabel: fmtDate(iso),
        value: m ? Number(m.energy || 3) : dObj?.energy || 0,
      };
    });
  }, [range, today, moods, data.days]);

  /* Filtered Journals */
  const filteredJournals = useMemo(() => {
    const q = journalSearch.trim().toLowerCase();
    return [...(data.journals || [])]
      .filter((j) => {
        if (onlyGratitude && !j.gratitude) return false;
        if (!q) return true;
        return (
          (j.title || "").toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
          (j.content || "").toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
          (j.gratitude || "").toLowerCase().includes(q)
        );
      })
      .sort((a, b) => (b.date || "").localeCompare(a.date || ""));
  }, [data.journals, journalSearch, onlyGratitude]);

  /* Social Exposure Ladder Handlers */
  const ladder = data.ladder || {};
  const completedLadderCount = EXPOSURE_LADDER.filter((item) =>
    Boolean(ladder[item.id]?.done)
  ).length;
  const totalLadderReps = EXPOSURE_LADDER.reduce(
    (sum, item) => sum + safeNum(ladder[item.id]?.reps, 0),
    0
  );

  const handleLadderToggle = (stepId, done) => {
    setData((old) => {
      const prevStep = (old.ladder || {})[stepId] || {};
      const nextReps =
        done && !prevStep.reps ? 1 : safeNum(prevStep.reps, 0);
      return {
        ...old,
        ladder: {
          ...(old.ladder || {}),
          [stepId]: { ...prevStep, done, reps: nextReps, lastDate: today },
        },
      };
    });
  };

  const handleLadderRepDelta = (stepId, delta) => {
    setData((old) => {
      const prevStep = (old.ladder || {})[stepId] || {};
      const nextReps = Math.max(0, safeNum(prevStep.reps, 0) + delta);
      return {
        ...old,
        ladder: {
          ...(old.ladder || {}),
          [stepId]: {
            ...prevStep,
            reps: nextReps,
            done: nextReps > 0 ? true : prevStep.done,
            lastDate: today,
          },
        },
      };
    });
  };

  const handleLadderNoteChange = (stepId, note) => {
    setData((old) => ({
      ...old,
      ladder: {
        ...(old.ladder || {}),
        [stepId]: { ...((old.ladder || {})[stepId] || {}), note },
      },
    }));
  };

  /* CBT Thought Record Handlers */
  const handleSaveThought = () => {
    if (!thoughtInput.thought.trim()) return;
    const entry = {
      id: uid(),
      date: today,
      ...thoughtInput,
      intensity: safeNum(thoughtInput.intensity, 7),
      afterIntensity: safeNum(thoughtInput.afterIntensity, 3),
    };
    setData((old) => ({ ...old, thoughts: [entry, ...(old.thoughts || [])] }));
    setThoughtInput({
      situation: "",
      thought: "",
      distortion: "spotlight",
      intensity: 7,
      afterIntensity: 3,
      balanced: "",
    });
    notify("CBT thought record saved");
  };

  const handleDeleteThought = (id) => {
    setData((old) => ({
      ...old,
      thoughts: (old.thoughts || []).filter((t) => t.id !== id),
    }));
    notify("Thought record deleted");
  };

  /* PHQ-9 & GAD-7 Screening Handlers */
  const currentScreenArr = activeScreen === "phq9" ? phqAnswers : gadAnswers;
  const currentScreenTotal = currentScreenArr.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);
  const currentScreenMax = activeScreen === "phq9" ? 27 : 21;
  const currentScreenBand =
    currentScreenTotal >= 15
      ? "Severe"
      : currentScreenTotal >= 10
      ? "Moderate"
      : currentScreenTotal >= 5
      ? "Mild"
      : "Minimal";

  const handleSaveScreening = () => {
    const isPhq = activeScreen === "phq9";
    const arr = isPhq ? phqAnswers : gadAnswers;
    const total = arr.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);
    const band =
      total >= 15
        ? "Severe"
        : total >= 10
        ? "Moderate"
        : total >= 5
        ? "Mild"
        : "Minimal";
    const entry = {
      id: uid(),
      date: today,
      type: isPhq ? "PHQ-9" : "GAD-7",
      total,
      band,
    };
    setData((old) => ({
      ...old,
      mentalCheckins: [entry, ...(old.mentalCheckins || [])],
    }));
    notify(`${entry.type} saved (${total} - ${band})`);
  };

  const handleDeleteScreening = (id) => {
    setData((old) => ({
      ...old,
      mentalCheckins: (old.mentalCheckins || []).filter((x) => x.id !== id),
    }));
  };

  return (
    <>
      <Heading
        title="Reflection, mood & social confidence"
        description="Mood & energy analytics, searchable private journal, 10-step social exposure ladder, CBT cognitive restructuring, and PHQ-9/GAD-7 tracking."
        action={
          <div className="heading-btn-group">
            <Button onClick={onMood}>â™¡ Mood check-in</Button>
            <Button kind="primary" onClick={onJournal}>
              ï¼‹ Write reflection
            </Button>
          </div>
        }
      />

      <div className="tabs large-tabs">
        {[
          ["insights", "Mood Charts & Journal"],
          [
            "ladder",
            `Social Confidence Ladder (${completedLadderCount}/10)`,
          ],
          ["cbt", `CBT Thought Record (${(data.thoughts || []).length})`],
          [
            "screening",
            `PHQ-9 & GAD-7 Check (${(data.mentalCheckins || []).length})`,
          ],
        ].map(([k, lbl]) => (
          <button
            type="button"
            key={k}
            className={subTab === k ? "selected" : ""}
            onClick={() => setSubTab(k)}
          >
            {lbl}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>

      {/* =====================================================================
          TAB 1: MOOD CHARTS & SEARCHABLE JOURNAL
          ===================================================================== */}
      {subTab === "insights" && (
        <>
          <div className="stats-grid jsx-29b7980a" >
            <Stat
              icon="â™¡"
              label={`Avg Mood (${range}d)`}
              value={`${average}/5`}
              detail={`${recent.length} mood check-ins in range`}
              color="lavender"
            />
            <Stat
              icon="âš¡"
              label={`Avg Energy (${range}d)`}
              value={`${avgEnergy}/5`}
              detail="Dashed copper line on chart"
              color="peach"
            />
            <Stat
              icon="â—Œ"
              label={`Avg Stress (${range}d)`}
              value={`${avgStress}/5`}
              detail="Lower is calmer"
              color="blue"
            />
            <Stat
              icon="â–¤"
              label="Total Reflections"
              value={(data.journals || []).length}
              detail="Saved to your database"
              color="sage"
            />
          </div>

          <div className="insight-grid">
            <Card className="mood-chart-card">
              <div className="section-head">
                <div>
                  <div className="eyebrow">EMOTIONAL WEATHER &amp; VITALITY</div>
                  <h2>Mood &amp; energy over time (Dual spline chart)</h2>
                  <p>
                    Solid purple tracks Mood (/5); dashed copper tracks Energy
                    (/5).
                  </p>
                </div>
                <select
                  className="compact-select"
                  value={range}
                  onChange={(e) => setRange(Number(e.target.value))}
                >
                  <option value="7">7 days</option>
                  <option value="14">14 days</option>
                  <option value="30">30 days</option>
                </select>
              </div>
              <div className="mood-average">
                <strong>
                  {average}
                  <small>/5</small>
                </strong>
                <span>
                  Average mood across {range} days Â· Energy avg: {avgEnergy}/5
                </span>
              </div>
              <ModernAreaChart
                data={moodChartData}
                maxVal={5}
                unit="/5"
                color="#766694"
                secondaryData={energyChartData}
                secondaryUnit="/5 energy"
                secondaryColor="#c89572"
                height={180}
              />
            </Card>

            <Card className="completion-card">
              <div className="eyebrow">SHOWING UP</div>
              <h2>Habit completion rate</h2>
              <p>Completed check-ins across your saved history.</p>
              <div className="big-number">
                {percent(
                  (data.checkins || []).filter((c) => c.done).length,
                  (data.checkins || []).length
                )}
                <small>%</small>
              </div>
              <Progress
                value={percent(
                  (data.checkins || []).filter((c) => c.done).length,
                  (data.checkins || []).length
                )}
              />
              <div className="completion-stats">
                <div>
                  <strong>
                    {(data.checkins || []).filter((c) => c.done).length}

                  type="search"

                  value={journalSearch}
                  onChange={(e) => setJournalSearch(e.target.value)}
                />
                <Button
                  kind={onlyGratitude ? "primary" : "secondary"}
                  onClick={() => setOnlyGratitude((v) => !v)}
                >
                  â™¡ Gratitude only
                </Button>
                <Button kind="primary" onClick={onJournal}>
                  ï¼‹ New entry
                </Button>
              </div>
            </div>

            {filteredJournals.length ? (
              filteredJournals.map((j) => (
                <article className="journal-entry" key={j.id}>
                  <div className="journal-date">{fmtDate(j.date)}</div>
                  <div className="journal-entry-body">
                    <h3>{j.title}</h3>

                    {j.gratitude && (
                      <div className="gratitude">
                        <span>â™¡</span> {j.gratitude}

              ))
            ) : (
              <Empty
                icon="â–¤"
                title={
                  journalSearch || onlyGratitude
                    ? "No matching journal entries"
                    : "Your story, in your own words"
                }
                description="Start with a few lines about what felt important today."
                action={
                  <Button kind="primary" onClick={onJournal}>
                    ï¼‹ Write your first reflection
                  </Button>

          </Card>

      )}

      {/* =====================================================================

      {subTab === "ladder" && (
        <div className="stack-col">
          <Card>
            <div className="section-head">

                <h2>10-Step social confidence ladder</h2>

                  time and log your real-world reps.
                </p>
              </div>
              <span className="status-pill complete">
                {completedLadderCount}/10 Steps Â· {totalLadderReps} Total Reps
              </span>

              <Progress value={percent(completedLadderCount, 10)} />
            </div>

            <div className="habit-list jsx-dffc4db2" >
              {EXPOSURE_LADDER.map((item) => {
                const stepObj = ladder[item.id] || {};
                const done = Boolean(stepObj.done);
                const reps = safeNum(stepObj.reps, 0);

                return (
                  <div
                    className={`jsx-714f3452 ${`habit-row ${done ? "checked" : ""}`}`}
                    key={item.id}
                    
                  >
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      className="check-circle"
                      onClick={() => handleLadderToggle(item.id, !done)}
                    >

                        placeholder="Quick note: How did it feel when you tried this?"
                        value={stepObj.note || ""}
                        onChange={(e) =>
                          handleLadderNoteChange(item.id, e.target.value)
                        }
                      />
                    </div>

                    <div className="heading-btn-group">
                      <Button
                        onClick={() => handleLadderRepDelta(item.id, -1)}
                        disabled={reps <= 0}
                      >
                        âˆ’
                      </Button>
                      <span
                        className={`status-pill ${done ? "complete" : ""}`}
                      >
                        {reps} reps
                      </span>
                      <Button
                        kind="primary"

                      >
                        ï¼‹1 Rep

      )}

          TAB 3: CBT THOUGHT RECORD & COGNITIVE DISTORTION DETECTOR
          ===================================================================== */}
      {subTab === "cbt" && (
        <div className="dashboard-grid">
          <Card>
            <div className="eyebrow">COGNITIVE RESTRUCTURING</div>
            <h2>Reframe automatic negative thoughts</h2>
            <div className="stack-col jsx-dffc4db2" >
              <Field label="1. Situation (What happened?)">
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={thoughtInput.situation}
                  placeholder="e.g. Walked into the gym / Sat in class"
                  onChange={(e) =>
                    setThoughtInput({
                      ...thoughtInput,
                      situation: e.target.value,
                    })
                  }
                />
              </Field>

              <Field label="2. Automatic Negative Thought (ANT)">
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={thoughtInput.thought}
                  placeholder="e.g. Everyone is judging how skinny I am"
                  onChange={(e) =>
                    setThoughtInput({
                      ...thoughtInput,
                      thought: e.target.value,
                    })
                  }
                />
              </Field>

              <Field
                label="3. Spot the Cognitive Trap (Distortion)"
                hint={
                  COGNITIVE_DISTORTIONS.find(
                    (d) => d.id === thoughtInput.distortion
                  )?.desc
                }
              >
                <select
                  value={thoughtInput.distortion}
                  onChange={(e) =>
                    setThoughtInput({
                      ...thoughtInput,
                      distortion: e.target.value,
                    })
                  }
                >
                  {COGNITIVE_DISTORTIONS.map((d) => (
                    <option key={d.id} value={d.id}>
                      {d.label}
                    </option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              </Field>

                <Field
                  label={`Distress Before (1â€“10): ${thoughtInput.intensity}/10`}
                >
                  <input
                    type="range"
                    min="1"
                    max="10"
                    value={thoughtInput.intensity}
                    onChange={(e) =>
                      setThoughtInput({
                        ...thoughtInput,
                        intensity: Number(e.target.value),
                      })
                    }
                  />
                </Field>
                <Field
                  label={`Distress After Reframe (1â€“10): ${thoughtInput.afterIntensity}/10`}
                >
                  <input
                    type="range"
                    min="1"
                    max="10"
                    value={thoughtInput.afterIntensity}
                    onChange={(e) =>
                      setThoughtInput({
                        ...thoughtInput,
                        afterIntensity: Number(e.target.value),
                      })
                    }
                  />
                </Field>
              </div>

              <Field label="4. Balanced, Realistic Truth (Evidence-based)">
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={thoughtInput.balanced}
                  placeholder="e.g. Everyone is focused on their own workout; showing up builds my frame"
                  onChange={(e) =>
                    setThoughtInput({
                      ...thoughtInput,
                      balanced: e.target.value,
                    })
                  }
                />
              </Field>

              <Button kind="primary" onClick={handleSaveThought}>
                Save thought record
              </Button>
            </div>
          </Card>

          <Card>
            <div className="eyebrow">SAVED THOUGHT RECORDS</div>
            <h2>Your balanced truths</h2>
            {(data.thoughts || []).length === 0 ? (
              <Empty
                icon="â–¤"
                title="No thought records yet"
                description="Write down anxious thoughts to separate facts from fear."
              />
            ) : (
              <div className="stack-col jsx-4bb90035" >
                {(data.thoughts || []).map((t) => {
                  const distLabel =
                    COGNITIVE_DISTORTIONS.find((d) => d.id === t.distortion)
                      ?.label || "Cognitive Reframe";
                  return (
                    <div key={t.id} className="routine-step-box">
                      <div className="row-between">
                        <small className="eyebrow">
                          {fmtDate(t.date)} Â· {distLabel}
                        </small>
                        <div className="heading-btn-group">
                          <span className="status-pill complete">
                            Distress {t.intensity || 7}/10 â†’{" "}
                            {t.afterIntensity ?? "â€”"}/10
                          </span>
                          <button
                            type="button"
                            className="danger-text"
                            onClick={() => handleDeleteThought(t.id)}
                          >
                            Ã—
                          </button>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                      {t.situation && (
                        <p>
                          <strong>Situation:</strong> {t.situation}
                        </p>
                      )}
                      <p>
                        <strong>Thought:</strong> {t.thought}
                      </p>
                      <p>
                        <strong>Balanced Truth:</strong> {t.balanced}
                      </p>
                    </div>

              </div>
            )}
          </Card>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* =====================================================================
          TAB 4: PHQ-9 & GAD-7 SELF-CHECK + LONGITUDINAL HISTORY
          ===================================================================== */}
      {subTab === "screening" && (

                </div>
                <h2>
                  {activeScreen === "phq9"
                    ? "PHQ-9 Mood Check"
                    : "GAD-7 Anxiety Check"}
                </h2>
                <p>
                  Over the last 2 weeks, how often have you been bothered by any
                  of the following problems?
                </p>
              </div>
              <div className="tabs">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  className={activeScreen === "phq9" ? "selected" : ""}
                  onClick={() => setActiveScreen("phq9")}
                >
                  PHQ-9
                </button>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  className={activeScreen === "gad7" ? "selected" : ""}
                  onClick={() => setActiveScreen("gad7")}
                >
                  GAD-7
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Live Score Preview Bar */}
            <div
              className="mini-stat-row jsx-156c83db"

              <div>
                <span className="mini-stat-number">
                  {currentScreenTotal}/{currentScreenMax}
                </span>
                <span>Current Live Score</span>
              </div>
              <div>
                <span className="mini-stat-number">{currentScreenBand}</span>
                <span>Severity Band</span>
              </div>
              <div>
                <span className="mini-stat-number">
                  {(data.mentalCheckins || []).length}
                </span>
                <span>Saved Check-ins</span>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Crisis Safety Callout if PHQ-9 Question 9 > 0 */}
            {activeScreen === "phq9" && phqAnswers[8] > 0 && (
              <div className="intro-callout jsx-29b7980a" >
                <div className="callout-icon">â™¡</div>
                <div>
                  <strong>You matter, and confidential support is available right now</strong>

                    Please reach out to <strong>Tele-MANAS</strong> at{" "}
                    <strong>14416</strong> or <strong>1800-891-4416</strong>{" "}
                    (free, 24Ã—7 in Hindi &amp; English) or talk to a trusted
                    family member or doctor today.
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>
            )}

            <div className="stack-col">
              {(activeScreen === "phq9" ? PHQ9_QUESTIONS : GAD7_QUESTIONS).map(
                (q, idx) => {
                  const arr =
                    activeScreen === "phq9" ? phqAnswers : gadAnswers;
                  return (
                    <div key={idx} className="audit-q-box">
                      <strong>{q}</strong>
                      <div className="category-pills jsx-0d69e4b1" >
                        {[
                          [0, "Not at all (0)"],
                          [1, "Several days (1)"],
                          [2, "More than half (2)"],
                          [3, "Nearly every day (3)"],
                        ].map(([val, lbl]) => (
                          <button
                            type="button"
                            key={val}
                            className={arr[idx] === val ? "selected" : ""}
                            onClick={() => {
                              const next = [...arr];
                              next[idx] = val;
                              if (activeScreen === "phq9") setPhqAnswers(next);
                              else setGadAnswers(next);
                            }}
                          >
                            {lbl}
                          </button>
                        ))}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  );
                }

              <div className="heading-btn-group">
                <Button kind="primary" onClick={handleSaveScreening}>
                  Save check-in score ({currentScreenTotal} Â·{" "}
                  {currentScreenBand})
                </Button>
                <Button
                  onClick={() =>
                    activeScreen === "phq9"
                      ? setPhqAnswers(Array(9).fill(0))
                      : setGadAnswers(Array(7).fill(0))
                  }
                >
                  Reset answers
                </Button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </Card>

          {/* SAVED SCREENING HISTORY */}
          <Card>
            <div className="section-head">
              <div>
                <div className="eyebrow">LONGITUDINAL SELF-CHECK HISTORY</div>
                <h2>Past PHQ-9 &amp; GAD-7 scores</h2>
              </div>
            </div>

            {(data.mentalCheckins || []).length === 0 ? (
              <Empty
                icon="â™¡"
                title="No saved check-ins yet"
                description="Save a biweekly PHQ-9 or GAD-7 check above to track your progress over the 90 days."
              />
            ) : (
              <div className="habit-list jsx-4bb90035" >
                {(data.mentalCheckins || []).map((mc) => (
                  <div className="habit-row checked" key={mc.id}>
                    <span className="status-pill complete">{mc.type}</span>
                    <div className="habit-info">
                      <strong>
                        Score: {mc.total} ({mc.band})
                      </strong>
                      <small>{fmtDate(mc.date)}</small>
                    </div>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      className="danger-text"
                      onClick={() => handleDeleteScreening(mc.id)}
                    >
                      Delete
                    </button>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            )}
          </Card>
        </div>
      )}
    </>
  );
}
